# Taskop Tasks — Part 1: Contracts & API — Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Let `tasks.manage` holders hand one-off tasks (ad hoc, or the fix for a closed run's problem) to a person or a team, let workers accept, start, answer with text, photos and videos, and complete them online or offline, and let managers list, edit, reassign, cancel and review them, by adding the task contracts, i18n strings, `@taskop/api-client` methods, the four task tables, the `tasks` NestJS module and the sync and problem additions of the sub-project 5 spec §3–§6.

**Architecture:**
- **Pure task logic** lives in `@taskop/contracts` so the phone and the API decide the same thing: `taskTransition` (the status table of spec §3), `taskEvidenceMet` and `problemResolutionOf`, next to the input schemas, `TASK_LIMITS` and the read models.
- **One new NestJS module, `tasks`**, imports `SchedulingModule` (for `JobsService` and `SchedulingScope`). It never imports `ExecutionsModule`; it reads problems through the plain helpers in `executions/problem-rows.ts`. `ExecutionsModule` imports `TasksModule` for one provider, `TaskSync`, which `GET /me/sync` uses.
  - `TaskScope` turns spec §6.1 into SQL: `readable`, `manageable`, `reviewable`, plus `lock`, `access` and `assertWorker`.
  - `TasksService` handles the manager commands (create, edit/reassign, cancel, review). `TaskWorkService` handles the worker commands (accept, start, submit). `TaskMediaService` registers, confirms and signs task media. `TaskQueries` builds `TaskSummary`, `TaskDetail` and the phone's `SyncTask`.
  - `TaskJobs` runs `tasks.sweep` (every minute) and `task-media.cleanup` (daily). `TaskListeners` returns team tasks of deactivated users to their team.
- **Lock order** for every task command: the task row first (`SELECT … FOR UPDATE`), then the access decision on the committed row. Two accepts of one team task are therefore serialised and the second sees the winner. The sweep uses `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED`, so it never blocks a command.
- **Problems** gain a derived `resolution` and `taskId` from a left join on `tasks.problem_id`. `ProblemWriter` never deletes a problem that has a task.

**Tech Stack:** Node 24, TypeScript 7, NestJS 12, nestjs-zod 5, Zod 4, Drizzle ORM 0.45 + drizzle-kit, PostgreSQL 18, pg-boss 12, `@aws-sdk/client-s3` + `@aws-sdk/s3-request-presigner`, SeaweedFS (tests: `testcontainers`), Vitest 5, Testcontainers (Postgres), supertest. **No new packages.**

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-12-tasks-design.md`

**This plan is Part 1 of 3:**
- Part 1 (this file): contracts, i18n strings for errors, statuses, labels and issues, the API client, the DB, the `tasks` module, the problem and sync additions, API tests, the seed.
- Part 2 (`apps/mobile`): `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-12-tasks-2-mobile.md`. Part 3 (`apps/web`): `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-12-tasks-3-web.md`. Both are written against the section **Interfaces for Parts 2 and 3** below, and they do not depend on each other.
- Parts 2 and 3 can start once **Tasks 1–4** are merged into the feature branch: those tasks need nothing from the API and give both apps every type, schema, pure function, error code, i18n key and client method. They need Tasks 5–18 merged before their integration and end-to-end tests run against a real API.
- The spec §9 Playwright flow (closed run → task from problem on the web → submit through the API → reject → submit → approve → *resolved*) belongs to Part 3's last task. It needs no seed or helper from this part: like `apps/web/e2e/executions.spec.ts`, it builds its tenant, closed run and phone calls through the public API.

**Deployment plan note (not a task).** The web is the first browser client to `PUT` to a presigned SeaweedFS URL and to load media from it, so the bucket needs a CORS configuration (Task 17 adds it for local dev). `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-09-deployment-design.md` lives on `origin/claude/remaining-phases-72a9e6` (PR #2); whoever merges it after this sub-project adds to §5 "Presigned photo and video uploads": "The `taskop-media` bucket on `files.taskop.app` allows CORS from origin `https://admin.taskop.app` only: methods `GET`, `PUT`, `HEAD`; headers `Content-Type`, `Content-Length`; expose `ETag`. Apply it once after the bucket is created with `aws --endpoint-url http://seaweedfs:8333 s3api put-bucket-cors --bucket taskop-media --cors-configuration file://cors.prod.json` (the dev file `docker/seaweedfs/cors.json` with the origins swapped). A bucket CORS rule replaces SeaweedFS's permissive default (`-s3.allowedOrigins`, default `*`), so other origins are refused." Phones are not browsers and send no `Origin`; they are unaffected.

## Global Constraints

- The product name is **Taskop**. Packages are `@taskop/*`. Azerbaijani (`az`) is the only locale.
- No new npm packages. Everything this part needs (`@aws-sdk/*`, `testcontainers`, `uuidv7`) is already a dependency of `apps/api`.
- IDs:
  - `tasks.id`, `task_submissions.id` and `task_media.id` are **client-generated UUIDv7** and have no database default.
  - `task_status_history.id` is server-generated (UUIDv7 default).
- Every new table has `tenant_id uuid not null`, RLS `FORCE`d with the usual `nullif(current_setting('app.tenant_id', true), '')::uuid` policy, and composite `(tenant_id, x_id)` foreign keys.
- Grants (to `taskop_app` and `taskop_platform`, as every earlier migration does): `SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE` on `tasks`, `task_submissions`, `task_media`; `SELECT, INSERT` on `task_status_history`. No `DELETE` anywhere (see Spec decisions 3).
- Statuses: `new`, `accepted`, `in_progress`, `awaiting_review`, `completed`, `cancelled`. Final: `completed`, `cancelled`.
- Priorities: `normal`, `high`. Actions: `accept`, `start`, `submit`, `approve`, `reject`, `cancel`, `reassign`.
- Evidence: `{ photo: 'none' | 'optional' | 'required', video: 'none' | 'optional' | 'required', liveOnly: boolean }`, default `{ photo: 'optional', video: 'optional', liveOnly: false }`.
- Limits (spec §4), exactly:
  - Title 1–200 characters (trimmed). Instructions ≤ 5,000. Submission text ≤ 5,000.
  - Cancel reason 1–1,000. Review comment ≤ 1,000, required (non-empty) to reject.
  - Instruction media ≤ 5 per task. Response media per submission: photos ≤ 10, videos ≤ 3.
  - File size, type and resize limits are `MEDIA_LIMITS` (photo ≤ 5,242,880 bytes, `image/jpeg | image/png`; video ≤ 62,914,560 bytes, ≤ 60 s, short edge ≤ 720, `video/mp4 | video/quicktime`).
  - Presigned PUT 15 min, presigned GET 5 min (`MEDIA_LIMITS.uploadUrlTtlSeconds`, `downloadUrlTtlSeconds`).
  - Pending task media: storage object deleted by `task-media.cleanup` after **14 days** (`MEDIA_LIMITS.pendingCleanupDays`).
  - Sync: closed (completed or cancelled) tasks of the user stay in `/me/sync` for **7 days** after closing (`updated_at`). `knownTaskIds` ≤ **200**.
- Device times (`at`, `submittedAt`, `capturedAt`) later than server receipt + **2 min** (`EXECUTION_LIMITS.clockFutureToleranceMs`) → `422 CLOCK_INVALID`. Earlier than the previous step → raised to it (see Spec decisions 1).
- Overdue: `overdue_at = now` once `now ≥ due_at` while not final; set once, never cleared by completion. Editing `due_at` to `null` or to a time after now clears it.
- Storage keys: `t/{tenantId}/k/{taskId}/{mediaId}.{ext}`, `ext` from `MEDIA_LIMITS.extensions`. Bucket `taskop-media`.
- Jobs: `tasks.sweep` cron `* * * * *`; `task-media.cleanup` cron `45 3 * * *` (UTC). Both through `JobsService.define`, `perTenant` and `tenantsWith`.
- New error codes and HTTP statuses:
  - `TASK_ALREADY_TAKEN` 409
  - `TASK_STATE_INVALID` 409
  - `TASK_ASSIGNEE_INVALID` 422 (with `fields.assignee = 'errors.TASK_ASSIGNEE_INVALID'`)
  - `PROBLEM_NOT_CLOSED` 409
  - `PROBLEM_ALREADY_HAS_TASK` 409
  - `EVIDENCE_REQUIRED` 422 (with `fields['evidence.photo'] = 'tasks.issues.photoRequired'` and/or `fields['evidence.video'] = 'tasks.issues.videoRequired'`)
  - Existing codes reused: `NOT_FOUND`, `FORBIDDEN`, `VALIDATION_FAILED` (400), `CLOCK_INVALID`, `MEDIA_TYPE_INVALID`, `MEDIA_TOO_LARGE`, `MEDIA_LIMIT_REACHED`, `MEDIA_NOT_FOUND_IN_STORAGE`, `EVIDENCE_LIVE_ONLY`, `SITE_INACTIVE`, `SITE_OUT_OF_SCOPE`, `REFERENCE_NOT_FOUND`.
- Permissions: `tasks.view`, `tasks.manage` in a new `tasks` group. `SYSTEM_ROLE_DEFAULTS`: owner and admin all keys, manager both, auditor `tasks.view`, worker none.
- Audit (`entityType: 'task'`): `task.created`, `task.updated`, `task.reassigned`, `task.cancelled`, `task.approved`, `task.rejected`. Accept, start and submit write `task_status_history` only.
- Domain events: `task.assigned`, `task.accepted`, `task.submitted`, `task.rejected`, `task.completed`, `task.cancelled`, `task.overdue`, payload `{ tenantId, taskId, actorUserId, assigneeUserId, assigneeTeamId, createdByUserId }` (nullable where the spec says optional). No listeners in SP5.
- Every task status change writes one `task_status_history` row per step, in the same transaction. Worker steps carry the bounded device time and the worker as actor; manager steps carry server time and the manager.
- The task endpoints are tenant-only (no `platform/tenants/:tenantId/…` variants).
- No test sleeps to wait for wall-clock time. Use `FakeClock`. Only the pg-boss wiring tests poll, with a timeout. Tests that need SeaweedFS live only in `apps/api/test/tasks-storage.test.ts` (plus the existing storage files); every other test uses the dummy `S3_*` values from `testEnv`.

### Spec decisions this plan makes (ambiguities and conflicts with the code, resolved)

1. **Device times follow SP4's rule, not "5 minutes, else server time".** `apps/api/src/executions/device-time.ts` refuses any device time later than receipt + 2 min with `422 CLOCK_INVALID`; tasks do the same through `assertNotFuture`. A device time earlier than the previous step is raised to it (`boundTaskTime`), like SP4's `clampStart`: the floor is `created_at` for accept, `accepted_at ?? created_at` for start, `started_at ?? created_at` for submit, so history stays in order. `capturedAt` only has the future bound. Task commands carry no `clientOffsetMs` and tasks have no `clock_suspect`; instead the client moves each time onto the server's clock before sending it (`device time − clockOffsetMs` from its last `/me/sync`, Interfaces → *Server behaviour*), and the server applies the time as received.
2. **`/me/sync` has no change cursor.** `SyncService.pull` returns a full snapshot on every call. The `tasks` section is therefore the complete current set, and `removedTaskIds` is computed from a new `knownTaskIds` query parameter (comma-separated, ≤ 200, the task ids the phone holds): the known ids that are not in the set.
3. **Task media rows are never deleted.** SP4's `media.cleanup` keeps rows and sets `storage_purged_at`; `task-media.cleanup` does the same (`task_media.storage_purged_at`), so a late confirm still answers `MEDIA_NOT_FOUND_IN_STORAGE` instead of `404`. The spec's `DELETE` grant on `task_media` is therefore not given.
4. **`execution_problems` gains one constraint**, `execution_problems_tenant_id_uq (tenant_id, id)`, so `tasks` can reference it with the composite foreign key every table uses. No column changes.
5. **A problem that has a task is never deleted.** A swept `partial` execution (no `completed_at`) still accepts late answers captured inside its window (SP4), and `ProblemWriter.rewrite` deletes problems the answers no longer derive. With `ON DELETE RESTRICT` that late sync would fail. `rewrite` now keeps linked problems.
6. **"The problem must be readable"** means readable as `GET /problems` decides: `assignments.view` and the occurrence within `SchedulingScope.occurrences`. Otherwise `404`. `executions.state` `completed | partial` is "closed"; `active` → `PROBLEM_NOT_CLOSED`.
7. **Permission backfill covers admin, manager and auditor.** SP2–SP4 backfilled Admin only. Nothing is deployed yet and the demo manager (`manager@demo.taskop.az`) needs `tasks.manage` on an existing dev database, so `0010` backfills exactly `SYSTEM_ROLE_DEFAULTS`: admin and manager get both keys, auditor `tasks.view`.
8. **Who may run a worker command** (`accept`, `start`, `submit`, response media), decided after the row lock:
   - The assignee, or a member of the task's team while `assignee_user_id` is null → allowed.
   - A member of the task's team while someone else is the assignee → `409 TASK_ALREADY_TAKEN`, even though the spec's read rule no longer lets them read it.
   - Anyone else who can read the task (creator, manager) → `403 FORBIDDEN`. Everyone else → `404`.
   - `submit` and response media on an unaccepted team task → `409 TASK_STATE_INVALID` (accept or start first).
9. **Replays are recognised before state checks.** Accept by the assignee after the task moved past `new`, and start after it moved past `accepted`, return the task unchanged (`200`), except on `cancelled` (`409 TASK_STATE_INVALID`). A known submission id or media id returns the stored result whatever the status.
10. **No optimistic concurrency on tasks.** The spec's optional `rev` in the accept body is dropped (the row lock serialises commands), and `PATCH /tasks/:id` has no revision check. `rev` is still bumped on every change and returned.
11. **Overdue writes no history row.** `overdue_at` is a flag, not a status; the `task_status_history.to_status` column holds statuses only. `overdue_at` itself is the record. Editing `due_at` clears it when the new due is `null` or later than now ("a later time" read as: no longer due).
12. **Submission refusals:** media ids that are duplicated, not `response` media of this task, captured by someone else, or already linked to an earlier submission → `400 VALIDATION_FAILED` with `fields.mediaIds = 'tasks.issues.unknownMedia'`; more than 10 photos or 3 videos → `fields.mediaIds = 'tasks.issues.tooManyMedia'`. `EVIDENCE_REQUIRED` names the missing kinds in `fields` (Global Constraints), so `ApiError` needs no new property.
13. **Team membership** for read rule 2 and for sync is a `user_teams` row. Only *assigning* checks that the team is active and has an active member.
14. **New endpoint `GET /tasks/:id/problem-media/:mediaId/url`.** The phone shows the problem's photos (spec §7.2), but `GET /media/:id/url` requires the executor or `assignments.view`. Anyone who can read a problem task gets a presigned GET for the media ids listed on its problem.
15. **`ProblemDto` also gains `executionState`**, so the web can disable "Tapşırıq yarat" while the run is open (spec §8) without a second request.
16. **Audit actions are exported as `TASK_AUDIT_ACTIONS` from `contracts/audit.ts`.** No earlier sub-project listed its actions there; this is the first list.
17. **`POST /tasks` answers `201`, also on a replay.** Every other task command answers `200`.
18. **`syncQuerySchema` and `syncResponseSchema` move to a new `contracts/sync.ts`.** `task-views.ts` imports `problemDtoSchema` from `execution-views.ts`, and the sync response needs `syncTaskSchema`; keeping both in `execution-views.ts` would make an import cycle. The exported names do not change.
19. **A task's site** must exist (`422 REFERENCE_NOT_FOUND`), be active (`409 SITE_INACTIVE`) and be writable in the creator's scope (`403 SITE_OUT_OF_SCOPE`, `SchedulingScope.assertSiteWritable`), as for assignments. A task from a problem takes the problem's site without these checks, and ignores `siteId` in the body.
20. **Media caps at registration.** Instruction media: at most 5 registrations per task (any status) → `MEDIA_LIMIT_REACHED`. Response media: per kind, the unlinked registrations of the task are capped at SP4's `storageCap(limit)` (`max(limit × 3, 10)`), leaving room for retakes; the exact 10/3 is checked on the submission. A kind whose evidence is `none` cannot be registered (`MEDIA_LIMIT_REACHED`). Response media may be registered only by the assignee while `accepted` or `in_progress`.
21. **Reassigning** to the current person (with no team) or to the current team (with nobody accepted) is not a change. A real change writes a history row only when the status actually moves (`from ≠ 'new'`).
22. **`TaskDetail` carries `canManage` and `canReview`** for the caller, so the web drawer can show Approve, Reject, Cancel and Edit without re-implementing §6.1.
23. **The `assignee_deactivated` history comment** is stored as that code (`TASK_HISTORY_REASONS`); the web translates it with `tasks.historyReasons`. Reviewer comments and cancel reasons are free text in the same column.
24. **`ProblemDto` also gains `media: { id, kind, status }[]`**, read from `execution_media` in `toProblemDtos`, in `mediaIds` order. The phone and the web need photo vs video and pending vs uploaded to draw a problem's media without signing each id first. `mediaIds` stays: the problems page counts it (`problems-page.tsx`) and the execution tab's answers view filters `ExecutionProblem.mediaIds`, so removing it would change SP4 code for nothing. A medium whose object `media.cleanup` purged keeps `status: 'pending'` (the row is never deleted and its status never changes); signing it answers `MEDIA_NOT_FOUND_IN_STORAGE`.
25. **`GET /tasks/assignees` also returns `sites`**: the active sites the caller may name on a task (`SchedulingScope.writableSites`, the SQL form of `assertSiteWritable`: every site for `all`, the subtree for `site_subtree`, none for narrower scopes), at most 200, by name, **not** filtered by `q` (the picker's search is for people and teams). A `tasks.manage` holder without `sites.view` can therefore still give a task a site, and the list never offers a site the create would refuse with `SITE_OUT_OF_SCOPE` or `SITE_INACTIVE`.
26. **Bucket CORS for the browser.** The pinned image is `chrislusf/seaweedfs:latest` (4.48 when this plan was written). Its S3 gateway answers every preflight from any origin by default (`-s3.allowedOrigins`, default `*`), and it supports bucket CORS (`PutBucketCors`), which takes precedence over that flag for the bucket and refuses other origins with `403`. Task 17 sets the bucket rule explicitly from `docker/seaweedfs/cors.json` (dev origins `http://localhost:5173` for `vite dev` and Playwright, `http://localhost:4173` for `vite preview`; `apps/web/vite.config.ts`, `apps/web/playwright.config.ts`), in `seaweedfs-init` and in the test container, so the web never depends on the permissive default.

## Review Focus

These are the five input classes most likely to bite users that no spec test names. Each line gives the task whose tests pin it.

1. **A teammate's accept arriving after the race was lost.** Two phones accept one team task offline; the second command syncs minutes later. The loser can no longer read the task, but must get `409 TASK_ALREADY_TAKEN` (so the phone shows "Bu tapşırığı artıq başqası qəbul edib"), not `404`; for two concurrent requests exactly one wins. Pinned in Task 9 (`a teammate’s later accept or start gets TASK_ALREADY_TAKEN`, `lets exactly one of two concurrent accepts win`).
2. **Replays after the task moved on.** The outbox resends an accept after the start went through, a submission after it put the task in review, or a media registration after the submission linked it. Each must return the stored result, not `TASK_STATE_INVALID`, which would park it red forever. Pinned in Task 9 (`repeating start or accept afterwards is a no-op`), Task 10 (`replays an uploaded medium’s registration without a URL`) and Task 11 (`is idempotent on the submission id, also after the task moved on`).
3. **A late answers sync on a swept run whose problem already has a task.** The manager turns a partial run's problem into a task; the worker's phone then syncs answers captured before the close that no longer raise the problem. The sync must succeed and the problem must stay. Pinned in Task 6 (`keeps a problem that has a task when a late answers sync no longer derives it`).
4. **The previous assignee's offline work after a reassignment.** A manager reassigns while the first worker is offline mid-task; their queued start or submission must not complete the task, and the new assignee must not be able to submit the old assignee's photos. Pinned in Task 12 (`reassigning resets the task; the previous assignee’s later commands are refused`).
5. **Overdue boundaries.** A task due exactly now is flagged; final tasks are never flagged; completing a late task keeps the flag; moving the due date later clears it without the next sweep re-flagging it; a task locked by a running command is skipped, not waited for. Pinned in Task 12 (`clears overdue only when the due date moves past now`) and Task 14 (all four sweep tests).

## Interfaces for Parts 2 and 3

Everything below is exported from `@taskop/contracts`, `@taskop/i18n` or `@taskop/api-client` once **Tasks 1–4** are merged. Parts 2 and 3 import these names exactly. All instants are ISO-8601 UTC strings.

### `@taskop/contracts`

**Task logic** (`tasks.ts`, Task 1):
- `TASK_STATUSES = ['new', 'accepted', 'in_progress', 'awaiting_review', 'completed', 'cancelled']`, `taskStatusSchema`, `type TaskStatus`
- `FINAL_TASK_STATUSES = ['completed', 'cancelled']`, `isFinalTaskStatus(s: TaskStatus): boolean`
- `TASK_PRIORITIES = ['normal', 'high']`, `taskPrioritySchema`, `type TaskPriority`
- `TASK_ACTIONS = ['accept', 'start', 'submit', 'approve', 'reject', 'cancel', 'reassign']`, `taskActionSchema`, `type TaskAction`
- `EVIDENCE_LEVELS = ['none', 'optional', 'required']`, `evidenceLevelSchema`, `type EvidenceLevel`
- `taskEvidenceSchema`, `type TaskEvidence = { photo: EvidenceLevel; video: EvidenceLevel; liveOnly: boolean }`, `DEFAULT_TASK_EVIDENCE = { photo: 'optional', video: 'optional', liveOnly: false }`
- `TASK_MEDIA_ROLES = ['instruction', 'response']`, `taskMediaRoleSchema`, `type TaskMediaRole`
- `TASK_REVIEW_DECISIONS = ['approved', 'rejected']`, `taskReviewDecisionSchema`, `type TaskReviewDecision` (stored on submissions)
- `TASK_SOURCES = ['problem', 'adhoc']`, `taskSourceSchema`, `type TaskSource`
- `PROBLEM_RESOLUTIONS = ['none', 'open', 'resolved', 'cancelled']`, `problemResolutionSchema`, `type ProblemResolution`
- `TASK_HISTORY_REASONS = ['assignee_deactivated']`, `type TaskHistoryReason`
- `TASK_LIMITS`:
  - `titleMax: 200`, `instructionsMax: 5000`, `submissionTextMax: 5000`, `commentMax: 1000`
  - `instructionMedia: 5`, `responsePhotos: 10`, `responseVideos: 3`
  - `syncClosedDays: 7`, `knownTaskIds: 200`
- `TASK_ISSUE_CODES` (full i18n keys), `type TaskIssueCode`: `tasks.issues.commentRequired`, `tasks.issues.unknownMedia`, `tasks.issues.tooManyMedia`, `tasks.issues.photoRequired`, `tasks.issues.videoRequired`
- `taskTransition(status: TaskStatus, action: TaskAction, opts: { requiresReview: boolean }): TaskStatus | null` — pure, the full table:

| from \ action | accept | start | submit | approve | reject | cancel | reassign |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `new` | `accepted` | `in_progress` | null | null | null | `cancelled` | `new` |
| `accepted` | null | `in_progress` | null | null | null | `cancelled` | `new` |
| `in_progress` | null | null | `awaiting_review` if `requiresReview`, else `completed` | null | null | `cancelled` | `new` |
| `awaiting_review` | null | null | null | `completed` | `in_progress` | `cancelled` | `new` |
| `completed`, `cancelled` | null | null | null | null | null | null | null |

  `start` on `new` means "accept, then start": the API writes both history rows.
- `type TaskEvidenceResult = { ok: true } | { ok: false; missing: MediaKind[] }`
- `taskEvidenceMet(evidence: TaskEvidence, media: ReadonlyArray<{ kind: MediaKind }>): TaskEvidenceResult` — a kind is missing when its level is `required` and `media` has none of it; `missing` is in `MEDIA_KINDS` order.
- `problemResolutionOf(taskStatus: TaskStatus | null): ProblemResolution` — `null → 'none'`, `completed → 'resolved'`, `cancelled → 'cancelled'`, otherwise `'open'`.
- Inputs (types are `z.input<…>`):
  - `taskAssigneeSchema` / `type TaskAssignee = { userId: string } | { teamId: string }` (strict: exactly one key)
  - `createTaskSchema` / `type CreateTaskInput`: `{ id: uuid; title; instructions?: string (default ''); priority? (default 'normal'); siteId?: uuid | null (default null); dueAt?: iso | null (default null); evidence?: TaskEvidence (default DEFAULT_TASK_EVIDENCE); requiresReview?: boolean (server default: true with problemId, else false); assignee: TaskAssignee; problemId?: uuid | null (default null) }`
  - `updateTaskSchema` / `type UpdateTaskInput`: every field optional: `{ title, instructions, priority, siteId: uuid | null, dueAt: iso | null, evidence, requiresReview, assignee }`
  - `cancelTaskSchema` / `type CancelTaskInput = { reason: string }` (1–1,000)
  - `reviewTaskSchema` / `type ReviewTaskInput = { decision: 'approve' | 'reject'; comment?: string }` — reject without a non-empty comment fails with `path: ['comment']`, message `tasks.issues.commentRequired`
  - `taskActionCommandSchema` / `type TaskActionCommand = { at: iso }` (accept and start)
  - `submissionSchema` / `type SubmissionInput = { id: uuid; text?: string (default '', ≤ 5,000); mediaIds?: uuid[] (default [], ≤ 13); submittedAt: iso }`
  - `taskMediaRegisterSchema` / `type TaskMediaRegisterInput = { id: uuid; role: TaskMediaRole; kind: MediaKind; source: MediaSource; mime: string; bytes: int ≥ 1; width?: int | null; height?: int | null; durationMs?: int | null; capturedAt: iso }`
  - `taskListQuerySchema` / `type TaskListQuery`: `{ cursor?, limit? (1–200, default 50), status?: 'a,b' (comma list of TaskStatus), overdue?: 'true' | 'false', siteId?, assigneeUserId?, teamId?, createdBy?, source?: TaskSource, awaitingMyReview?: 'true' | 'false', dueFrom?: iso, dueTo?: iso }`
  - `taskAssigneeQuerySchema` / `type TaskAssigneeQuery = { q?: string }` (≤ 100)

**Read models** (`task-views.ts`, Task 2):
- `taskSiteRefSchema = { id, name }`, `taskTeamRefSchema = { id, name }`, user refs use the existing `userRefSchema = { id, fullName }`
- `taskSummarySchema` / `type TaskSummary`:
  - `{ id, title, priority, status, site: { id, name } | null, assignee: UserRef | null, team: { id, name } | null, assigneeInactive: boolean, createdBy: UserRef, requiresReview, source: TaskSource, problemId: uuid | null, dueAt: iso | null, overdueAt: iso | null, rev: number, createdAt, updatedAt }`
  - A team task waiting for acceptance has `assignee: null` and `team` set; once accepted it has both. `overdueAt !== null` is the "Gecikib" badge.
- `taskMediaDtoSchema` / `type TaskMediaDto = { id, role, submissionId: uuid | null, kind, source, mime, bytes, width, height, durationMs, capturedAt, capturedBy: UserRef, status: MediaStatus, uploadedAt: iso | null }`
  - `TaskDetail.media` and `SyncTask.media` list **every registered medium of the task**, instruction and response, `pending` and `uploaded` alike, by capture time. Rows are never deleted (decision 3).
  - A medium never confirmed whose object `task-media.cleanup` purged after 14 days is still listed, with `status: 'pending'` and `uploadedAt: null`: there is no purged status. Its URL answers `MEDIA_NOT_FOUND_IN_STORAGE`; re-registering the same id returns a fresh PUT URL, and a later upload and confirm make it `uploaded` again.
  - Only `uploaded` media can be signed (`GET /task-media/:id/url`); draw `pending` ones as "not uploaded yet" without asking for a URL.
  - The instruction-media cap (5) counts **every** listed `role: 'instruction'` medium, pending and purged included (decision 20); a client computing the room left counts the same list.
- `taskSubmissionDtoSchema` / `type TaskSubmissionDto = { id, submittedBy: UserRef, text, mediaIds: uuid[], submittedAt, receivedAt, reviewDecision: TaskReviewDecision | null, reviewComment: string | null, reviewedBy: UserRef | null, reviewedAt: iso | null }` (oldest first)
- `taskHistoryEntrySchema` / `type TaskHistoryEntry = { id, fromStatus: TaskStatus | null, toStatus: TaskStatus, at, actor: UserRef | null, comment: string | null }` (actor null = system; comment `assignee_deactivated` is a `TaskHistoryReason`)
- `taskDetailSchema` / `type TaskDetail`: `TaskSummary` plus `{ instructions, evidence: TaskEvidence, acceptedAt, startedAt, submittedAt, completedAt, cancelledAt (iso | null each), cancelReason: string | null, media: TaskMediaDto[], submissions: TaskSubmissionDto[], problem: ProblemDto | null, history: TaskHistoryEntry[], canManage: boolean, canReview: boolean }`
- `syncTaskSchema` / `type SyncTask` = `TaskDetail` without `history`, `canManage`, `canReview`
- `taskAssigneeOptionsSchema` / `type TaskAssigneeOptions = { users: { id, fullName, jobTitle: string | null, kind: 'worker' | 'staff' }[]; teams: { id, name, memberCount: number }[]; sites: { id, name }[] }`
  - `users` and `teams`: ≤ 50 each, by name, filtered by `q`.
  - `sites`: the active sites the caller may put on a task (decision 25), ≤ 200, by name, **not** filtered by `q`. Use it for the site field of create and edit; it needs no `sites.view`.

**Sync** (`sync.ts`, Task 2; same exported names as before):
- `syncQuerySchema` / `type SyncQuery = { knownVersionIds?: 'a,b'; knownTaskIds?: 'a,b' }` (≤ 200 each)
- `syncResponseSchema` / `type SyncResponse = { serverTime; occurrences; checklistVersions; executions; tasks: SyncTask[]; removedTaskIds: string[] }`

**Problems** (`execution-views.ts`, Task 2):
- `problemDtoSchema` / `ProblemDto` gains `resolution: ProblemResolution`, `taskId: uuid | null`, `executionState: ExecutionState`, and `media: ProblemMediaRef[]`
  - `problemMediaRefSchema` / `type ProblemMediaRef = { id: uuid; kind: MediaKind; status: MediaStatus }`, in `mediaIds` order (decision 24). `mediaIds` stays, with the same ids.
  - A purged problem medium keeps `status: 'pending'`. Sign only `uploaded` ones, through `tasks.problemMediaUrl(taskId, id)` (task readers) or `media.url(id)` (SP4 readers).
- `executionProblemSchema` / `ExecutionProblem` (inside `ExecutionDetail.problems`) gains `resolution`, `taskId`
- `problemListQuerySchema` / `ProblemListQuery` gains `resolution?: ProblemResolution`

**Errors, permissions, audit** (Task 2):
- `ErrorCode` adds `TASK_ALREADY_TAKEN` (409), `TASK_STATE_INVALID` (409), `TASK_ASSIGNEE_INVALID` (422), `PROBLEM_NOT_CLOSED` (409), `PROBLEM_ALREADY_HAS_TASK` (409), `EVIDENCE_REQUIRED` (422)
- `PERMISSION_GROUPS` gains `{ group: 'tasks', keys: ['tasks.view', 'tasks.manage'] }`; `PermissionKey` includes both
- `TASK_AUDIT_ACTIONS = ['task.created', 'task.updated', 'task.reassigned', 'task.cancelled', 'task.approved', 'task.rejected']`, `type TaskAuditAction`

### `@taskop/i18n` (Task 3)

- `az.errors.<CODE>` for the 6 new codes. `TASK_ALREADY_TAKEN` is "Bu tapşırığı artıq başqası qəbul edib."
- `az.tasks`:
  - `statuses.<TaskStatus>`, `priorities.<TaskPriority>`, `sources.<TaskSource>`, `resolutions.<ProblemResolution>`
  - `evidenceLevels.<EvidenceLevel>`, `mediaRoles.<TaskMediaRole>`, `reviewDecisions.<TaskReviewDecision>`
  - `historyReasons.assignee_deactivated`
  - `issues.<code without the tasks.issues. prefix>`
  - `flags.{ overdue, assigneeInactive }` (`overdue` = "Gecikib")
  - `alreadyTaken` ("Bu tapşırığı artıq başqası qəbul edib")
- `az.roles.groups.tasks`, `az.roles.keys.tasks_view`, `az.roles.keys.tasks_manage`, `az.audit.entityTypes.task`, `az.nav.tasks` ("Tapşırıqlar")
- Part 2 adds phone UI strings to `az.mobile`. Part 3 adds web UI strings to `az.tasks` (new keys only; it never renames these).

### `@taskop/api-client` (Task 4)

`createTaskopApi(c)` now also spreads `createTasksApi(c)`, which is also exported on its own with `type TasksApi`:

| Method | HTTP | Returns |
|---|---|---|
| `tasks.list(query: TaskListQuery = {})` | `GET /tasks` | `Page<TaskSummary>` |
| `tasks.get(id: string)` | `GET /tasks/:id` | `TaskDetail` |
| `tasks.create(body: CreateTaskInput)` | `POST /tasks` (201) | `TaskDetail` |
| `tasks.update(id: string, body: UpdateTaskInput)` | `PATCH /tasks/:id` | `TaskDetail` |
| `tasks.cancel(id: string, body: CancelTaskInput)` | `POST /tasks/:id/cancel` | `TaskDetail` |
| `tasks.review(id: string, body: ReviewTaskInput)` | `POST /tasks/:id/review` | `TaskDetail` |
| `tasks.accept(id: string, body: TaskActionCommand)` | `POST /tasks/:id/accept` | `TaskDetail` |
| `tasks.start(id: string, body: TaskActionCommand)` | `POST /tasks/:id/start` | `TaskDetail` |
| `tasks.submit(id: string, body: SubmissionInput)` | `POST /tasks/:id/submissions` | `TaskDetail` |
| `tasks.registerMedia(id: string, body: TaskMediaRegisterInput)` | `POST /tasks/:id/media` | `MediaUploadTicket` |
| `tasks.assignees(query: TaskAssigneeQuery = {})` | `GET /tasks/assignees` | `TaskAssigneeOptions` |
| `tasks.problemMediaUrl(taskId: string, mediaId: string)` | `GET /tasks/:id/problem-media/:mediaId/url` | `MediaUrl` |
| `taskMedia.confirmUploaded(id: string)` | `POST /task-media/:id/uploaded` | `MediaConfirmResult` |
| `taskMedia.url(id: string)` | `GET /task-media/:id/url` | `MediaUrl` |
| `sync.pull(knownVersionIds: string[] = [], knownTaskIds: string[] = [])` | `GET /me/sync?knownVersionIds=…&knownTaskIds=…` | `SyncResponse` |

- The upload itself (`PUT uploadUrl` with `ticket.headers`) is a plain `fetch` / `FileSystem.uploadAsync`, as in SP4.
- `problems.list(query)` and `executions.get(id)` are unchanged calls whose results carry the new fields.

### Server behaviour Parts 2 and 3 rely on

- **Outbox order per task:** accept or start → register media → upload → confirm → submit (latest draft). Every command is idempotent: a repeated accept/start by the assignee returns the task, a known submission or media id returns the stored result.
- **Device times are sent on the server's clock.** Task commands carry no `clientOffsetMs` (decision 1), and the server applies `at`, `submittedAt` and `capturedAt` exactly as received: later than receipt + 2 min (`EXECUTION_LIMITS.clockFutureToleranceMs`) is `422 CLOCK_INVALID`, earlier than the previous step is raised to it. A client therefore sends `device time − clockOffsetMs`, where `clockOffsetMs` = device clock − server clock as measured at its last `/me/sync` (SP4's `measureOffset`; 0 before the first sync). The phone stores the device time locally and corrects it only when it builds the request body; a phone running ahead then never earns a permanent `CLOCK_INVALID`. The web sends no device times except `capturedAt` (the moment a file was picked), which is browser time and needs no correction beyond the 2-minute tolerance.
- **Refusals** (all `4xx` are permanent; network errors and `5xx` are retryable; a concurrent duplicate `POST /tasks` with the same id may produce one `500`, and the retry returns the task):
  - `409 TASK_ALREADY_TAKEN`: a teammate accepted the team task first. Remove it locally.
  - `409 TASK_STATE_INVALID`: the task changed (cancelled, reassigned back to `new`, already in review…). Refresh it from `/me/sync`.
  - `404` on a task command: the caller can no longer read the task (reassigned away). Treat it like `TASK_ALREADY_TAKEN`.
  - `422 EVIDENCE_REQUIRED` (with `fields['evidence.photo' | 'evidence.video']`), `422 EVIDENCE_LIVE_ONLY`, `422 MEDIA_*`, `400 VALIDATION_FAILED` (`fields.mediaIds`): show on the task.
- **Sync:** pass the ids of every locally held task as `knownTaskIds`; delete the returned `removedTaskIds` (keep drafts with unsynced commands until they resolve). `tasks` holds: non-final tasks assigned to the user, unaccepted tasks of the user's teams, and the user's tasks closed in the last 7 days. Tasks awaiting someone's *review* come from `GET /tasks?awaitingMyReview=true`, not sync.
- **Reads:** `GET /tasks/:id`, `GET /task-media/:id/url` and `GET /tasks/:id/problem-media/:mediaId/url` answer `404` to anyone who cannot read the task (spec §6.1). `GET /tasks` lists only readable tasks; `tasks.view` widens it to the data scope.
- **Media status:** `TaskDetail.media` / `SyncTask.media` and `ProblemDto.media` carry each medium's `kind` and `status`. Sign only `uploaded` media; a `pending` one (not uploaded yet, or purged) is drawn as not uploaded and its URL would answer `422 MEDIA_NOT_FOUND_IN_STORAGE`.
- **Assignee picker:** `GET /tasks/assignees` is `tasks.manage`-only (`403` otherwise), so `tasks.view`-only holders (auditors) get no person-or-team picker. Its `sites` are the site options for create and edit.
- **Browser uploads (web):** the presigned `PUT` (with `ticket.headers`: `Content-Type`, `Content-Length`) and the presigned `GET` go straight from the browser to storage. Locally the `taskop-media` bucket allows them from `http://localhost:5173` and `http://localhost:4173` (Task 17, `docker/seaweedfs/cors.json`); in production from `https://admin.taskop.app` (Deployment plan note). The browser sets `Content-Length` itself.
- **Problem → task (web):** the API takes the site from the problem and defaults `requiresReview` to `true`; the web pre-fills title (`itemLabel ?? ''`), instructions (`note ?? ''`) and priority (`high` for `critical`). `ProblemDto.executionState === 'active'` means the run is open (`PROBLEM_NOT_CLOSED`).
- **Reassign:** `PATCH` with `assignee` resets the task to `new` (history row) and clears `acceptedAt`/`startedAt`; earlier submissions stay.

```ts
      dueAt: null,
      evidence: DEFAULT_TASK_EVIDENCE,
      assignee: { teamId: ID },
      problemId: null,
    });
  });

  it('enforces the spec §4 limits', () => {
    const base = { id: ID, title: 'x', assignee: { userId: ID } };
    expect(createTaskSchema.safeParse({ ...base, title: ' ' }).success).toBe(false);
    expect(createTaskSchema.safeParse({ ...base, title: 'x'.repeat(201) }).success).toBe(false);
    expect(createTaskSchema.safeParse({ ...base, title: 'x'.repeat(200), instructions: 'y'.repeat(5000) }).success).toBe(true);
    expect(createTaskSchema.safeParse({ ...base, instructions: 'y'.repeat(5001) }).success).toBe(false);
    expect(cancelTaskSchema.safeParse({ reason: '' }).success).toBe(false);
    expect(cancelTaskSchema.safeParse({ reason: 'r'.repeat(1001) }).success).toBe(false);
    expect(submissionSchema.safeParse({ id: ID, submittedAt: AT, mediaIds: Array(14).fill(ID) }).success).toBe(false);
    expect(submissionSchema.parse({ id: ID, submittedAt: AT })).toEqual({ id: ID, text: '', mediaIds: [], submittedAt: AT });
    expect(updateTaskSchema.parse({})).toEqual({});
  });

  it('takes exactly one assignee', () => {
    expect(taskAssigneeSchema.safeParse({ userId: ID }).success).toBe(true);
    expect(taskAssigneeSchema.safeParse({ teamId: ID }).success).toBe(true);
    expect(taskAssigneeSchema.safeParse({ userId: ID, teamId: ID }).success).toBe(false);
    expect(taskAssigneeSchema.safeParse({}).success).toBe(false);
  });

  it('needs a comment to reject', () => {
    expect(reviewTaskSchema.safeParse({ decision: 'approve' }).success).toBe(true);
    const r = reviewTaskSchema.safeParse({ decision: 'reject', comment: '  ' });
    expect(r.success).toBe(false);
    expect(r.error!.issues).toEqual([expect.objectContaining({ path: ['comment'], message: 'tasks.issues.commentRequired' })]);
  });

  it('parses list filters from the query string', () => {
    expect(taskListQuerySchema.parse({ status: 'new,in_progress', overdue: 'true', awaitingMyReview: 'false' })).toMatchObject({
      status: ['new', 'in_progress'],

      awaitingMyReview: false,
      limit: 50,

    expect(taskMediaRegisterSchema.parse(m)).toEqual(m);
    expect(taskMediaRegisterSchema.safeParse({ ...m, role: 'problem' }).success).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

`packages/contracts/src/tasks.ts`:

```ts
import { z } from 'zod';
import { cursorQuerySchema, idSchema, isoDateTimeSchema } from './common.js';
import { MEDIA_KINDS, type MediaKind, mediaKindSchema, mediaSourceSchema } from './executions.js';

/** Spec §3. `completed` and `cancelled` are final: nothing leaves them. */
export const TASK_STATUSES = ['new', 'accepted', 'in_progress', 'awaiting_review', 'completed', 'cancelled'] as const;
export const taskStatusSchema = z.enum(TASK_STATUSES);
export type TaskStatus = z.infer<typeof taskStatusSchema>;
export const FINAL_TASK_STATUSES = ['completed', 'cancelled'] as const satisfies readonly TaskStatus[];
export const isFinalTaskStatus = (s: TaskStatus): boolean => (FINAL_TASK_STATUSES as readonly TaskStatus[]).includes(s);

export const TASK_PRIORITIES = ['normal', 'high'] as const;
export const taskPrioritySchema = z.enum(TASK_PRIORITIES);
export type TaskPriority = z.infer<typeof taskPrioritySchema>;

export const TASK_ACTIONS = ['accept', 'start', 'submit', 'approve', 'reject', 'cancel', 'reassign'] as const;
export const taskActionSchema = z.enum(TASK_ACTIONS);
export type TaskAction = z.infer<typeof taskActionSchema>;

export const EVIDENCE_LEVELS = ['none', 'optional', 'required'] as const;
export const evidenceLevelSchema = z.enum(EVIDENCE_LEVELS);
export type EvidenceLevel = z.infer<typeof evidenceLevelSchema>;

export const taskEvidenceSchema = z.object({ photo: evidenceLevelSchema, video: evidenceLevelSchema, liveOnly: z.boolean() });
export type TaskEvidence = z.infer<typeof taskEvidenceSchema>;
export const DEFAULT_TASK_EVIDENCE: TaskEvidence = { photo: 'optional', video: 'optional', liveOnly: false };

export const TASK_MEDIA_ROLES = ['instruction', 'response'] as const;
export const taskMediaRoleSchema = z.enum(TASK_MEDIA_ROLES);
export type TaskMediaRole = z.infer<typeof taskMediaRoleSchema>;

/** Stored on a submission once reviewed. */
export const TASK_REVIEW_DECISIONS = ['approved', 'rejected'] as const;
export const taskReviewDecisionSchema = z.enum(TASK_REVIEW_DECISIONS);
export type TaskReviewDecision = z.infer<typeof taskReviewDecisionSchema>;

export const TASK_SOURCES = ['problem', 'adhoc'] as const;
export const taskSourceSchema = z.enum(TASK_SOURCES);
export type TaskSource = z.infer<typeof taskSourceSchema>;

/** A problem's resolution, derived from its task (spec §5). The strings are also i18n keys under tasks.resolutions. */
export const PROBLEM_RESOLUTIONS = ['none', 'open', 'resolved', 'cancelled'] as const;
export const problemResolutionSchema = z.enum(PROBLEM_RESOLUTIONS);
export type ProblemResolution = z.infer<typeof problemResolutionSchema>;

/** History comments the system writes; reviewers' comments and cancel reasons are free text. */
export const TASK_HISTORY_REASONS = ['assignee_deactivated'] as const;
export type TaskHistoryReason = (typeof TASK_HISTORY_REASONS)[number];

/** Spec §4. File sizes, types and resizing are MEDIA_LIMITS. */
export const TASK_LIMITS = {
  titleMax: 200,
  instructionsMax: 5000,
  submissionTextMax: 5000,
  /** Cancel reason and review comment. */
  commentMax: 1000,
  instructionMedia: 5,
  responsePhotos: 10,
  responseVideos: 3,
  /** Closed tasks stay on the phone this long after closing (spec §6.4). */
  syncClosedDays: 7,
  knownTaskIds: 200,
} as const;

export const TASK_ISSUE_CODES = [
  'tasks.issues.commentRequired',
  'tasks.issues.unknownMedia',
  'tasks.issues.tooManyMedia',
  'tasks.issues.photoRequired',
  'tasks.issues.videoRequired',
] as const;
export type TaskIssueCode = (typeof TASK_ISSUE_CODES)[number];

/** 'submitted' resolves to awaiting_review or completed by the task's review setting. */
const MOVES: Record<TaskStatus, Partial<Record<TaskAction, TaskStatus | 'submitted'>>> = {
  new: { accept: 'accepted', start: 'in_progress', cancel: 'cancelled', reassign: 'new' },
  accepted: { start: 'in_progress', cancel: 'cancelled', reassign: 'new' },
  in_progress: { submit: 'submitted', cancel: 'cancelled', reassign: 'new' },
  awaiting_review: { approve: 'completed', reject: 'in_progress', cancel: 'cancelled', reassign: 'new' },
  completed: {},
  cancelled: {},
};

/** The spec §3 status table: the next status, or null when the action is refused. Shared by the phone and the API. */
export function taskTransition(status: TaskStatus, action: TaskAction, opts: { requiresReview: boolean }): TaskStatus | null {
  const next = MOVES[status][action];
  if (next === undefined) return null;
  if (next === 'submitted') return opts.requiresReview ? 'awaiting_review' : 'completed';
  return next;
}

export type TaskEvidenceResult = { ok: true } | { ok: false; missing: MediaKind[] };

/** FR-12.01–02: a required kind needs at least one medium of that kind in the submission. */
export function taskEvidenceMet(evidence: TaskEvidence, media: ReadonlyArray<{ kind: MediaKind }>): TaskEvidenceResult {
  const missing = MEDIA_KINDS.filter((k) => evidence[k] === 'required' && !media.some((m) => m.kind === k));
  return missing.length ? { ok: false, missing } : { ok: true };
}

/** Spec §5: no task → none; task not final → open; completed → resolved; cancelled → cancelled. */
export function problemResolutionOf(taskStatus: TaskStatus | null): ProblemResolution {
  if (taskStatus === null) return 'none';
  if (taskStatus === 'completed') return 'resolved';
  if (taskStatus === 'cancelled') return 'cancelled';
  return 'open';
}

/** A person or a team, exactly one (spec §4). */
export const taskAssigneeSchema = z.union([z.strictObject({ userId: idSchema }), z.strictObject({ teamId: idSchema })]);
export type TaskAssignee = z.infer<typeof taskAssigneeSchema>;

const titleSchema = z.string().trim().min(1).max(TASK_LIMITS.titleMax);
const instructionsSchema = z.string().trim().max(TASK_LIMITS.instructionsMax);

export const createTaskSchema = z.object({
  /** Client-generated UUIDv7: repeating the request returns the stored task. */
  id: idSchema,
  title: titleSchema,
  instructions: instructionsSchema.default(''),
  priority: taskPrioritySchema.default('normal'),
  /** Ignored when problemId is given: the problem's site is used. */
  siteId: idSchema.nullable().default(null),
  dueAt: isoDateTimeSchema.nullable().default(null),
  evidence: taskEvidenceSchema.default(() => ({ ...DEFAULT_TASK_EVIDENCE })),
  /** Defaults to true for a task created from a problem, false otherwise (spec §1). */
  requiresReview: z.boolean().optional(),
  assignee: taskAssigneeSchema,
  problemId: idSchema.nullable().default(null),
});
export type CreateTaskInput = z.input<typeof createTaskSchema>;

/** Changing `assignee` reassigns: back to `new` (spec §3). */
export const updateTaskSchema = z.object({
  title: titleSchema.optional(),
  instructions: instructionsSchema.optional(),
  priority: taskPrioritySchema.optional(),
  siteId: idSchema.nullable().optional(),
  dueAt: isoDateTimeSchema.nullable().optional(),
  evidence: taskEvidenceSchema.optional(),
  requiresReview: z.boolean().optional(),
  assignee: taskAssigneeSchema.optional(),
});
export type UpdateTaskInput = z.input<typeof updateTaskSchema>;

export const cancelTaskSchema = z.object({ reason: z.string().trim().min(1).max(TASK_LIMITS.commentMax) });
export type CancelTaskInput = z.input<typeof cancelTaskSchema>;

export const reviewTaskSchema = z
  .object({ decision: z.enum(['approve', 'reject']), comment: z.string().trim().max(TASK_LIMITS.commentMax).optional() })
  .superRefine((v, ctx) => {
    if (v.decision === 'reject' && !v.comment) ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: ['comment'], message: 'tasks.issues.commentRequired' });
  });
export type ReviewTaskInput = z.input<typeof reviewTaskSchema>;

/** Accept and start carry the device time of the tap (spec §6.3). */
export const taskActionCommandSchema = z.object({ at: isoDateTimeSchema });
export type TaskActionCommand = z.input<typeof taskActionCommandSchema>;

export const submissionSchema = z.object({
  /** Client-generated UUIDv7: the command is idempotent on it. */
  id: idSchema,
  text: z.string().trim().max(TASK_LIMITS.submissionTextMax).default(''),
  /** Registered response media of this task; per kind ≤ responsePhotos / responseVideos (checked by the API). */
  mediaIds: z.array(idSchema).max(TASK_LIMITS.responsePhotos + TASK_LIMITS.responseVideos).default([]),
  submittedAt: isoDateTimeSchema,
});
export type SubmissionInput = z.input<typeof submissionSchema>;

export const taskMediaRegisterSchema = z.object({
  id: idSchema,
  role: taskMediaRoleSchema,
  kind: mediaKindSchema,
  source: mediaSourceSchema,
  mime: z.string().max(100),
  bytes: z.number().int().min(1),
  width: z.number().int().min(1).max(20_000).nullable().optional(),
  height: z.number().int().min(1).max(20_000).nullable().optional(),
  durationMs: z.number().int().min(0).nullable().optional(),
  capturedAt: isoDateTimeSchema,
});
export type TaskMediaRegisterInput = z.input<typeof taskMediaRegisterSchema>;

/** `status=new,in_progress` in the query string. */
const statusList = z
  .string()
  .transform((s) => s.split(',').filter(Boolean))
  .pipe(z.array(taskStatusSchema).min(1));

export const taskListQuerySchema = cursorQuerySchema.extend({
  status: statusList.optional(),
  overdue: z.stringbool().optional(),
  siteId: idSchema.optional(),
  assigneeUserId: idSchema.optional(),
  teamId: idSchema.optional(),
  createdBy: idSchema.optional(),
  source: taskSourceSchema.optional(),
  /** Tasks in awaiting_review that the caller may review. */
  awaitingMyReview: z.stringbool().optional(),
  dueFrom: isoDateTimeSchema.optional(),
  dueTo: isoDateTimeSchema.optional(),
});
export type TaskListQuery = z.input<typeof taskListQuerySchema>;

export const taskAssigneeQuerySchema = z.object({ q: z.string().trim().max(100).optional() });
export type TaskAssigneeQuery = z.input<typeof taskAssigneeQuerySchema>;
```

```ts
  ],
  problem: {
    id: ID, executionId: ID, occurrenceId: ID, localDate: '2026-11-02', siteId: ID, siteName: 'Anbar №1', checklistId: ID,
    checklistName: 'Açılış', itemId: ID, itemLabel: 'Temperatur', source: 'manual', severity: 'critical', note: 'Qapı bağlanmır',
    mediaIds: [ID], media: [{ id: ID, kind: 'video', status: 'pending' }], executorName: 'Elvin', createdAt: AT, resolution: 'open',
    taskId: ID, executionState: 'partial',

  canManage: true,
  canReview: true,
};

describe('task read models', () => {
  it('parses a task detail and strips history and rights for the phone', () => {

    expect(syncTaskSchema.parse(detail)).toEqual(phone);

    expect(syncQuerySchema.safeParse({ knownTaskIds: Array(201).fill(ID).join(',') }).success).toBe(false);

    expect(problemDtoSchema.safeParse({ ...detail.problem, media: [{ id: ID, kind: 'video', status: 'purged' }] }).success).toBe(false);
    expect(problemListQuerySchema.parse({ from: '2026-11-01', to: '2026-11-30', resolution: 'none' })).toMatchObject({ resolution: 'none' });
    expect(problemListQuerySchema.safeParse({ from: '2026-11-01', to: '2026-11-30', resolution: 'fixed' }).success).toBe(false);
  });

  it('gives the assignee picker people, teams and the sites a task may name', () => {

    expect(taskAssigneeOptionsSchema.parse(options)).toEqual(options);
    expect(taskAssigneeOptionsSchema.safeParse({ users: [], teams: [] }).success).toBe(false);
  });

  it('registers the task error codes, permissions and audit actions', () => {
    expect(ERROR_HTTP_STATUS.TASK_ALREADY_TAKEN).toBe(409);
    expect(ERROR_HTTP_STATUS.TASK_STATE_INVALID).toBe(409);
    expect(ERROR_HTTP_STATUS.TASK_ASSIGNEE_INVALID).toBe(422);
    expect(ERROR_HTTP_STATUS.PROBLEM_NOT_CLOSED).toBe(409);
    expect(ERROR_HTTP_STATUS.PROBLEM_ALREADY_HAS_TASK).toBe(409);
    expect(ERROR_HTTP_STATUS.EVIDENCE_REQUIRED).toBe(422);
    expect(ALL_PERMISSIONS).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining(['tasks.view', 'tasks.manage']));
    expect(SYSTEM_ROLE_DEFAULTS.manager.permissions).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining(['tasks.view', 'tasks.manage']));
    expect(SYSTEM_ROLE_DEFAULTS.auditor.permissions).toContain('tasks.view');
    expect(SYSTEM_ROLE_DEFAULTS.auditor.permissions).not.toContain('tasks.manage');
    expect(SYSTEM_ROLE_DEFAULTS.worker.permissions).toEqual([]);
    expect(TASK_AUDIT_ACTIONS).toEqual(['task.created', 'task.updated', 'task.reassigned', 'task.cancelled', 'task.approved', 'task.rejected']);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Add the task read models**

`packages/contracts/src/task-views.ts`:

```ts
import { z } from 'zod';
import { idSchema, isoDateTimeSchema } from './common.js';
import { problemDtoSchema } from './execution-views.js';
import { mediaKindSchema, mediaSourceSchema, mediaStatusSchema } from './executions.js';
import { userRefSchema } from './occurrences.js';
import { taskEvidenceSchema, taskMediaRoleSchema, taskPrioritySchema, taskReviewDecisionSchema, taskSourceSchema, taskStatusSchema } from './tasks.js';

export const taskSiteRefSchema = z.object({ id: idSchema, name: z.string() });
export const taskTeamRefSchema = z.object({ id: idSchema, name: z.string() });

/** A row of GET /tasks (spec §6.2). */
export const taskSummarySchema = z.object({
  id: idSchema,
  title: z.string(),
  priority: taskPrioritySchema,
  status: taskStatusSchema,
  site: taskSiteRefSchema.nullable(),
  /** Null while a team task waits for a member to accept it. */
  assignee: userRefSchema.nullable(),
  team: taskTeamRefSchema.nullable(),
  /** The assignee was deactivated; a manager should reassign (spec §6.5). */
  assigneeInactive: z.boolean(),
  createdBy: userRefSchema,
  requiresReview: z.boolean(),
  source: taskSourceSchema,
  problemId: idSchema.nullable(),
  dueAt: isoDateTimeSchema.nullable(),
  /** Set by the sweep once due; completion does not clear it (spec §3). */
  overdueAt: isoDateTimeSchema.nullable(),
  rev: z.number().int(),
  createdAt: isoDateTimeSchema,
  updatedAt: isoDateTimeSchema,
});
export type TaskSummary = z.infer<typeof taskSummarySchema>;

export const taskMediaDtoSchema = z.object({
  id: idSchema,
  role: taskMediaRoleSchema,
  /** The submission that lists it; null for instruction media and unsubmitted responses. */
  submissionId: idSchema.nullable(),
  kind: mediaKindSchema,
  source: mediaSourceSchema,
  mime: z.string(),
  bytes: z.number().int(),
  width: z.number().int().nullable(),
  height: z.number().int().nullable(),
  durationMs: z.number().int().nullable(),
  capturedAt: isoDateTimeSchema,
  capturedBy: userRefSchema,
  status: mediaStatusSchema,
  uploadedAt: isoDateTimeSchema.nullable(),
});
export type TaskMediaDto = z.infer<typeof taskMediaDtoSchema>;

export const taskSubmissionDtoSchema = z.object({
  id: idSchema,
  submittedBy: userRefSchema,
  text: z.string(),
  mediaIds: z.array(idSchema),
  /** Device time (bounded); receivedAt is the server's. */
  submittedAt: isoDateTimeSchema,
  receivedAt: isoDateTimeSchema,
  reviewDecision: taskReviewDecisionSchema.nullable(),
  reviewComment: z.string().nullable(),
  reviewedBy: userRefSchema.nullable(),
  reviewedAt: isoDateTimeSchema.nullable(),
});
export type TaskSubmissionDto = z.infer<typeof taskSubmissionDtoSchema>;

export const taskHistoryEntrySchema = z.object({
  id: idSchema,
  fromStatus: taskStatusSchema.nullable(),
  toStatus: taskStatusSchema,
  at: isoDateTimeSchema,
  /** Null for the system (the deactivation listener). */
  actor: userRefSchema.nullable(),
  /** A reviewer's comment, a cancel reason, or a TASK_HISTORY_REASONS code. */
  comment: z.string().nullable(),
});
export type TaskHistoryEntry = z.infer<typeof taskHistoryEntrySchema>;

/** GET /tasks/:id (spec §6.2): everything the web drawer and the phone's task screen show. */
export const taskDetailSchema = taskSummarySchema.extend({
  instructions: z.string(),
  evidence: taskEvidenceSchema,
  acceptedAt: isoDateTimeSchema.nullable(),
  startedAt: isoDateTimeSchema.nullable(),
  submittedAt: isoDateTimeSchema.nullable(),
  completedAt: isoDateTimeSchema.nullable(),
  cancelledAt: isoDateTimeSchema.nullable(),
  cancelReason: z.string().nullable(),
  /** Instruction and response media, by capture time. */
  media: z.array(taskMediaDtoSchema),
  /** Oldest first; every submission is kept (FR-14.10 groundwork). */
  submissions: z.array(taskSubmissionDtoSchema),
  /** The source problem of a task created from one. */
  problem: problemDtoSchema.nullable(),
  history: z.array(taskHistoryEntrySchema),
  /** The caller may edit, reassign and cancel (spec §6.1). */
  canManage: z.boolean(),
  /** The caller may approve or reject. */
  canReview: z.boolean(),
});
export type TaskDetail = z.infer<typeof taskDetailSchema>;

/** The phone's copy of a task in GET /me/sync (spec §6.4): no history, no caller rights. */
export const syncTaskSchema = taskDetailSchema.omit({ history: true, canManage: true, canReview: true });
export type SyncTask = z.infer<typeof syncTaskSchema>;

/** GET /tasks/assignees: the person-or-team picker. */
export const taskAssigneeOptionsSchema = z.object({
  users: z.array(z.object({ id: idSchema, fullName: z.string(), jobTitle: z.string().nullable(), kind: z.enum(['worker', 'staff']) })),
  teams: z.array(z.object({ id: idSchema, name: z.string(), memberCount: z.number().int() })),
  /** Active sites the caller may put on a task (Spec decision 25); not filtered by `q`. */
  sites: z.array(taskSiteRefSchema),
});
export type TaskAssigneeOptions = z.infer<typeof taskAssigneeOptionsSchema>;
```

`packages/contracts/src/sync.ts`:

```ts
import { z } from 'zod';
import { idSchema, isoDateTimeSchema } from './common.js';
import { myExecutionSchema, syncChecklistVersionSchema, syncOccurrenceSchema } from './execution-views.js';
import { syncTaskSchema } from './task-views.js';
import { TASK_LIMITS } from './tasks.js';

/** `a,b` in the query string. */
const idList = (max: number) =>
  z
    .string()
    .optional()
    .transform((s) => (s ? s.split(',').filter(Boolean) : []))
    .pipe(z.array(idSchema).max(max));

export const syncQuerySchema = z.object({
  /** Checklist versions the phone already holds. */
  knownVersionIds: idList(200),
  /** Tasks the phone holds; those no longer in `tasks` come back in `removedTaskIds` (spec §6.4). */
  knownTaskIds: idList(TASK_LIMITS.knownTaskIds),
});
export type SyncQuery = z.input<typeof syncQuerySchema>;

export const syncResponseSchema = z.object({
  serverTime: isoDateTimeSchema,
  occurrences: z.array(syncOccurrenceSchema),
  checklistVersions: z.array(syncChecklistVersionSchema),
  executions: z.array(myExecutionSchema),
  /** The complete current set: open tasks of the user, unaccepted team tasks, and tasks closed in the last 7 days. */
  tasks: z.array(syncTaskSchema),
  removedTaskIds: z.array(idSchema),
});
export type SyncResponse = z.infer<typeof syncResponseSchema>;
```

```ts
/** Sub-project 5: tasks on the web and the phone. */
export function createTasksApi(c: ApiClient) {
  return {
    tasks: {
      list: (query: TaskListQuery = {}) => c.request('GET', '/tasks', { query: q(query), schema: pageOf(taskSummarySchema) }),
      get: (id: string) => c.request('GET', `/tasks/${id}`, { schema: taskDetailSchema }),
      create: (body: CreateTaskInput) => c.request('POST', '/tasks', { body, schema: taskDetailSchema }),
      update: (id: string, body: UpdateTaskInput) => c.request('PATCH', `/tasks/${id}`, { body, schema: taskDetailSchema }),
      cancel: (id: string, body: CancelTaskInput) => c.request('POST', `/tasks/${id}/cancel`, { body, schema: taskDetailSchema }),
      review: (id: string, body: ReviewTaskInput) => c.request('POST', `/tasks/${id}/review`, { body, schema: taskDetailSchema }),
      accept: (id: string, body: TaskActionCommand) => c.request('POST', `/tasks/${id}/accept`, { body, schema: taskDetailSchema }),
      start: (id: string, body: TaskActionCommand) => c.request('POST', `/tasks/${id}/start`, { body, schema: taskDetailSchema }),
      submit: (id: string, body: SubmissionInput) => c.request('POST', `/tasks/${id}/submissions`, { body, schema: taskDetailSchema }),
      registerMedia: (id: string, body: TaskMediaRegisterInput) => c.request('POST', `/tasks/${id}/media`, { body, schema: mediaUploadTicketSchema }),
      assignees: (query: TaskAssigneeQuery = {}) => c.request('GET', '/tasks/assignees', { query: q(query), schema: taskAssigneeOptionsSchema }),
      problemMediaUrl: (taskId: string, mediaId: string) => c.request('GET', `/tasks/${taskId}/problem-media/${mediaId}/url`, { schema: mediaUrlSchema }),
    },
    taskMedia: {
      confirmUploaded: (id: string) => c.request('POST', `/task-media/${id}/uploaded`, { body: {}, schema: mediaConfirmResultSchema }),
      url: (id: string) => c.request('GET', `/task-media/${id}/url`, { schema: mediaUrlSchema }),
    },
  };
}
export type TasksApi = ReturnType<typeof createTasksApi>;
```

```ts
import { uuidv7 } from 'uuidv7';
import { ownerQuery } from './owner-db';

export interface TaskRowInput {
  tenantId: string;
  createdBy: string;
  assigneeUserId?: string | null;
  assigneeTeamId?: string | null;
  problemId?: string | null;
  status?: string;
  id?: string;
}

/** A task row straight in the DB (owner connection, no API), for tests that only need a task to exist. */
export async function insertTaskRow(v: TaskRowInput): Promise<string> {
  const id = v.id ?? uuidv7();
  await ownerQuery(
    `insert into tasks (id, tenant_id, title, created_by_user_id, assignee_user_id, assignee_team_id, problem_id, status)
     values ($1, $2, 'Test tapşırığı', $3, $4, $5, $6, $7)`,
    [id, v.tenantId, v.createdBy, v.assigneeUserId ?? null, v.assigneeTeamId ?? null, v.problemId ?? null, v.status ?? 'new'],
  );
  return id;
}
```

`apps/api/test/tasks-db.test.ts`:

```ts
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import pg from 'pg';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, inject, it } from 'vitest';
import { createTestApp, type TestApp } from './app';
import { answersBody, claimOk, executionWorld } from './execution-fixtures';
import { FakeClock } from './fake-clock';
import { signupTenant } from './fixtures';
import { ownerQuery } from './owner-db';
import { MONDAY_0800 } from './scheduling-fixtures';
import { insertTaskRow } from './task-fixtures';

const TABLES = ['tasks', 'task_submissions', 'task_media', 'task_status_history'];

async function asApp<T>(tenantId: string, fn: (c: pg.PoolClient) => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
  const pool = new pg.Pool({ connectionString: inject('db').appUrl, max: 1 });
  const c = await pool.connect();
  try {
    await c.query('begin');
    await c.query("select set_config('app.tenant_id', $1, true)", [tenantId]);
    return await fn(c);
  } finally {
    await c.query('rollback').catch(() => undefined);
    c.release();
    await pool.end();
  }
}

describe('task tables', () => {
  const clock = new FakeClock(MONDAY_0800);
  let t: TestApp;
  beforeAll(async () => {
    t = await createTestApp({}, { clock });
  });
  afterAll(() => t.close());

  it('forces row level security on every new table', async () => {
    const r = await ownerQuery<{ relname: string; relrowsecurity: boolean; relforcerowsecurity: boolean }>(
      'select relname, relrowsecurity, relforcerowsecurity from pg_class where relname = any($1) order by relname',
      [TABLES],
    );
    expect(r.rows).toHaveLength(TABLES.length);
    for (const row of r.rows) expect(row, row.relname).toMatchObject({ relrowsecurity: true, relforcerowsecurity: true });
  });

  it('needs a person or a team, and hides another tenant’s tasks', async () => {
    const a = await signupTenant(t);
    const b = await signupTenant(t);
    await expect(insertTaskRow({ tenantId: a.tenantId, createdBy: a.ownerId })).rejects.toThrow(/tasks_assignee_ck/);
    const id = await insertTaskRow({ tenantId: a.tenantId, createdBy: a.ownerId, assigneeUserId: a.ownerId });
    expect((await asApp(b.tenantId, (c) => c.query('select id from tasks'))).rowCount).toBe(0);
    expect((await asApp(a.tenantId, (c) => c.query('select id from tasks'))).rows).toEqual([{ id }]);
  });

  it('keeps history append-only and never lets the app delete task rows', async () => {
    const a = await signupTenant(t);
    const id = await insertTaskRow({ tenantId: a.tenantId, createdBy: a.ownerId, assigneeUserId: a.ownerId });
    await ownerQuery("insert into task_status_history (tenant_id, task_id, to_status, at) values ($1, $2, 'new', now())", [a.tenantId, id]);
    await asApp(a.tenantId, async (c) => {
      for (const stmt of ["update task_status_history set comment = 'x'", 'delete from task_status_history', 'delete from task_media', 'delete from task_submissions', 'delete from tasks']) {
        await c.query('savepoint sp');
        await expect(c.query(stmt), stmt).rejects.toThrow(/permission denied/);
        await c.query('rollback to savepoint sp');
      }
      expect((await c.query("update tasks set title = 'Yeni ad'")).rowCount).toBe(1);
    });
  });

  it('links one task per problem and keeps the problem while the task exists', async () => {
    const w = await executionWorld(t);
    clock.set('2026-11-02T04:20:00Z');
    const executionId = await claimOk(w.workers[0].api, w.occurrenceId, '2026-11-02T04:10:00.000Z');
    const saved = await w.workers[0].api.put(
      `/api/v1/executions/${executionId}/answers`,
      answersBody(1, { [w.c.temp.id]: { number: 5, problem: { severity: 'normal', note: 'Qapı bağlanmır', mediaIds: [] } } }, '2026-11-02T04:20:00.000Z'),
    );
    expect(saved.status, JSON.stringify(saved.body)).toBe(200);
    const problemId = (await ownerQuery<{ id: string }>('select id from execution_problems where execution_id = $1', [executionId])).rows[0]!.id;
    const task = { tenantId: w.s.tenantId, createdBy: w.s.ownerId, assigneeUserId: w.workers[1].id, problemId };
    await insertTaskRow(task);
    await expect(insertTaskRow(task)).rejects.toThrow(/tasks_problem_uq/);
    await expect(ownerQuery('delete from execution_problems where id = $1', [problemId])).rejects.toThrow(/tasks_problem_fk/);
  });

  it('backfills the task keys for existing Admin, Manager and Auditor roles', async () => {
    const s = await signupTenant(t);
    const roleOf = async (key: string) => (await ownerQuery<{ id: string }>('select id from roles where tenant_id = $1 and system_key = $2', [s.tenantId, key])).rows[0]!.id;
    const roles = { admin: await roleOf('admin'), manager: await roleOf('manager'), auditor: await roleOf('auditor'), worker: await roleOf('worker') };
    await ownerQuery("delete from role_permissions where role_id = any($1) and permission_key like 'tasks.%'", [Object.values(roles)]);
    const file = readFileSync(path.resolve(__dirname, '../drizzle/0010_tasks_security.sql'), 'utf8');
    const stmts = file.split('--> statement-breakpoint').map((x) => x.trim());
    const start = stmts.findIndex((x) => /row_security\s*=\s*off/i.test(x));
    const client = new pg.Client({ connectionString: inject('db').ownerUrl });
    await client.connect();
    try {
      await client.query('begin');
      for (const stmt of stmts.slice(start)) await client.query(stmt);
      await client.query('commit');
    } finally {
      await client.end();
    }
    const keys = async (roleId: string) =>
      (await ownerQuery<{ k: string }>("select permission_key as k from role_permissions where role_id = $1 and permission_key like 'tasks.%' order by 1", [roleId])).rows.map((r) => r.k);
    expect(await keys(roles.admin)).toEqual(['tasks.manage', 'tasks.view']);
    expect(await keys(roles.manager)).toEqual(['tasks.manage', 'tasks.view']);
    expect(await keys(roles.auditor)).toEqual(['tasks.view']);
    expect(await keys(roles.worker)).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

2. Append at the end of the file:

```ts
// Must match TASK_STATUSES, TASK_PRIORITIES, TASK_MEDIA_ROLES and TASK_REVIEW_DECISIONS in @taskop/contracts.
export const taskStatus = pgEnum('task_status', ['new', 'accepted', 'in_progress', 'awaiting_review', 'completed', 'cancelled']);
export const taskPriority = pgEnum('task_priority', ['normal', 'high']);
export const taskMediaRole = pgEnum('task_media_role', ['instruction', 'response']);
export const taskReviewDecision = pgEnum('task_review_decision', ['approved', 'rejected']);

/** One-off work for a person or a team (SP5 spec §5). The id is generated by the client, so creation is idempotent. */
export const tasks = pgTable(
  'tasks',
  {
    id: uuid('id').primaryKey(),
    tenantId: tenantId(),
    title: text('title').notNull(),
    instructions: text('instructions').notNull().default(''),
    priority: taskPriority('priority').notNull().default('normal'),
    siteId: uuid('site_id'),
    createdByUserId: uuid('created_by_user_id').notNull(),
    // A team task has only the team until a member accepts it; then both (spec §5).
    assigneeUserId: uuid('assignee_user_id'),
    assigneeTeamId: uuid('assignee_team_id'),
    requiresReview: boolean('requires_review').notNull().default(false),
    evidence: jsonb('evidence').notNull().default({ photo: 'optional', video: 'optional', liveOnly: false }),
    dueAt: ts('due_at'),
    // Set once by tasks.sweep; a flag, not a status (spec §3).
    overdueAt: ts('overdue_at'),
    problemId: uuid('problem_id'),
    status: taskStatus('status').notNull().default('new'),
    // Bumped on every change.
    rev: integer('rev').notNull().default(1),
    // Device times for worker steps, server times for manager steps (spec §5).
    acceptedAt: ts('accepted_at'),
    startedAt: ts('started_at'),
    submittedAt: ts('submitted_at'),
    completedAt: ts('completed_at'),
    cancelledAt: ts('cancelled_at'),
    cancelReason: text('cancel_reason'),
    createdAt: createdAt(),
    updatedAt: updatedAt(),
  },
  (t) => [
    unique('tasks_tenant_id_uq').on(t.tenantId, t.id),
    // One task per problem (spec §1).
    unique('tasks_problem_uq').on(t.problemId),
    foreignKey({ columns: [t.tenantId, t.siteId], foreignColumns: [sites.tenantId, sites.id], name: 'tasks_site_fk' }),
    foreignKey({ columns: [t.tenantId, t.createdByUserId], foreignColumns: [users.tenantId, users.id], name: 'tasks_created_by_fk' }),
    foreignKey({ columns: [t.tenantId, t.assigneeUserId], foreignColumns: [users.tenantId, users.id], name: 'tasks_assignee_user_fk' }),
    foreignKey({ columns: [t.tenantId, t.assigneeTeamId], foreignColumns: [teams.tenantId, teams.id], name: 'tasks_assignee_team_fk' }),
    foreignKey({ columns: [t.tenantId, t.problemId], foreignColumns: [executionProblems.tenantId, executionProblems.id], name: 'tasks_problem_fk' }).onDelete('restrict'),
    check('tasks_assignee_ck', sql`${t.assigneeUserId} is not null or ${t.assigneeTeamId} is not null`),
    index('tasks_status_idx').on(t.tenantId, t.status),
    index('tasks_assignee_user_idx').on(t.tenantId, t.assigneeUserId),
    index('tasks_assignee_team_idx').on(t.tenantId, t.assigneeTeamId).where(sql`assignee_user_id is null`),
    index('tasks_site_idx').on(t.tenantId, t.siteId),
    index('tasks_due_idx').on(t.tenantId, t.dueAt).where(sql`status not in ('completed', 'cancelled')`),
    index('tasks_sync_idx').on(t.tenantId, t.updatedAt, t.id),
    // GET /tasks keyset.
    index('tasks_created_idx').on(t.tenantId, t.createdAt, t.id),
  ],
);

/** Every submission is kept, with its review (FR-14.10 groundwork). */
export const taskSubmissions = pgTable(
  'task_submissions',
  {
    id: uuid('id').primaryKey(),
    tenantId: tenantId(),
    taskId: uuid('task_id').notNull(),
    submittedByUserId: uuid('submitted_by_user_id').notNull(),
    text: text('text').notNull().default(''),
    mediaIds: uuid('media_ids').array().notNull().default(sql`'{}'::uuid[]`),
    submittedAt: ts('submitted_at').notNull(),
    receivedAt: ts('received_at').notNull(),
    reviewDecision: taskReviewDecision('review_decision'),
    reviewComment: text('review_comment'),
    reviewedByUserId: uuid('reviewed_by_user_id'),
    reviewedAt: ts('reviewed_at'),
  },
  (t) => [
    unique('task_submissions_tenant_id_uq').on(t.tenantId, t.id),
    foreignKey({ columns: [t.tenantId, t.taskId], foreignColumns: [tasks.tenantId, tasks.id], name: 'task_submissions_task_fk' }),
    foreignKey({ columns: [t.tenantId, t.submittedByUserId], foreignColumns: [users.tenantId, users.id], name: 'task_submissions_submitted_by_fk' }),
    foreignKey({ columns: [t.tenantId, t.reviewedByUserId], foreignColumns: [users.tenantId, users.id], name: 'task_submissions_reviewed_by_fk' }),
    index('task_submissions_task_idx').on(t.tenantId, t.taskId, t.receivedAt),
  ],
);

/** Instruction and response photos/videos. The file lives in object storage under storage_key. */
export const taskMedia = pgTable(
  'task_media',
  {
    id: uuid('id').primaryKey(),
    tenantId: tenantId(),
    taskId: uuid('task_id').notNull(),
    // Set when the submission that lists it is accepted.
    submissionId: uuid('submission_id'),
    role: taskMediaRole('role').notNull(),
    kind: mediaKind('kind').notNull(),
    source: mediaSource('source').notNull(),
    mime: text('mime').notNull(),
    bytes: integer('bytes').notNull(),
    width: integer('width'),
    height: integer('height'),
    durationMs: integer('duration_ms'),
    capturedAt: ts('captured_at').notNull(),
    capturedByUserId: uuid('captured_by_user_id').notNull(),
    storageKey: text('storage_key').notNull(),
    status: mediaStatus('status').notNull().default('pending'),
    uploadedAt: ts('uploaded_at'),
    // Set by task-media.cleanup when it deleted the object of a never-uploaded medium; the row stays.
    storagePurgedAt: ts('storage_purged_at'),
    createdAt: createdAt(),
  },
  (t) => [
    unique('task_media_tenant_id_uq').on(t.tenantId, t.id),
    foreignKey({ columns: [t.tenantId, t.taskId], foreignColumns: [tasks.tenantId, tasks.id], name: 'task_media_task_fk' }),
    foreignKey({ columns: [t.tenantId, t.submissionId], foreignColumns: [taskSubmissions.tenantId, taskSubmissions.id], name: 'task_media_submission_fk' }),
    foreignKey({ columns: [t.tenantId, t.capturedByUserId], foreignColumns: [users.tenantId, users.id], name: 'task_media_captured_by_fk' }),
    index('task_media_task_idx').on(t.tenantId, t.taskId),
    index('task_media_status_idx').on(t.tenantId, t.status, t.createdAt),
  ],
);

/** FR-14.09: append-only. Actor null = the system. */
export const taskStatusHistory = pgTable(
  'task_status_history',
  {
    id: id(),
    tenantId: tenantId(),
    taskId: uuid('task_id').notNull(),
    fromStatus: taskStatus('from_status'),
    toStatus: taskStatus('to_status').notNull(),
    actorUserId: uuid('actor_user_id'),
    at: ts('at').notNull(),
    comment: text('comment'),
  },
  (t) => [
    foreignKey({ columns: [t.tenantId, t.taskId], foreignColumns: [tasks.tenantId, tasks.id], name: 'task_status_history_task_fk' }),
    foreignKey({ columns: [t.tenantId, t.actorUserId], foreignColumns: [users.tenantId, users.id], name: 'task_status_history_actor_fk' }),
    index('task_status_history_task_idx').on(t.tenantId, t.taskId, t.at),
  ],
);
```

```bash
pnpm --filter @taskop/api exec drizzle-kit generate --name tasks
pnpm --filter @taskop/api exec drizzle-kit generate --custom --name tasks_security
```

Check the output:
- `apps/api/drizzle/0009_tasks.sql` contains the 4 `CREATE TYPE`s, the 4 `CREATE TABLE`s, `ALTER TABLE "execution_problems" ADD CONSTRAINT "execution_problems_tenant_id_uq" UNIQUE("tenant_id","id")` (before `tasks_problem_fk`), the check, the foreign keys (`tasks_problem_fk` with `ON DELETE restrict`) and the indexes.
- The partial indexes end in `WHERE assignee_user_id is null` and `WHERE status not in ('completed', 'cancelled')`.
- `meta/_journal.json` has entries `0009_tasks` and `0010_tasks_security`; `0010_tasks_security.sql` is created empty.

Write `apps/api/drizzle/0010_tasks_security.sql`:

```sql
DO $$
DECLARE t text;
BEGIN
  FOREACH t IN ARRAY ARRAY['tasks','task_submissions','task_media','task_status_history']
  LOOP
    EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %I ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY', t);
    EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %I FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY', t);
    EXECUTE format(
      'CREATE POLICY tenant_isolation ON %I USING (tenant_id = nullif(current_setting(''app.tenant_id'', true), '''')::uuid) WITH CHECK (tenant_id = nullif(current_setting(''app.tenant_id'', true), '''')::uuid)',
      t);
  END LOOP;
END $$;
--> statement-breakpoint
-- Tasks, submissions and media are never deleted (FR-04.11); task-media.cleanup purges objects and keeps rows.
GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE ON tasks, task_submissions, task_media TO taskop_app, taskop_platform;
--> statement-breakpoint
-- FR-14.09: status history is append-only.
GRANT SELECT, INSERT ON task_status_history TO taskop_app, taskop_platform;
--> statement-breakpoint
-- The backfill below must see every tenant's rows (see 0004 for why row_security is switched off).
SET LOCAL row_security = off;
--> statement-breakpoint
-- Existing tenants get the SYSTEM_ROLE_DEFAULTS task keys (new tenants get them at signup).
INSERT INTO role_permissions (tenant_id, role_id, permission_key)
  SELECT r.tenant_id, r.id, k
  FROM roles r CROSS JOIN unnest(ARRAY['tasks.view','tasks.manage']) AS k
  WHERE r.system_key IN ('admin', 'manager')
  ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING;
--> statement-breakpoint
INSERT INTO role_permissions (tenant_id, role_id, permission_key)
  SELECT r.tenant_id, r.id, 'tasks.view' FROM roles r WHERE r.system_key = 'auditor'
  ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING;
--> statement-breakpoint
-- Bump the version so cached (roleId, version) permission sets reload.
UPDATE roles SET version = version + 1 WHERE system_key IN ('admin', 'manager', 'auditor');
--> statement-breakpoint
SET LOCAL row_security = on;
```
