### Task 6: Problem resolution and task link on the problem reads; linked problems survive late syncs

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/api/src/executions/problem-rows.ts`
- Modify: `apps/api/src/executions/problems.service.ts`, `apps/api/src/executions/execution-queries.ts`, `apps/api/src/executions/mappers.ts`, `apps/api/src/executions/problem-writer.ts`, `apps/api/test/task-fixtures.ts`
- Test: `apps/api/test/problems-resolution.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `tasks` (Task 5); `problemResolutionOf`, `ProblemResolution` (Task 1); `storedContent` from `../checklists/content`.
- Produces:
  - `selectProblemRows(tx: Executor)` — a dynamic select over `execution_problems` with place, executor, `versionId`, `executionState`, `taskId`, `taskStatus`; `type ProblemRow`
  - `toProblemDtos(tx: Executor, rows: ProblemRow[]): Promise<ProblemDto[]>` (item labels from each pinned version; `media` kind and status from `execution_media`, in `mediaIds` order)
  - `resolutionFilter(r: ProblemResolution): SQL`
  - `toProblemEntry(p, taskId, taskStatus)` (3 parameters now)
  - `GET /problems` and `GET /executions/:id` problems carry the real `resolution` and `taskId`; `GET /problems?resolution=…` filters; `GET /problems` items (and `TaskDetail.problem`) carry `media` with each medium's kind and status.
  - `ProblemWriter.rewrite` never deletes a problem that has a task.
  - Test fixture `closedRunWithProblems(t, clock)`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Append to `apps/api/test/task-fixtures.ts` (merge the imports with the existing ones at the top of the file):

```ts
import type { Answers } from '@taskop/contracts';
import { expect } from 'vitest';
import type { TestApp } from './app';
import { claimOk, completeExecution, executionWorld, registerPhoto } from './execution-fixtures';
import type { FakeClock } from './fake-clock';

/**
 * İşçi 1 completes today's run at 08:20 Baku with two problems on the temperature: a manual critical one
 * ("Kondisioner xarabdır") and the rule one (10 °C, note "isti"). The run is closed, so both can get tasks.
 */
export async function closedRunWithProblems(t: TestApp, clock: FakeClock) {
  const w = await executionWorld(t);
  const api = w.workers[0].api;
  clock.set('2026-11-02T04:20:00Z');
  const executionId = await claimOk(api, w.occurrenceId, '2026-11-02T04:10:00.000Z');
  const photo = await registerPhoto(api, executionId, w.c.photo.id);
  const answers: Answers = {
    [w.c.problem.id]: { optionIds: [w.c.no.id] },
    [w.c.temp.id]: { number: 10, note: 'isti', problem: { severity: 'critical', note: 'Kondisioner xarabdır', mediaIds: [] } },
    [w.c.photo.id]: { photos: [photo] },
  };
  const res = await completeExecution(api, executionId, 1, answers, '2026-11-02T04:20:00.000Z');
  expect(res.status, JSON.stringify(res.body)).toBe(200);
  const rows = (await ownerQuery<{ id: string; source: string }>('select id, source from execution_problems where execution_id = $1', [executionId])).rows;
  const idOf = (source: string) => rows.find((r) => r.source === source)!.id;
  return { w, executionId, manualProblemId: idOf('manual'), ruleProblemId: idOf('rule') };
}
```

`apps/api/test/problems-resolution.test.ts`:

```ts
import { afterAll, beforeAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { OccurrenceJobs } from '../src/scheduling/occurrence-jobs';
import { createTestApp, type TestApp } from './app';
import { answersBody, claimOk, executionWorld, registerPhoto } from './execution-fixtures';
import { FakeClock } from './fake-clock';
import { ownerQuery } from './owner-db';
import { MONDAY_0800 } from './scheduling-fixtures';
import { closedRunWithProblems, insertTaskRow } from './task-fixtures';

type Row = { id: string; resolution: string; taskId: string | null };
const byId = (items: Row[]) => Object.fromEntries(items.map((p) => [p.id, [p.resolution, p.taskId]]));
const ids = (items: Row[]) => items.map((p) => p.id);

describe('problem resolution', () => {
  const clock = new FakeClock(MONDAY_0800);
  let t: TestApp;
  beforeAll(async () => {
    t = await createTestApp({}, { clock });
  });
  beforeEach(() => clock.set(MONDAY_0800));
  afterAll(() => t.close());

  it('derives resolution and taskId from the linked task on /problems and the execution detail, and filters by it', async () => {
    const { w, executionId, manualProblemId, ruleProblemId } = await closedRunWithProblems(t, clock);
    const taskId = await insertTaskRow({ tenantId: w.s.tenantId, createdBy: w.s.ownerId, assigneeUserId: w.workers[1].id, problemId: manualProblemId, status: 'in_progress' });
    const get = async (qs = '') => (await w.owner.get(`/api/v1/problems?from=2026-11-01&to=2026-11-30${qs}`)).body.items as Row[];
    expect(byId(await get())).toEqual({ [manualProblemId]: ['open', taskId], [ruleProblemId]: ['none', null] });
    expect((await get())[0]).toMatchObject({ executionState: 'completed' });
    expect(ids(await get('&resolution=open'))).toEqual([manualProblemId]);
    expect(ids(await get('&resolution=none'))).toEqual([ruleProblemId]);
    expect(byId((await w.owner.get(`/api/v1/executions/${executionId}`)).body.problems)).toEqual({ [manualProblemId]: ['open', taskId], [ruleProblemId]: ['none', null] });
    await ownerQuery("update tasks set status = 'completed' where id = $1", [taskId]);
    expect(ids(await get('&resolution=resolved'))).toEqual([manualProblemId]);
    await ownerQuery("update tasks set status = 'cancelled' where id = $1", [taskId]);
    expect(ids(await get('&resolution=cancelled'))).toEqual([manualProblemId]);
    expect(await get('&resolution=open')).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('gives each problem medium its kind and status, in the order the answer lists them', async () => {
    const w = await executionWorld(t);
    const api = w.workers[0].api;
    clock.set('2026-11-02T04:20:00Z');
    const executionId = await claimOk(api, w.occurrenceId, '2026-11-02T04:10:00.000Z');
    const video = await registerPhoto(api, executionId, null, { kind: 'video', mime: 'video/mp4', width: 1280, height: 720, durationMs: 10_000 });
    const photo = await registerPhoto(api, executionId, null);
    // Uploaded and confirmed (no storage in this suite): the row is what the DTO reads.
    await ownerQuery("update execution_media set status = 'uploaded', uploaded_at = now() where id = $1", [photo]);
    const answers = answersBody(1, { [w.c.temp.id]: { number: 5, problem: { severity: 'normal', note: 'Qapı bağlanmır', mediaIds: [video, photo] } } }, '2026-11-02T04:20:00.000Z');
    expect((await api.put(`/api/v1/executions/${executionId}/answers`, answers)).status).toBe(200);
    const items = (await w.owner.get('/api/v1/problems?from=2026-11-01&to=2026-11-30')).body.items as Array<{ executionId: string; mediaIds: string[]; media: unknown[] }>;
    const p = items.find((i) => i.executionId === executionId)!;
    expect(p.mediaIds).toEqual([video, photo]);
    expect(p.media).toEqual([
      { id: video, kind: 'video', status: 'pending' },
      { id: photo, kind: 'photo', status: 'uploaded' },
    ]);
  });

  it('keeps a problem that has a task when a late answers sync no longer derives it', async () => {
    const w = await executionWorld(t);
    const api = w.workers[0].api;
    clock.set('2026-11-02T04:20:00Z');
    const executionId = await claimOk(api, w.occurrenceId, '2026-11-02T04:10:00.000Z');
    expect((await api.put(`/api/v1/executions/${executionId}/answers`, answersBody(1, { [w.c.temp.id]: { number: 10, note: 'isti' } }, '2026-11-02T04:15:00.000Z'))).status).toBe(200);
    clock.set('2026-11-02T07:00:00Z');
    await t.app.get(OccurrenceJobs).sweepAll([w.s.tenantId]);
    const problemId = (await ownerQuery<{ id: string }>('select id from execution_problems where execution_id = $1', [executionId])).rows[0]!.id;
    await insertTaskRow({ tenantId: w.s.tenantId, createdBy: w.s.ownerId, assigneeUserId: w.workers[1].id, problemId });
    // Captured at 10:30 Baku, inside the window, synced after the sweep: the temperature was fine after all.
    const late = await api.put(`/api/v1/executions/${executionId}/answers`, answersBody(2, { [w.c.temp.id]: { number: 5 } }, '2026-11-02T06:30:00.000Z'));
    expect(late.status, JSON.stringify(late.body)).toBe(200);
    expect((await ownerQuery('select id from execution_problems where execution_id = $1', [executionId])).rows).toEqual([{ id: problemId }]);
  });

  it('still deletes vanished problems that have no task', async () => {
    const w = await executionWorld(t);
    const api = w.workers[0].api;
    clock.set('2026-11-02T04:20:00Z');
    const executionId = await claimOk(api, w.occurrenceId, '2026-11-02T04:10:00.000Z');
    await api.put(`/api/v1/executions/${executionId}/answers`, answersBody(1, { [w.c.temp.id]: { number: 10, note: 'isti' } }, '2026-11-02T04:15:00.000Z'));
    await api.put(`/api/v1/executions/${executionId}/answers`, answersBody(2, { [w.c.temp.id]: { number: 5 } }, '2026-11-02T04:16:00.000Z'));
    expect((await ownerQuery('select id from execution_problems where execution_id = $1', [executionId])).rows).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

`apps/api/src/executions/problem-rows.ts`:

```ts
import { type ProblemDto, type ProblemMediaRef, type ProblemResolution, problemResolutionOf, walkItems } from '@taskop/contracts';
import { and, eq, getTableColumns, inArray, isNotNull, isNull, notInArray, type SQL } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { storedContent } from '../checklists/content';
import type { Executor } from '../db/db.service';
import { checklists, checklistVersions, executionMedia, executionProblems, executions, occurrences, sites, tasks, users } from '../db/schema';

/**
 * Problem rows with their place, executor, run state and linked task (spec §6.2 "Problem endpoints").
 * Plain functions, not a provider: the tasks module reads problem cards without importing ExecutionsModule.
 */
export function selectProblemRows(tx: Executor) {
  return tx
    .select({
      pr: getTableColumns(executionProblems),
      localDate: occurrences.localDate,
      siteName: sites.name,
      checklistName: checklists.name,
      executorName: users.fullName,
      versionId: executions.checklistVersionId,
      executionState: executions.state,
      taskId: tasks.id,
      taskStatus: tasks.status,
    })
    .from(executionProblems)
    .innerJoin(occurrences, eq(occurrences.id, executionProblems.occurrenceId))
    .innerJoin(executions, eq(executions.id, executionProblems.executionId))
    .innerJoin(users, eq(users.id, executions.executorUserId))
    .innerJoin(sites, eq(sites.id, executionProblems.siteId))
    .innerJoin(checklists, eq(checklists.id, executionProblems.checklistId))
    .leftJoin(tasks, eq(tasks.problemId, executionProblems.id))
    .$dynamic();
}
export type ProblemRow = Awaited<ReturnType<typeof selectProblemRows>>[number];

/**
 * Rows → DTOs. Item labels come from each pinned version; null if the item is not there. Media get their kind and
 * status from execution_media, in `mediaIds` order (Spec decision 24); a purged object is still `pending`.
 */
export async function toProblemDtos(tx: Executor, rows: ProblemRow[]): Promise<ProblemDto[]> {
  const mediaIds = [...new Set(rows.flatMap((r) => r.pr.mediaIds))];
  const media = mediaIds.length
    ? await tx
        .select({ id: executionMedia.id, executionId: executionMedia.executionId, kind: executionMedia.kind, status: executionMedia.status })
        .from(executionMedia)
        .where(inArray(executionMedia.id, mediaIds))
    : [];
  const mediaById = new Map(media.map((m) => [m.id, m]));
  // The answers validation only lets a problem list media of its own run; the executionId check keeps that true here.
  const refsOf = (r: ProblemRow): ProblemMediaRef[] =>
    r.pr.mediaIds.flatMap((id) => {
      const m = mediaById.get(id);
      return m && m.executionId === r.pr.executionId ? [{ id, kind: m.kind, status: m.status }] : [];
    });
  const versionIds = [...new Set(rows.map((r) => r.versionId))];
  const versions = versionIds.length
    ? await tx.select({ id: checklistVersions.id, content: checklistVersions.content }).from(checklistVersions).where(inArray(checklistVersions.id, versionIds))
    : [];
  const labels = new Map<string, Map<string, string>>();
  for (const v of versions) {
    const byItem = new Map<string, string>();
    walkItems(storedContent(v.content), (item) => byItem.set(item.id, item.label));
    labels.set(v.id, byItem);
  }
  return rows.map((r) => ({
    id: r.pr.id,
    executionId: r.pr.executionId,
    occurrenceId: r.pr.occurrenceId,
    localDate: r.localDate,
    siteId: r.pr.siteId,
    siteName: r.siteName,
    checklistId: r.pr.checklistId,
    checklistName: r.checklistName,
    itemId: r.pr.itemId,
    itemLabel: labels.get(r.versionId)?.get(r.pr.itemId) ?? null,
    source: r.pr.source,
    severity: r.pr.severity,
    note: r.pr.note,
    mediaIds: r.pr.mediaIds,
    media: refsOf(r),
    executorName: r.executorName,
    createdAt: r.pr.createdAt.toISOString(),
    resolution: problemResolutionOf(r.taskStatus),
    taskId: r.taskId,
    executionState: r.executionState,
  }));
}

/** GET /problems?resolution=… over the left-joined task. */
export function resolutionFilter(r: ProblemResolution): SQL {
  switch (r) {
    case 'none':
      return isNull(tasks.id);
    case 'open':
      return and(isNotNull(tasks.id), notInArray(tasks.status, ['completed', 'cancelled']))!;
    case 'resolved':
      return eq(tasks.status, 'completed');
    case 'cancelled':
      return eq(tasks.status, 'cancelled');
  }
}
```

Replace `apps/api/src/executions/problems.service.ts` with:

```ts
import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import type { Page, ProblemDto } from '@taskop/contracts';
import { and, between, desc, eq, type SQL, sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import type { Principal } from '../common/request';
import { DbService } from '../db/db.service';
import { executionProblems, occurrences } from '../db/schema';
import { SchedulingScope } from '../scheduling/scheduling-scope';
import type { ProblemListQueryDto } from './dto';
import { resolutionFilter, selectProblemRows, toProblemDtos } from './problem-rows';

/** GET /problems (spec §6.8): read-only, newest first, data scope through the occurrence; resolution from the task. */
@Injectable()
export class ProblemsService {
  constructor(
    private readonly db: DbService,
    private readonly scope: SchedulingScope,
  ) {}

  async list(p: Principal, q: ProblemListQueryDto): Promise<Page<ProblemDto>> {
    const conds: (SQL | undefined)[] = [this.scope.occurrences(p), between(occurrences.localDate, q.from, q.to)];
    if (q.siteId) conds.push(eq(executionProblems.siteId, q.siteId));
    if (q.checklistId) conds.push(eq(executionProblems.checklistId, q.checklistId));
    if (q.severity) conds.push(eq(executionProblems.severity, q.severity));
    if (q.source) conds.push(eq(executionProblems.source, q.source));
    if (q.resolution) conds.push(resolutionFilter(q.resolution));
    if (q.cursor) {
      conds.push(sql`(${executionProblems.createdAt}, ${executionProblems.id}) < (select c.created_at, c.id from execution_problems c where c.id = ${q.cursor})`);
    }
    const tx = this.db.tx();
    const rows = await selectProblemRows(tx)
      .where(and(...conds))
      .orderBy(desc(executionProblems.createdAt), desc(executionProblems.id))
      .limit(q.limit + 1);
    const page = rows.slice(0, q.limit);
    return { items: await toProblemDtos(tx, page), nextCursor: rows.length > q.limit ? page[page.length - 1]!.pr.id : null };
  }
}
```

```ts
import type { TaskAssignee, TaskDetail } from '@taskop/contracts';
import { DomainEvents, type TaskEventName } from '../src/common/domain-events';
import type { TestWorker } from './execution-fixtures';
import { as, createUserDirect, loginStaff, loginWorker, type SignedUpTenant, signupTenant, siteTypeIdOf } from './fixtures';
import type { Api } from './scheduling-fixtures';

export interface TaskWorld {
  s: SignedUpTenant;
  owner: Api;
  /** Filial 1 and Filial 2. */
  siteId: string;
  otherSiteId: string;
  /** "Menecer": built-in manager role (site_subtree, tasks.view + tasks.manage), linked to Filial 1. */
  manager: TestWorker;
  /** İşçi 1 and İşçi 2 at Filial 1 (both in the team), İşçi 3 at Filial 2; logged in on mobile. */
  workers: [TestWorker, TestWorker, TestWorker];
  /** "Texniki xidmət": İşçi 1 and İşçi 2. */
  teamId: string;
}

export async function taskWorld(t: TestApp): Promise<TaskWorld> {
  const s = await signupTenant(t);
  const owner = as(t, s.accessToken);
  const typeId = await siteTypeIdOf(s.tenantId);
  const siteId = (await owner.post('/api/v1/sites', { parentId: null, typeId, name: 'Filial 1' })).body.id as string;
  const otherSiteId = (await owner.post('/api/v1/sites', { parentId: null, typeId, name: 'Filial 2' })).body.id as string;
  const workers: TestWorker[] = [];
  for (const [fullName, site] of [['İşçi 1', siteId], ['İşçi 2', siteId], ['İşçi 3', otherSiteId]] as const) {
    const u = await createUserDirect(t, s.tenantId, { fullName });
    expect((await owner.put(`/api/v1/users/${u.id}/sites`, { siteIds: [site] })).status).toBe(200);
    workers.push({ id: u.id, api: as(t, (await loginWorker(t, s.orgCode, u.username!, u.secret)).accessToken) });
  }
  const m = await createUserDirect(t, s.tenantId, { kind: 'staff', roleKey: 'manager', fullName: 'Menecer', emailVerified: true });
  expect((await owner.put(`/api/v1/users/${m.id}/sites`, { siteIds: [siteId] })).status).toBe(200);
  const manager = { id: m.id, api: as(t, (await loginStaff(t, m.email!, m.secret)).accessToken) };
  const team = await owner.post('/api/v1/teams', { name: 'Texniki xidmət' });
  expect(team.status, JSON.stringify(team.body)).toBe(201);
  expect((await owner.put(`/api/v1/teams/${team.body.id}/members`, { userIds: [workers[0]!.id, workers[1]!.id] })).status).toBe(200);
  return { s, owner, siteId, otherSiteId, manager, workers: workers as [TestWorker, TestWorker, TestWorker], teamId: team.body.id as string };
}

export const newTaskBody = (assignee: TaskAssignee, over: Record<string, unknown> = {}) => ({
  id: uuidv7(),
  title: 'Soyuducunun qapısını təmir edin',
  instructions: 'Qapının rezinini dəyişin.',
  assignee,
  ...over,
});

export async function createTask(api: Api, body: object): Promise<TaskDetail> {
  const res = await api.post('/api/v1/tasks', body);
  expect(res.status, JSON.stringify(res.body)).toBe(201);
  return res.body as TaskDetail;
}

/** [from, to, actor user id, comment] in order. */
export async function taskHistory(taskId: string): Promise<Array<[string | null, string, string | null, string | null]>> {
  const r = await ownerQuery<{ from_status: string | null; to_status: string; actor_user_id: string | null; comment: string | null }>(
    'select from_status, to_status, actor_user_id, comment from task_status_history where task_id = $1 order by at, id',
    [taskId],
  );
  return r.rows.map((h) => [h.from_status, h.to_status, h.actor_user_id, h.comment]);
}

export async function auditActions(taskId: string): Promise<string[]> {
  const r = await ownerQuery<{ action: string }>("select action from audit_log where entity_type = 'task' and entity_id = $1 order by occurred_at, id", [taskId]);
  return r.rows.map((x) => x.action);
}

const TASK_EVENTS: TaskEventName[] = ['task.assigned', 'task.accepted', 'task.submitted', 'task.rejected', 'task.completed', 'task.cancelled', 'task.overdue'];

/** Records [event, taskId, actorUserId] for every task.* event until `stop()`. */
export function captureTaskEvents(t: TestApp) {
  const events = t.app.get(DomainEvents);
  const seen: Array<[TaskEventName, string, string | null]> = [];
  const offs = TASK_EVENTS.map((name) => events.on(name, (e) => void seen.push([name, e.taskId, e.actorUserId])));
  return { seen, stop: () => offs.forEach((off) => off()) };
}

export interface TaskDbRow {
  status: string;
  assignee_user_id: string | null;
  assignee_team_id: string | null;
  accepted_at: Date | null;
  started_at: Date | null;
  submitted_at: Date | null;
  completed_at: Date | null;
  cancelled_at: Date | null;
  cancel_reason: string | null;
  due_at: Date | null;
  overdue_at: Date | null;
  rev: number;
  updated_at: Date;
}

export const taskRow = async (id: string): Promise<TaskDbRow> => (await ownerQuery<TaskDbRow>('select * from tasks where id = $1', [id])).rows[0]!;
```

```ts
import { uuidv7 } from 'uuidv7';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { createTestApp, type TestApp } from './app';
import { FakeClock } from './fake-clock';
import { type Api, MONDAY_0800 } from './scheduling-fixtures';
import { auditActions, captureTaskEvents, createTask, newTaskBody, taskHistory, taskWorld } from './task-fixtures';

describe('creating and reading tasks', () => {
  const clock = new FakeClock(MONDAY_0800);
  let t: TestApp;
  beforeAll(async () => {
    t = await createTestApp({}, { clock });
  });
  beforeEach(() => clock.set(MONDAY_0800));
  afterAll(() => t.close());

  it('creates a task for a person in scope with history, audit and a task.assigned event', async () => {
    const w = await taskWorld(t);
    const events = captureTaskEvents(t);
    const body = newTaskBody({ userId: w.workers[0].id }, { siteId: w.siteId, dueAt: '2026-11-03T14:00:00.000Z', priority: 'high' });
    const task = await createTask(w.manager.api, body);
    events.stop();
    expect(task).toMatchObject({
      id: body.id,
      title: 'Soyuducunun qapısını təmir edin',
      instructions: 'Qapının rezinini dəyişin.',
      priority: 'high',
      status: 'new',
      site: { id: w.siteId, name: 'Filial 1' },
      assignee: { id: w.workers[0].id, fullName: 'İşçi 1' },
      team: null,
      assigneeInactive: false,
      createdBy: { id: w.manager.id, fullName: 'Menecer' },
      requiresReview: false,
      source: 'adhoc',
      problemId: null,
      problem: null,
      evidence: { photo: 'optional', video: 'optional', liveOnly: false },
      dueAt: '2026-11-03T14:00:00.000Z',
      overdueAt: null,
      acceptedAt: null,
      rev: 1,
      createdAt: '2026-11-02T04:00:00.000Z',
      media: [],
      submissions: [],
      canManage: true,
      canReview: true,
    });
    expect(task.history).toEqual([
      { id: expect.any(String), fromStatus: null, toStatus: 'new', at: '2026-11-02T04:00:00.000Z', actor: { id: w.manager.id, fullName: 'Menecer' }, comment: null },
    ]);
    expect(await auditActions(task.id)).toEqual(['task.created']);
    expect(events.seen).toEqual([['task.assigned', task.id, w.manager.id]]);
  });

  it('returns the stored task when the same id is sent again', async () => {
    const w = await taskWorld(t);
    const body = newTaskBody({ teamId: w.teamId });
    const first = await createTask(w.manager.api, body);
    expect(first).toMatchObject({ assignee: null, team: { id: w.teamId, name: 'Texniki xidmət' } });
    clock.advanceMinutes(5);
    const again = await createTask(w.manager.api, { ...body, title: 'Başqa ad' });
    expect(again).toMatchObject({ id: first.id, title: first.title, createdAt: first.createdAt, rev: 1 });
    expect(await taskHistory(first.id)).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(await auditActions(first.id)).toEqual(['task.created']);
  });

  it('assigns only active people within the creator’s scope, and active teams with an active member', async () => {
    const w = await taskWorld(t);
    const refused = async (api: Api, assignee: { userId: string } | { teamId: string }) => {
      const res = await api.post('/api/v1/tasks', newTaskBody(assignee));
      expect([res.status, res.body.error?.code, res.body.error?.fields], JSON.stringify(res.body)).toEqual([422, 'TASK_ASSIGNEE_INVALID', { assignee: 'errors.TASK_ASSIGNEE_INVALID' }]);
    };
    await refused(w.manager.api, { userId: w.workers[2].id });
    await createTask(w.owner, newTaskBody({ userId: w.workers[2].id }));
    await createTask(w.manager.api, newTaskBody({ userId: w.workers[0].id }));
    expect((await w.owner.post(`/api/v1/users/${w.workers[1].id}/deactivate`)).status).toBe(200);
    await refused(w.owner, { userId: w.workers[1].id });
    await refused(w.owner, { userId: uuidv7() });
    const empty = (await w.owner.post('/api/v1/teams', { name: 'Boş komanda' })).body.id as string;
    await refused(w.owner, { teamId: empty });
    await createTask(w.manager.api, newTaskBody({ teamId: w.teamId }));
    expect((await w.owner.patch(`/api/v1/teams/${w.teamId}`, { active: false })).status).toBe(200);
    await refused(w.owner, { teamId: w.teamId });
  });

  it('validates the body and the site', async () => {
    const w = await taskWorld(t);
    const assignee = { userId: w.workers[0].id };
    const both = await w.owner.post('/api/v1/tasks', newTaskBody({ userId: w.workers[0].id, teamId: w.teamId } as unknown as { userId: string }));
    expect([both.status, both.body.error.code]).toEqual([400, 'VALIDATION_FAILED']);
    const long = await w.owner.post('/api/v1/tasks', newTaskBody(assignee, { title: 'x'.repeat(201) }));
    expect([long.status, long.body.error.fields]).toEqual([400, { title: 'errors.validation.tooLong' }]);
    const outside = await w.manager.api.post('/api/v1/tasks', newTaskBody(assignee, { siteId: w.otherSiteId }));
    expect([outside.status, outside.body.error.code]).toEqual([403, 'SITE_OUT_OF_SCOPE']);
    expect((await w.owner.patch(`/api/v1/sites/${w.otherSiteId}`, { active: false })).status).toBe(200);
    const inactive = await w.owner.post('/api/v1/tasks', newTaskBody(assignee, { siteId: w.otherSiteId }));
    expect([inactive.status, inactive.body.error.code]).toEqual([409, 'SITE_INACTIVE']);
    const missing = await w.owner.post('/api/v1/tasks', newTaskBody(assignee, { siteId: uuidv7() }));
    expect([missing.status, missing.body.error.code]).toEqual([422, 'REFERENCE_NOT_FOUND']);
  });

  it('lets only tasks.manage holders create tasks and list assignees', async () => {
    const w = await taskWorld(t);
    expect((await w.workers[0].api.post('/api/v1/tasks', newTaskBody({ userId: w.workers[0].id }))).status).toBe(403);
    expect((await w.workers[0].api.get('/api/v1/tasks/assignees')).status).toBe(403);
  });

  it('lists people in the caller’s scope and active teams with members, filtered by name', async () => {
    const w = await taskWorld(t);
    const res = await w.manager.api.get('/api/v1/tasks/assignees');
    expect(res.status, JSON.stringify(res.body)).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body.users.map((u: { fullName: string }) => u.fullName).sort()).toEqual(['Menecer', 'İşçi 1', 'İşçi 2']);
    expect(res.body.users.find((u: { id: string }) => u.id === w.workers[0].id)).toEqual({ id: w.workers[0].id, fullName: 'İşçi 1', jobTitle: null, kind: 'worker' });
    expect(res.body.teams).toEqual([{ id: w.teamId, name: 'Texniki xidmət', memberCount: 2 }]);
    const two = await w.manager.api.get('/api/v1/tasks/assignees?q=2');
    expect([two.body.users.map((u: { fullName: string }) => u.fullName), two.body.teams]).toEqual([['İşçi 2'], []]);
  });

  it('offers the active sites a task may name, without sites.view and whatever the search', async () => {
    const w = await taskWorld(t);
    const names = (sites: Array<{ name: string }>) => sites.map((s) => s.name);
    // The site_subtree manager (no sites.view needed): Filial 1 only; the search narrows people, never sites.
    const mine = await w.manager.api.get('/api/v1/tasks/assignees?q=zzz');
    expect([mine.body.users, mine.body.teams, mine.body.sites]).toEqual([[], [], [{ id: w.siteId, name: 'Filial 1' }]]);
    // The owner (all): every active site, by name.
    expect(names((await w.owner.get('/api/v1/tasks/assignees')).body.sites)).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining(['Filial 1', 'Filial 2']));
    expect((await w.owner.patch(`/api/v1/sites/${w.otherSiteId}`, { active: false })).status).toBe(200);
    expect(names((await w.owner.get('/api/v1/tasks/assignees')).body.sites)).not.toContain('Filial 2');
    // Every offered site is one the create accepts.
    for (const site of mine.body.sites as Array<{ id: string }>) {
      const res = await w.manager.api.post('/api/v1/tasks', newTaskBody({ userId: w.workers[0].id }, { siteId: site.id }));
      expect(res.status, JSON.stringify(res.body)).toBe(201);
    }
  });

  it('lets the creator, the assignee and members of an unaccepted team task read it, and nobody else', async () => {
    const w = await taskWorld(t);
    const direct = await createTask(w.manager.api, newTaskBody({ userId: w.workers[0].id }));
    const team = await createTask(w.manager.api, newTaskBody({ teamId: w.teamId }));
    const status = async (api: Api, id: string) => (await api.get(`/api/v1/tasks/${id}`)).status;
    expect(await status(w.workers[0].api, direct.id)).toBe(200);
    expect(await status(w.workers[1].api, direct.id)).toBe(404);
    expect(await status(w.workers[1].api, team.id)).toBe(200);
    expect(await status(w.workers[2].api, team.id)).toBe(404);
    expect(await status(w.owner, uuidv7())).toBe(404);
    const asWorker = (await w.workers[0].api.get(`/api/v1/tasks/${direct.id}`)).body;
    expect([asWorker.canManage, asWorker.canReview]).toEqual([false, false]);
  });
});
```

`apps/api/src/tasks/task-scope.ts`:

```ts
import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { and, eq, isNull, or, type SQL, sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import type { Principal } from '../common/request';
import { ScopeService } from '../common/scope.service';
import { DbService } from '../db/db.service';
import { tasks } from '../db/schema';
import { chain, inSubtree } from '../scheduling/scheduling-scope';

export type TaskRow = typeof tasks.$inferSelect;

export interface TaskAccess {
  canRead: boolean;
  canManage: boolean;
  canReview: boolean;
}

/** Spec §6.1 as SQL over `tasks`. Anything not readable is a 404. */
@Injectable()
export class TaskScope {
  constructor(
    private readonly db: DbService,
    private readonly users: ScopeService,
  ) {}

  /** Rule 3: the data scope covers the task. Callers check tasks.view / tasks.manage first. Unknown scopes fail closed. */
  covers(p: Principal): SQL {
    switch (p.dataScope) {
      case 'all':
        return sql`true`;
      case 'site_subtree':
        return sql`(${inSubtree(p.userId, tasks.siteId)} or exists (select 1 from users where users.id = ${tasks.assigneeUserId} and ${this.users.usersFilter(p)!}))`;
      case 'subordinates':
        return sql`${tasks.assigneeUserId} in ${chain(p.userId)}`;
      case 'own':
        return sql`false`;
      default: {
        const unhandled: never = p.dataScope;
        throw new Error(`Unhandled data scope: ${String(unhandled)}`);
      }
    }
  }

  /** The user is in the task's team (spec Decision 13: a user_teams row). */
  memberOfTeam(userId: string): SQL {
    return sql`exists (select 1 from user_teams ut where ut.team_id = ${tasks.assigneeTeamId} and ut.user_id = ${userId})`;
  }

  /** Rules 1–3: creator, assignee, member of an unaccepted team task, or tasks.view / tasks.manage within scope. */
  readable(p: Principal): SQL {
    const view = p.permissions.has('tasks.view') || p.permissions.has('tasks.manage');
    return or(eq(tasks.createdByUserId, p.userId), eq(tasks.assigneeUserId, p.userId), and(isNull(tasks.assigneeUserId), this.memberOfTeam(p.userId)), view ? this.covers(p) : undefined)!;
  }

  /** tasks.manage, and the creator or within scope. */
  manageable(p: Principal): SQL {
    return p.permissions.has('tasks.manage') ? or(eq(tasks.createdByUserId, p.userId), this.covers(p))! : sql`false`;
  }

  /** The creator, or anyone who can manage the task. */
  reviewable(p: Principal): SQL {
    return or(eq(tasks.createdByUserId, p.userId), this.manageable(p))!;
  }

  async access(p: Principal, id: string): Promise<TaskAccess> {
    const [r] = await this.db
      .tx()
      .select({
        canRead: sql<boolean>`(${this.readable(p)})`,
        canManage: sql<boolean>`(${this.manageable(p)})`,
        canReview: sql<boolean>`(${this.reviewable(p)})`,
      })
      .from(tasks)
      .where(eq(tasks.id, id));
    return r ?? { canRead: false, canManage: false, canReview: false };
  }
}
```

`apps/api/src/tasks/task-assignees.ts`:

```ts
import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import type { TaskAssignee, TaskAssigneeOptions } from '@taskop/contracts';
import { and, asc, eq, ilike, sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { AppError } from '../common/app-error';
import type { Principal } from '../common/request';
import { ScopeService } from '../common/scope.service';
import { escapeLike } from '../common/sql';
import { DbService } from '../db/db.service';
import { sites, teams, users } from '../db/schema';
import { SchedulingScope } from '../scheduling/scheduling-scope';

const activeMembers = sql<number>`(select count(*)::int from user_teams ut join users u on u.id = ut.user_id where ut.team_id = ${teams.id} and u.status = 'active')`;
const invalid = () => new AppError('TASK_ASSIGNEE_INVALID', { fields: { assignee: 'errors.TASK_ASSIGNEE_INVALID' } });

/** Spec §6.1 "Assignable". Site membership is not required. */
@Injectable()
export class TaskAssignees {
  constructor(
    private readonly db: DbService,
    private readonly scope: ScopeService,
    private readonly siteScope: SchedulingScope,
  ) {}

  /** A user: active and within the caller's users scope. A team: active, with at least one active member. */
  async resolve(p: Principal, a: TaskAssignee): Promise<{ userId: string | null; teamId: string | null }> {
    const tx = this.db.tx();
    if ('userId' in a) {
      const [u] = await tx.select({ id: users.id }).from(users).where(and(eq(users.id, a.userId), eq(users.status, 'active'), this.scope.usersFilter(p)));
      if (!u) throw invalid();
      return { userId: u.id, teamId: null };
    }
    const [team] = await tx.select({ id: teams.id }).from(teams).where(and(eq(teams.id, a.teamId), eq(teams.active, true), sql`${activeMembers} > 0`));
    if (!team) throw invalid();
    return { userId: null, teamId: team.id };
  }

  /** GET /tasks/assignees: up to 50 people and 50 teams, by name and filtered by `q`; up to 200 sites (Spec decision 25). */
  async options(p: Principal, q: string | undefined): Promise<TaskAssigneeOptions> {
    const tx = this.db.tx();
    const like = q ? `%${escapeLike(q)}%` : null;
    const people = await tx
      .select({ id: users.id, fullName: users.fullName, jobTitle: users.jobTitle, kind: users.kind })
      .from(users)
      .where(and(eq(users.status, 'active'), this.scope.usersFilter(p), like ? ilike(users.fullName, like) : undefined))
      .orderBy(asc(users.fullName), asc(users.id))
      .limit(50);
    const groups = await tx
      .select({ id: teams.id, name: teams.name, memberCount: activeMembers })
      .from(teams)
      .where(and(eq(teams.active, true), sql`${activeMembers} > 0`, like ? ilike(teams.name, like) : undefined))
      .orderBy(asc(teams.name), asc(teams.id))
      .limit(50);
    // The sites a create or edit accepts: active and writable in the caller's scope. Not narrowed by `q`.
    const places = await tx
      .select({ id: sites.id, name: sites.name })
      .from(sites)
      .where(and(eq(sites.active, true), this.siteScope.writableSites(p)))
      .orderBy(asc(sites.name), asc(sites.id))
      .limit(200);
    return { users: people, teams: groups, sites: places };
  }
}
```

`apps/api/src/tasks/task-rows.ts`:

```ts
import type { TaskHistoryEntry, TaskMediaDto, TaskSubmissionDto, TaskSummary } from '@taskop/contracts';
import { eq, getTableColumns } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { alias } from 'drizzle-orm/pg-core';
import type { Executor } from '../db/db.service';
import { sites, taskMedia, taskStatusHistory, taskSubmissions, tasks, teams, users } from '../db/schema';
import type { TaskRow } from './task-scope';

const creator = alias(users, 'creator');
const assignee = alias(users, 'assignee');

export const iso = (d: Date | null): string | null => d?.toISOString() ?? null;

/** Tasks with their site, team, creator and assignee names. */
export function selectTasks(tx: Executor) {
  return tx
    .select({
      t: getTableColumns(tasks),
      siteName: sites.name,
      teamName: teams.name,
      creatorName: creator.fullName,
      assigneeName: assignee.fullName,
      assigneeStatus: assignee.status,
    })
    .from(tasks)
    .innerJoin(creator, eq(creator.id, tasks.createdByUserId))
    .leftJoin(assignee, eq(assignee.id, tasks.assigneeUserId))
    .leftJoin(teams, eq(teams.id, tasks.assigneeTeamId))
    .leftJoin(sites, eq(sites.id, tasks.siteId))
    .$dynamic();
}
export type TaskSummaryRow = Awaited<ReturnType<typeof selectTasks>>[number];

export const toTaskSummary = (r: TaskSummaryRow): TaskSummary => ({
  id: r.t.id,
  title: r.t.title,
  priority: r.t.priority,
  status: r.t.status,
  site: r.t.siteId ? { id: r.t.siteId, name: r.siteName ?? '' } : null,
  assignee: r.t.assigneeUserId ? { id: r.t.assigneeUserId, fullName: r.assigneeName ?? '' } : null,
  team: r.t.assigneeTeamId ? { id: r.t.assigneeTeamId, name: r.teamName ?? '' } : null,
  assigneeInactive: r.t.assigneeUserId !== null && r.assigneeStatus !== 'active',
  createdBy: { id: r.t.createdByUserId, fullName: r.creatorName },
  requiresReview: r.t.requiresReview,
  source: r.t.problemId ? 'problem' : 'adhoc',
  problemId: r.t.problemId,
  dueAt: iso(r.t.dueAt),
  overdueAt: iso(r.t.overdueAt),
  rev: r.t.rev,
  createdAt: r.t.createdAt.toISOString(),
  updatedAt: r.t.updatedAt.toISOString(),
});

export const toTaskMediaDto = (r: { m: typeof taskMedia.$inferSelect; capturedByName: string }): TaskMediaDto => ({
  id: r.m.id,
  role: r.m.role,
  submissionId: r.m.submissionId,
  kind: r.m.kind,
  source: r.m.source,
  mime: r.m.mime,
  bytes: r.m.bytes,
  width: r.m.width,
  height: r.m.height,
  durationMs: r.m.durationMs,
  capturedAt: r.m.capturedAt.toISOString(),
  capturedBy: { id: r.m.capturedByUserId, fullName: r.capturedByName },
  status: r.m.status,
  uploadedAt: iso(r.m.uploadedAt),
});

export const toSubmissionDto = (r: { s: typeof taskSubmissions.$inferSelect; submittedByName: string; reviewedByName: string | null }): TaskSubmissionDto => ({
  id: r.s.id,
  submittedBy: { id: r.s.submittedByUserId, fullName: r.submittedByName },
  text: r.s.text,
  mediaIds: r.s.mediaIds,
  submittedAt: r.s.submittedAt.toISOString(),
  receivedAt: r.s.receivedAt.toISOString(),
  reviewDecision: r.s.reviewDecision,
  reviewComment: r.s.reviewComment,
  reviewedBy: r.s.reviewedByUserId ? { id: r.s.reviewedByUserId, fullName: r.reviewedByName ?? '' } : null,
  reviewedAt: iso(r.s.reviewedAt),
});

export const toHistoryEntry = (r: { h: typeof taskStatusHistory.$inferSelect; actorName: string | null }): TaskHistoryEntry => ({
  id: r.h.id,
  fromStatus: r.h.fromStatus,
  toStatus: r.h.toStatus,
  at: r.h.at.toISOString(),
  actor: r.h.actorUserId ? { id: r.h.actorUserId, fullName: r.actorName ?? '' } : null,
  comment: r.h.comment,
});

/** The audited fields of a task (spec §6.6). */
export const auditView = (t: TaskRow) => ({
  title: t.title,
  instructions: t.instructions,
  priority: t.priority,
  siteId: t.siteId,
  assigneeUserId: t.assigneeUserId,
  assigneeTeamId: t.assigneeTeamId,
  dueAt: iso(t.dueAt),
  requiresReview: t.requiresReview,
  evidence: t.evidence,
  problemId: t.problemId,
  status: t.status,
});
```

`apps/api/src/tasks/task-problems.ts`:

```ts
import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import type { ProblemDto } from '@taskop/contracts';
import { inArray } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { DbService } from '../db/db.service';
import { executionProblems } from '../db/schema';
import { selectProblemRows, toProblemDtos } from '../executions/problem-rows';

/** The source problems of tasks (spec §6.2). Reads through executions/problem-rows, never through ExecutionsModule. */
@Injectable()
export class TaskProblems {
  constructor(private readonly db: DbService) {}

  /** Problem cards for task details: shown to whoever can read the task. */
  async cards(problemIds: string[]): Promise<Map<string, ProblemDto>> {
    if (!problemIds.length) return new Map();
    const tx = this.db.tx();
    const dtos = await toProblemDtos(tx, await selectProblemRows(tx).where(inArray(executionProblems.id, problemIds)));
    return new Map(dtos.map((d) => [d.id, d]));
  }
}
```

`apps/api/src/tasks/task-writer.ts`:

```ts
import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import type { TaskStatus } from '@taskop/contracts';
import { DomainEvents, type TaskEventName } from '../common/domain-events';
import { DbService } from '../db/db.service';
import { taskStatusHistory } from '../db/schema';
import type { TaskRow } from './task-scope';

export interface HistoryStep {
  from: TaskStatus | null;
  to: TaskStatus;
  at: Date;
  /** Null for the system. */
  actorUserId: string | null;
  comment?: string | null;
}

/** History rows and task.* events, in the caller's transaction. */
@Injectable()
export class TaskWriter {
  constructor(
    private readonly db: DbService,
    private readonly events: DomainEvents,
  ) {}

  async history(t: Pick<TaskRow, 'id' | 'tenantId'>, steps: HistoryStep[]): Promise<void> {
    if (!steps.length) return;
    await this.db
      .tx()
      .insert(taskStatusHistory)
      .values(steps.map((s) => ({ tenantId: t.tenantId, taskId: t.id, fromStatus: s.from, toStatus: s.to, at: s.at, actorUserId: s.actorUserId, comment: s.comment ?? null })));
  }

  /** `t` is the task after the change, so the payload names the current assignee. */
  async emit(name: TaskEventName, t: Pick<TaskRow, 'id' | 'tenantId' | 'assigneeUserId' | 'assigneeTeamId' | 'createdByUserId'>, actorUserId: string | null): Promise<void> {
    await this.events.emit(name, {
      tenantId: t.tenantId,
      taskId: t.id,
      actorUserId,
      assigneeUserId: t.assigneeUserId,
      assigneeTeamId: t.assigneeTeamId,
      createdByUserId: t.createdByUserId,
    });
  }
}
```

`apps/api/src/tasks/task-queries.ts`:

```ts
import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import type { SyncTask, TaskDetail, TaskEvidence, TaskHistoryEntry } from '@taskop/contracts';
import { asc, eq, getTableColumns, inArray } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { alias } from 'drizzle-orm/pg-core';
import { AppError } from '../common/app-error';
import type { Principal } from '../common/request';
import { DbService } from '../db/db.service';
import { taskMedia, taskStatusHistory, taskSubmissions, tasks, users } from '../db/schema';
import { TaskProblems } from './task-problems';
import { iso, selectTasks, toHistoryEntry, toSubmissionDto, toTaskMediaDto, toTaskSummary } from './task-rows';
import { TaskScope } from './task-scope';

const submitter = alias(users, 'submitter');
const reviewer = alias(users, 'reviewer');

/** GET /tasks/:id and the phone's copies (spec §6.2, §6.4). */
@Injectable()
export class TaskQueries {
  constructor(
    private readonly db: DbService,
    private readonly scope: TaskScope,
    private readonly problems: TaskProblems,
  ) {}

  async detail(p: Principal, id: string): Promise<TaskDetail> {
    const access = await this.scope.access(p, id);
    if (!access.canRead) throw new AppError('NOT_FOUND');
    const [task] = await this.syncTasks([id]);
    return { ...task!, history: await this.history(id), canManage: access.canManage, canReview: access.canReview };
  }

  /** Everything but history and rights, batched; the result keeps the order of `ids`. Callers check access. */
  async syncTasks(ids: string[]): Promise<SyncTask[]> {
    if (!ids.length) return [];
    const tx = this.db.tx();
    const rows = await selectTasks(tx).where(inArray(tasks.id, ids));
    const media = await tx
      .select({ m: getTableColumns(taskMedia), capturedByName: users.fullName })
      .from(taskMedia)
      .innerJoin(users, eq(users.id, taskMedia.capturedByUserId))
      .where(inArray(taskMedia.taskId, ids))
      .orderBy(asc(taskMedia.capturedAt), asc(taskMedia.id));
    const submissions = await tx
      .select({ s: getTableColumns(taskSubmissions), submittedByName: submitter.fullName, reviewedByName: reviewer.fullName })
      .from(taskSubmissions)
      .innerJoin(submitter, eq(submitter.id, taskSubmissions.submittedByUserId))
      .leftJoin(reviewer, eq(reviewer.id, taskSubmissions.reviewedByUserId))
      .where(inArray(taskSubmissions.taskId, ids))
      .orderBy(asc(taskSubmissions.receivedAt), asc(taskSubmissions.id));
    const problems = await this.problems.cards(rows.flatMap((r) => (r.t.problemId ? [r.t.problemId] : [])));
    const byId = new Map<string, SyncTask>(
      rows.map((r) => [
        r.t.id,
        {
          ...toTaskSummary(r),
          instructions: r.t.instructions,
          evidence: r.t.evidence as TaskEvidence,
          acceptedAt: iso(r.t.acceptedAt),
          startedAt: iso(r.t.startedAt),
          submittedAt: iso(r.t.submittedAt),
          completedAt: iso(r.t.completedAt),
          cancelledAt: iso(r.t.cancelledAt),
          cancelReason: r.t.cancelReason,
          media: media.filter((m) => m.m.taskId === r.t.id).map(toTaskMediaDto),
          submissions: submissions.filter((s) => s.s.taskId === r.t.id).map(toSubmissionDto),
          problem: r.t.problemId ? (problems.get(r.t.problemId) ?? null) : null,
        },
      ]),
    );
    return ids.flatMap((id) => {
      const task = byId.get(id);
      return task ? [task] : [];
    });
  }

  private async history(taskId: string): Promise<TaskHistoryEntry[]> {
    const rows = await this.db
      .tx()
      .select({ h: getTableColumns(taskStatusHistory), actorName: users.fullName })
      .from(taskStatusHistory)
      .leftJoin(users, eq(users.id, taskStatusHistory.actorUserId))
      .where(eq(taskStatusHistory.taskId, taskId))
      .orderBy(asc(taskStatusHistory.at), asc(taskStatusHistory.id));
    return rows.map(toHistoryEntry);
  }
}
```

```ts
import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import type { TaskDetail } from '@taskop/contracts';
import { eq } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { AppError } from '../common/app-error';
import { Clock } from '../common/clock';
import type { Principal } from '../common/request';
import { AuditService } from '../db/audit.service';
import { DbService } from '../db/db.service';
import { sites, tasks } from '../db/schema';
import { SchedulingScope } from '../scheduling/scheduling-scope';
import type { CreateTaskDto } from './dto';
import { TaskAssignees } from './task-assignees';
import { TaskQueries } from './task-queries';
import { auditView } from './task-rows';
import { TaskWriter } from './task-writer';

/** Manager commands (spec §6.2). Each runs in the request's tenant transaction. */
@Injectable()
export class TasksService {
  constructor(
    private readonly db: DbService,
    private readonly clock: Clock,
    private readonly audit: AuditService,
    private readonly siteScope: SchedulingScope,
    private readonly assignees: TaskAssignees,
    private readonly writer: TaskWriter,
    private readonly queries: TaskQueries,
  ) {}

  /** POST /tasks. Idempotent on the client id: a repeat returns the stored task unchanged (the body is not compared). */
  async create(p: Principal, dto: CreateTaskDto): Promise<TaskDetail> {
    const tx = this.db.tx();
    const [known] = await tx.select({ id: tasks.id }).from(tasks).where(eq(tasks.id, dto.id));
    if (known) return this.queries.detail(p, dto.id);
    const now = this.clock.now();
    if (dto.siteId) await this.assertSite(p, dto.siteId);
    const assignee = await this.assignees.resolve(p, dto.assignee);
    const [row] = await tx
      .insert(tasks)
      .values({
        id: dto.id,
        tenantId: p.tenantId,
        title: dto.title,
        instructions: dto.instructions,
        priority: dto.priority,
        siteId: dto.siteId,
        createdByUserId: p.userId,
        assigneeUserId: assignee.userId,
        assigneeTeamId: assignee.teamId,
        requiresReview: dto.requiresReview ?? false,
        evidence: dto.evidence,
        dueAt: dto.dueAt ? new Date(dto.dueAt) : null,
        createdAt: now,
        updatedAt: now,
      })
      .returning();
    await this.writer.history(row!, [{ from: null, to: 'new', at: now, actorUserId: p.userId }]);
    await this.audit.record({ action: 'task.created', entityType: 'task', entityId: row!.id, after: auditView(row!) });
    await this.writer.emit('task.assigned', row!, p.userId);
    return this.queries.detail(p, row!.id);
  }

  /** Spec Decision 19: the site exists, is active and lies within the caller's scope. */
  private async assertSite(p: Principal, siteId: string): Promise<void> {
    const [site] = await this.db.tx().select({ active: sites.active }).from(sites).where(eq(sites.id, siteId));
    if (!site) throw new AppError('REFERENCE_NOT_FOUND', { fields: { siteId: 'errors.REFERENCE_NOT_FOUND' } });
    if (!site.active) throw new AppError('SITE_INACTIVE');
    await this.siteScope.assertSiteWritable(p, siteId);
  }
}
```

```ts
import { Body, Controller, Get, Inject, Param, Post, Query } from '@nestjs/common';
import { ApiBearerAuth, ApiCreatedResponse, ApiOkResponse, ApiTags } from '@nestjs/swagger';
import type { TaskAssigneeOptions, TaskDetail } from '@taskop/contracts';
import { CurrentPrincipal, RequirePermission } from '../common/decorators';
import { ParseIdPipe } from '../common/parse-id.pipe';
import type { Principal } from '../common/request';
import { CreateTaskDto, TaskAssigneeOptionsResponse, TaskAssigneeQueryDto, TaskDetailResponse } from './dto';
import { TaskAssignees } from './task-assignees';
import { TaskQueries } from './task-queries';
import { TasksService } from './tasks.service';

/** Spec §6.2. Routes without a permission decorator decide access in the service (spec §6.1). */
@ApiTags('tasks')
@ApiBearerAuth()
@Controller('tasks')
export class TasksController {
  constructor(
    @Inject(TasksService) private readonly tasks: TasksService,
    @Inject(TaskQueries) private readonly queries: TaskQueries,
    @Inject(TaskAssignees) private readonly assignees: TaskAssignees,
  ) {}

  /** Declared before `:id`, so "assignees" is never parsed as an id. */
  @Get('assignees')
  @RequirePermission('tasks.manage')
  @ApiOkResponse({ type: TaskAssigneeOptionsResponse })
  options(@CurrentPrincipal() p: Principal, @Query() q: TaskAssigneeQueryDto): Promise<TaskAssigneeOptions> {
    return this.assignees.options(p, q.q);
  }

  @Get(':id')
  @ApiOkResponse({ type: TaskDetailResponse })
  get(@CurrentPrincipal() p: Principal, @Param('id', ParseIdPipe) id: string): Promise<TaskDetail> {
    return this.queries.detail(p, id);
  }

  @Post()
  @RequirePermission('tasks.manage')
  @ApiCreatedResponse({ type: TaskDetailResponse })
  create(@CurrentPrincipal() p: Principal, @Body() body: CreateTaskDto): Promise<TaskDetail> {
    return this.tasks.create(p, body);
  }
}
```

```ts
import { afterAll, beforeAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { createTestApp, type TestApp } from './app';
import { answersBody, claimOk, executionWorld } from './execution-fixtures';
import { FakeClock } from './fake-clock';
import { as, createUserDirect, loginStaff } from './fixtures';
import { ownerQuery } from './owner-db';
import { MONDAY_0800 } from './scheduling-fixtures';
import { closedRunWithProblems, createTask, newTaskBody } from './task-fixtures';

describe('tasks from problems', () => {
  const clock = new FakeClock(MONDAY_0800);
  let t: TestApp;
  beforeAll(async () => {
    t = await createTestApp({}, { clock });
  });
  beforeEach(() => clock.set(MONDAY_0800));
  afterAll(() => t.close());

  it('takes the problem’s site, turns review on by default and shows the problem card to the assignee', async () => {
    const { w, manualProblemId, ruleProblemId } = await closedRunWithProblems(t, clock);
    const body = newTaskBody({ userId: w.workers[1].id }, { problemId: manualProblemId, siteId: w.otherSiteId, title: 'Temperatur', priority: 'high' });
    const task = await createTask(w.owner, body);
    const card = {
      id: manualProblemId,
      itemLabel: 'Temperatur',
      source: 'manual',
      severity: 'critical',
      note: 'Kondisioner xarabdır',
      siteName: 'Filial 1',
      checklistName: 'İcra yoxlaması',
      executorName: 'İşçi 1',
      resolution: 'open',
      taskId: task.id,
      executionState: 'completed',
    };
    expect(task).toMatchObject({ source: 'problem', problemId: manualProblemId, site: { id: w.siteId, name: 'Filial 1' }, requiresReview: true, problem: card });
    const asAssignee = await w.workers[1].api.get(`/api/v1/tasks/${task.id}`);
    expect(asAssignee.status, JSON.stringify(asAssignee.body)).toBe(200);
    expect(asAssignee.body.problem).toMatchObject(card);
    const open = (await w.owner.get('/api/v1/problems?from=2026-11-01&to=2026-11-30&resolution=open')).body.items;
    expect(open.map((p: { id: string; taskId: string }) => [p.id, p.taskId])).toEqual([[manualProblemId, task.id]]);
    const explicit = await createTask(w.owner, newTaskBody({ userId: w.workers[1].id }, { problemId: ruleProblemId, requiresReview: false }));
    expect(explicit.requiresReview).toBe(false);
  });

  it('refuses a problem of a run still open, a second task for a problem, and problems the caller cannot read', async () => {
    const w = await executionWorld(t);
    clock.set('2026-11-02T04:20:00Z');
    const executionId = await claimOk(w.workers[0].api, w.occurrenceId, '2026-11-02T04:10:00.000Z');
    await w.workers[0].api.put(
      `/api/v1/executions/${executionId}/answers`,
      answersBody(1, { [w.c.temp.id]: { number: 5, problem: { severity: 'normal', note: 'Qapı sınıqdır', mediaIds: [] } } }, '2026-11-02T04:20:00.000Z'),
    );
    const openProblem = (await ownerQuery<{ id: string }>('select id from execution_problems where execution_id = $1', [executionId])).rows[0]!.id;
    const open = await w.owner.post('/api/v1/tasks', newTaskBody({ userId: w.workers[1].id }, { problemId: openProblem }));
    expect([open.status, open.body.error.code]).toEqual([409, 'PROBLEM_NOT_CLOSED']);

    clock.set(MONDAY_0800); // a new world materialises today's occurrence only before its window opens
    const c = await closedRunWithProblems(t, clock);
    await createTask(c.w.owner, newTaskBody({ userId: c.w.workers[1].id }, { problemId: c.manualProblemId }));
    const again = await c.w.owner.post('/api/v1/tasks', newTaskBody({ userId: c.w.workers[1].id }, { problemId: c.manualProblemId }));
    expect([again.status, again.body.error.code]).toEqual([409, 'PROBLEM_ALREADY_HAS_TASK']);

    const u = await createUserDirect(t, c.w.s.tenantId, { kind: 'staff', roleKey: 'manager', emailVerified: true });
    await c.w.owner.put(`/api/v1/users/${u.id}/sites`, { siteIds: [c.w.otherSiteId] });
    const outside = as(t, (await loginStaff(t, u.email!, u.secret)).accessToken);
    expect((await outside.post('/api/v1/tasks', newTaskBody({ userId: u.id }, { problemId: c.ruleProblemId }))).status).toBe(404);
  });

  it('answers PROBLEM_ALREADY_HAS_TASK to the loser of two concurrent creates for one problem', async () => {
    const c = await closedRunWithProblems(t, clock);
    const body = () => newTaskBody({ userId: c.w.workers[1].id }, { problemId: c.ruleProblemId });
    const results = await Promise.all([c.w.owner.post('/api/v1/tasks', body()), c.w.owner.post('/api/v1/tasks', body())]);
    expect(results.map((r) => r.status).sort()).toEqual([201, 409]);
    expect(results.find((r) => r.status === 409)!.body.error.code).toBe('PROBLEM_ALREADY_HAS_TASK');
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Replace `apps/api/src/tasks/task-problems.ts` with:

```ts
import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import type { ProblemDto } from '@taskop/contracts';
import { and, eq, inArray } from 'drizzle-orm';
import type { Principal } from '../common/request';
import { DbService } from '../db/db.service';
import { executionProblems } from '../db/schema';
import { type ProblemRow, selectProblemRows, toProblemDtos } from '../executions/problem-rows';
import { SchedulingScope } from '../scheduling/scheduling-scope';

/** The source problems of tasks (spec §6.2). Reads through executions/problem-rows, never through ExecutionsModule. */
@Injectable()
export class TaskProblems {
  constructor(
    private readonly db: DbService,
    private readonly scope: SchedulingScope,
  ) {}

  /** Problem cards for task details: shown to whoever can read the task. */
  async cards(problemIds: string[]): Promise<Map<string, ProblemDto>> {
    if (!problemIds.length) return new Map();
    const tx = this.db.tx();
    const dtos = await toProblemDtos(tx, await selectProblemRows(tx).where(inArray(executionProblems.id, problemIds)));
    return new Map(dtos.map((d) => [d.id, d]));
  }

  /** A problem readable as GET /problems decides (spec Decision 6): assignments.view and the occurrence in scope. */
  async readable(p: Principal, problemId: string): Promise<ProblemRow | null> {
    if (!p.permissions.has('assignments.view')) return null;
    const [row] = await selectProblemRows(this.db.tx()).where(and(eq(executionProblems.id, problemId), this.scope.occurrences(p)));
    return row ?? null;
  }
}
```

In `apps/api/src/tasks/tasks.service.ts`:
- add `import { TaskProblems } from './task-problems';` and the constructor parameter `private readonly problems: TaskProblems,` after `assignees`
- replace `create` with:

```ts
  /** POST /tasks. Idempotent on the client id: a repeat returns the stored task unchanged (the body is not compared). */
  async create(p: Principal, dto: CreateTaskDto): Promise<TaskDetail> {
    const tx = this.db.tx();
    const [known] = await tx.select({ id: tasks.id }).from(tasks).where(eq(tasks.id, dto.id));
    if (known) return this.queries.detail(p, dto.id);
    const now = this.clock.now();
    let siteId = dto.siteId;
    if (dto.problemId) {
      const problem = await this.problems.readable(p, dto.problemId);
      if (!problem) throw new AppError('NOT_FOUND');
      // Only problems of closed runs: their rows no longer change (spec §1).
      if (problem.executionState !== 'completed' && problem.executionState !== 'partial') throw new AppError('PROBLEM_NOT_CLOSED');
      if (problem.taskId) throw new AppError('PROBLEM_ALREADY_HAS_TASK');
      siteId = problem.pr.siteId;
    } else if (siteId) {
      await this.assertSite(p, siteId);
    }
    const assignee = await this.assignees.resolve(p, dto.assignee);
    const [row] = await tx
      .insert(tasks)
      .values({
        id: dto.id,
        tenantId: p.tenantId,
        title: dto.title,
        instructions: dto.instructions,
        priority: dto.priority,
        siteId,
        createdByUserId: p.userId,
        assigneeUserId: assignee.userId,
        assigneeTeamId: assignee.teamId,
        // Problem fixes are reviewed unless the creator says otherwise (spec §1).
        requiresReview: dto.requiresReview ?? dto.problemId !== null,
        evidence: dto.evidence,
        dueAt: dto.dueAt ? new Date(dto.dueAt) : null,
        problemId: dto.problemId,
        createdAt: now,
        updatedAt: now,
      })
      .returning();
    await this.writer.history(row!, [{ from: null, to: 'new', at: now, actorUserId: p.userId }]);
    await this.audit.record({ action: 'task.created', entityType: 'task', entityId: row!.id, after: auditView(row!) });
    await this.writer.emit('task.assigned', row!, p.userId);
    return this.queries.detail(p, row!.id);
  }
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`apps/api/src/tasks/task-time.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { assertNotFuture, boundTaskTime } from './task-time';

const at = (iso: string) => new Date(iso);

describe('task device times (spec §6.3, SP4 rule)', () => {
  const rx = at('2026-11-02T04:20:00Z');
  const floor = at('2026-11-02T04:00:00Z');

  it('accepts device times up to 2 minutes ahead of the server and refuses later ones', () => {
    expect(() => assertNotFuture(at('2026-11-02T04:22:00Z'), rx)).not.toThrow();
    expect(() => assertNotFuture(at('2026-11-02T04:22:00.001Z'), rx)).toThrow('CLOCK_INVALID');
  });

  it('raises a time before the previous step to it', () => {
    expect(boundTaskTime(at('2026-11-02T04:10:00Z'), floor, rx)).toEqual(at('2026-11-02T04:10:00Z'));
    expect(boundTaskTime(at('2026-11-02T03:00:00Z'), floor, rx)).toEqual(floor);
    expect(() => boundTaskTime(at('2026-11-02T05:00:00Z'), floor, rx)).toThrow('CLOCK_INVALID');
  });
});
```

```ts
export const act = (api: Api, taskId: string, action: 'accept' | 'start', at: string) => api.post(`/api/v1/tasks/${taskId}/${action}`, { at });
```

`apps/api/test/tasks-work.test.ts`:

```ts
import { afterAll, beforeAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { createTestApp, type TestApp } from './app';
import { FakeClock } from './fake-clock';
import { ownerQuery } from './owner-db';
import { MONDAY_0800 } from './scheduling-fixtures';
import { act, auditActions, captureTaskEvents, createTask, newTaskBody, taskHistory, taskRow, taskWorld } from './task-fixtures';

const T = (hhmm: string) => `2026-11-02T${hhmm}:00.000Z`;

describe('accepting and starting tasks', () => {
  const clock = new FakeClock(MONDAY_0800);
  let t: TestApp;
  beforeAll(async () => {
    t = await createTestApp({}, { clock });
  });
  beforeEach(() => clock.set(MONDAY_0800));
  afterAll(() => t.close());

  it('accepts a direct task at the device time, with history and the task.accepted event; a repeat is a no-op', async () => {
    const w = await taskWorld(t);
    const task = await createTask(w.manager.api, newTaskBody({ userId: w.workers[0].id }));
    clock.set(T('04:20'));
    const events = captureTaskEvents(t);
    const res = await act(w.workers[0].api, task.id, 'accept', T('04:10'));
    expect(res.status, JSON.stringify(res.body)).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body).toMatchObject({ status: 'accepted', acceptedAt: T('04:10'), startedAt: null, rev: 2, assignee: { id: w.workers[0].id } });
    const again = await act(w.workers[0].api, task.id, 'accept', T('04:11'));
    expect([again.status, again.body.acceptedAt, again.body.rev]).toEqual([200, T('04:10'), 2]);
    events.stop();
    expect(events.seen).toEqual([['task.accepted', task.id, w.workers[0].id]]);
    expect(await taskHistory(task.id)).toEqual([
      [null, 'new', w.manager.id, null],
      ['new', 'accepted', w.workers[0].id, null],
    ]);
    expect(await auditActions(task.id)).toEqual(['task.created']);
  });

  it('start on a new task accepts and starts it in one step; repeating start or accept afterwards is a no-op', async () => {
    const w = await taskWorld(t);
    const task = await createTask(w.manager.api, newTaskBody({ userId: w.workers[0].id }));
    clock.set(T('04:20'));
    const res = await act(w.workers[0].api, task.id, 'start', T('04:12'));
    expect(res.status, JSON.stringify(res.body)).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body).toMatchObject({ status: 'in_progress', acceptedAt: T('04:12'), startedAt: T('04:12'), rev: 3 });
    expect(await taskHistory(task.id)).toEqual([
      [null, 'new', w.manager.id, null],
      ['new', 'accepted', w.workers[0].id, null],
      ['accepted', 'in_progress', w.workers[0].id, null],
    ]);
    expect((await act(w.workers[0].api, task.id, 'accept', T('04:13'))).body).toMatchObject({ status: 'in_progress', rev: 3 });
    expect((await act(w.workers[0].api, task.id, 'start', T('04:14'))).body).toMatchObject({ status: 'in_progress', startedAt: T('04:12'), rev: 3 });
  });

  // Clients send device time − their last measured clock offset (Interfaces → Server behaviour), so `at` is already on
  // the server's clock here: the server adds no offset and only bounds it.
  it('raises device times before the previous step and refuses times over 2 minutes ahead', async () => {
    const w = await taskWorld(t);
    const task = await createTask(w.manager.api, newTaskBody({ userId: w.workers[0].id }));
    clock.set(T('04:20'));
    expect((await act(w.workers[0].api, task.id, 'accept', T('03:00'))).body.acceptedAt).toBe(T('04:00'));
    const ahead = await act(w.workers[0].api, task.id, 'start', '2026-11-02T04:22:00.001Z');
    expect([ahead.status, ahead.body.error.code]).toEqual([422, 'CLOCK_INVALID']);
    expect((await act(w.workers[0].api, task.id, 'start', T('04:22'))).body).toMatchObject({ status: 'in_progress', startedAt: T('04:22') });
  });

  it('gives a team task to the first accept; a teammate’s later accept or start gets TASK_ALREADY_TAKEN', async () => {
    const w = await taskWorld(t);
    const [w1, w2, w3] = w.workers;
    const task = await createTask(w.manager.api, newTaskBody({ teamId: w.teamId }));
    clock.set(T('04:20'));
    const won = await act(w2.api, task.id, 'accept', T('04:15'));
    expect(won.status, JSON.stringify(won.body)).toBe(200);
    expect(won.body).toMatchObject({ assignee: { id: w2.id }, team: { id: w.teamId } });
    for (const action of ['accept', 'start'] as const) {
      const lost = await act(w1.api, task.id, action, T('04:16'));
      expect([lost.status, lost.body.error.code], action).toEqual([409, 'TASK_ALREADY_TAKEN']);
    }
    expect((await w1.api.get(`/api/v1/tasks/${task.id}`)).status).toBe(404);
    expect((await act(w3.api, task.id, 'accept', T('04:16'))).status).toBe(404);
    const owner = await act(w.owner, task.id, 'accept', T('04:16'));
    expect([owner.status, owner.body.error.code]).toEqual([403, 'FORBIDDEN']);
  });

  it('lets exactly one of two concurrent accepts win', async () => {
    const w = await taskWorld(t);
    const task = await createTask(w.manager.api, newTaskBody({ teamId: w.teamId }));
    clock.set(T('04:20'));
    const [a, b] = await Promise.all([act(w.workers[0].api, task.id, 'accept', T('04:19')), act(w.workers[1].api, task.id, 'accept', T('04:19'))]);
    expect([a.status, b.status].sort()).toEqual([200, 409]);
    const winner = a.status === 200 ? w.workers[0].id : w.workers[1].id;
    expect((await taskRow(task.id)).assignee_user_id).toBe(winner);
    expect((await taskHistory(task.id)).filter(([, to]) => to === 'accepted')).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('refuses accept and start once the task is cancelled', async () => {
    const w = await taskWorld(t);
    const direct = await createTask(w.manager.api, newTaskBody({ userId: w.workers[0].id }));
    const team = await createTask(w.manager.api, newTaskBody({ teamId: w.teamId }));
    clock.set(T('04:20'));
    await act(w.workers[0].api, direct.id, 'accept', T('04:10'));
    await ownerQuery("update tasks set status = 'cancelled' where id = any($1)", [[direct.id, team.id]]);
    for (const [id, action] of [[direct.id, 'accept'], [direct.id, 'start'], [team.id, 'accept'], [team.id, 'start']] as const) {
      const res = await act(w.workers[0].api, id, action, T('04:15'));
      expect([res.status, res.body.error.code], action).toEqual([409, 'TASK_STATE_INVALID']);
    }
  });
});
```

`apps/api/src/tasks/task-time.ts`:

```ts
import { EXECUTION_LIMITS } from '@taskop/contracts';
import { AppError } from '../common/app-error';

/**
 * Spec §6.3 with SP4's rule (spec Decision 1): later than server receipt + 2 min is CLOCK_INVALID. Task commands carry
 * no clientOffsetMs: clients send device time minus their last measured offset, so `at` is compared as received.
 */
export function assertNotFuture(at: Date, receivedAt: Date): void {
  if (+at > +receivedAt + EXECUTION_LIMITS.clockFutureToleranceMs) throw new AppError('CLOCK_INVALID');
}

/** A worker's device time: refused when in the future, raised to `floor` (the previous step) when earlier. */
export function boundTaskTime(at: Date, floor: Date, receivedAt: Date): Date {
  assertNotFuture(at, receivedAt);
  return at < floor ? floor : at;
}
```

In `apps/api/src/tasks/task-scope.ts`:
- add `import { AppError } from '../common/app-error';` and `userTeams` to the `../db/schema` import
- add these methods to `TaskScope`:

```ts
  /** Locks the task row first: every command decides access after the lock, so it sees the committed state. */
  async lock(id: string): Promise<TaskRow> {
    const [t] = await this.db.tx().select().from(tasks).where(eq(tasks.id, id)).for('update');
    if (!t) throw new AppError('NOT_FOUND');
    return t;
  }

  /**
   * Who may run a worker command on a locked task (spec Decision 8): the assignee, or a team member while nobody
   * has accepted. A member who lost the race gets TASK_ALREADY_TAKEN, other readers 403, everyone else 404.
   */
  async assertWorker(p: Principal, t: TaskRow): Promise<void> {
    if (t.assigneeUserId === p.userId) return;
    const member = t.assigneeTeamId !== null && (await this.isTeamMember(p.userId, t.assigneeTeamId));
    if (member && t.assigneeUserId === null) return;
    if (member) throw new AppError('TASK_ALREADY_TAKEN');
    if ((await this.access(p, t.id)).canRead) throw new AppError('FORBIDDEN');
    throw new AppError('NOT_FOUND');
  }

  private async isTeamMember(userId: string, teamId: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const [r] = await this.db
      .tx()
      .select({ userId: userTeams.userId })
      .from(userTeams)
      .where(and(eq(userTeams.userId, userId), eq(userTeams.teamId, teamId)));
    return r !== undefined;
  }
```

`apps/api/src/tasks/task-work.service.ts`:

```ts
import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { type TaskDetail, type TaskStatus, taskTransition } from '@taskop/contracts';
import { eq, sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { AppError } from '../common/app-error';
import { Clock } from '../common/clock';
import type { Principal } from '../common/request';
import { DbService } from '../db/db.service';
import { tasks } from '../db/schema';
import type { TaskActionCommandDto } from './dto';
import { TaskQueries } from './task-queries';
import { type TaskRow, TaskScope } from './task-scope';
import { boundTaskTime } from './task-time';
import { TaskWriter } from './task-writer';

/** After `start`, repeated accepts and starts by the assignee are replays. */
const STARTED: readonly TaskStatus[] = ['in_progress', 'awaiting_review', 'completed'];

/**
 * The worker's commands (spec §6.2): accept, start, submit. Each locks the task row first, so two accepts of one
 * team task are serialised and the first to reach the server wins. Every command is idempotent.
 */
@Injectable()
export class TaskWorkService {
  constructor(
    private readonly db: DbService,
    private readonly clock: Clock,
    private readonly scope: TaskScope,
    private readonly writer: TaskWriter,
    private readonly queries: TaskQueries,
  ) {}

  /** POST /tasks/:id/accept. */
  async accept(p: Principal, id: string, cmd: TaskActionCommandDto): Promise<TaskDetail> {
    const receivedAt = this.clock.now();
    const t = await this.scope.lock(id);
    await this.scope.assertWorker(p, t);
    if (t.assigneeUserId === p.userId && t.status !== 'new') return this.replayed(p, t);
    if (!taskTransition(t.status, 'accept', t)) throw new AppError('TASK_STATE_INVALID');
    await this.markAccepted(p, t, boundTaskTime(new Date(cmd.at), t.createdAt, receivedAt), receivedAt);
    return this.queries.detail(p, id);
  }

  /** POST /tasks/:id/start. On a new task it accepts first, at the same time: two history rows (spec §3). */
  async start(p: Principal, id: string, cmd: TaskActionCommandDto): Promise<TaskDetail> {
    const receivedAt = this.clock.now();
    const t = await this.scope.lock(id);
    await this.scope.assertWorker(p, t);
    if (t.assigneeUserId === p.userId && STARTED.includes(t.status)) return this.replayed(p, t);
    if (!taskTransition(t.status, 'start', t)) throw new AppError('TASK_STATE_INVALID');
    const at = boundTaskTime(new Date(cmd.at), t.acceptedAt ?? t.createdAt, receivedAt);
    if (t.status === 'new') await this.markAccepted(p, t, at, receivedAt);
    await this.db
      .tx()
      .update(tasks)
      .set({ status: 'in_progress', startedAt: at, rev: sql`${tasks.rev} + 1`, updatedAt: receivedAt })
      .where(eq(tasks.id, id));
    await this.writer.history(t, [{ from: 'accepted', to: 'in_progress', at, actorUserId: p.userId }]);
    return this.queries.detail(p, id);
  }

  /** A command repeated by the assignee after the task moved past it changes nothing; nothing leaves cancelled. */
  private async replayed(p: Principal, t: TaskRow): Promise<TaskDetail> {
    if (t.status === 'cancelled') throw new AppError('TASK_STATE_INVALID');
    return this.queries.detail(p, t.id);
  }

  /** new → accepted; for a team task the caller becomes the assignee. */
  private async markAccepted(p: Principal, t: TaskRow, at: Date, receivedAt: Date): Promise<void> {
    const [row] = await this.db
      .tx()
      .update(tasks)
      .set({ assigneeUserId: p.userId, status: 'accepted', acceptedAt: at, rev: sql`${tasks.rev} + 1`, updatedAt: receivedAt })
      .where(eq(tasks.id, t.id))
      .returning();
    await this.writer.history(t, [{ from: 'new', to: 'accepted', at, actorUserId: p.userId }]);
    await this.writer.emit('task.accepted', row!, p.userId);
  }
}
```

```ts
    await act(w.workers[0].api, task.id, 'start', '2026-11-02T04:00:00.000Z');

    const code = async (api: typeof w1.api, extra: Record<string, unknown>, role: 'instruction' | 'response' = 'response') => {

      return [res.status, res.body.error?.code];

    expect(await code(w1.api, videoExtra)).toEqual([422, 'MEDIA_LIMIT_REACHED']);
    expect(await code(w1.api, { mime: 'image/gif' })).toEqual([422, 'MEDIA_TYPE_INVALID']);
    expect(await code(w1.api, { bytes: 5_242_881 })).toEqual([422, 'MEDIA_TOO_LARGE']);

    await ownerQuery("update tasks set status = 'completed' where id = $1", [task.id]);
    const replay = await w.manager.api.post(`/api/v1/tasks/${task.id}/media`, body);
    expect(replay.status, JSON.stringify(replay.body)).toBe(200);
    expect(replay.body).toEqual({ mediaId: body.id, status: 'uploaded', uploadUrl: null, headers: {}, expiresAt: null });
    expect((await w.workers[0].api.post(`/api/v1/tasks/${task.id}/media`, body)).status).toBe(404);
  });
});
```
