In `apps/api/src/executions/media.service.ts`, export the two helpers so task media share the limits:

```ts
export const tooLarge = (cmd: Pick<RegisterMediaCommandDto, 'kind' | 'bytes' | 'durationMs' | 'width' | 'height'>): boolean =>
  cmd.kind === 'photo'
    ? cmd.bytes > MEDIA_LIMITS.photoMaxBytes
    : cmd.bytes > MEDIA_LIMITS.videoMaxBytes ||
      (cmd.durationMs ?? 0) > MEDIA_LIMITS.videoMaxSeconds * 1000 ||
      Math.min(cmd.width ?? 0, cmd.height ?? 0) > MEDIA_LIMITS.videoMaxShortEdge;

/** Registrations kept per item and kind: room for retakes of media the worker removed on the phone. */
export const storageCap = (limit: number): number => Math.max(limit * 3, 10);
```

`apps/api/src/tasks/task-media.service.ts`:

```ts
import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { isFinalTaskStatus, MEDIA_LIMITS, type MediaUploadTicket, TASK_LIMITS, type TaskEvidence, type TaskMediaRole } from '@taskop/contracts';
import { and, count, eq, isNull } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { AppError } from '../common/app-error';
import { Clock } from '../common/clock';
import type { Principal } from '../common/request';
import { DbService } from '../db/db.service';
import { taskMedia } from '../db/schema';
import { storageCap, tooLarge } from '../executions/media.service';
import { S3Service } from '../storage/s3.service';
import type { TaskMediaRegisterDto } from './dto';
import { type TaskRow, TaskScope } from './task-scope';
import { assertNotFuture } from './task-time';

export type TaskMediaRow = typeof taskMedia.$inferSelect;

/** Task photos and videos (spec §6.2). Files go straight to storage; the API only signs and checks. */
@Injectable()
export class TaskMediaService {
  constructor(
    private readonly db: DbService,
    private readonly clock: Clock,
    private readonly s3: S3Service,
    private readonly scope: TaskScope,
  ) {}

  /**
   * POST /tasks/:id/media: registers the medium as pending and returns a presigned PUT. The same id again returns a
   * fresh URL while pending and no URL once uploaded, whatever the task's status now (spec Decision 9).
   */
  async register(p: Principal, taskId: string, cmd: TaskMediaRegisterDto): Promise<MediaUploadTicket> {
    const tx = this.db.tx();
    const receivedAt = this.clock.now();
    const t = await this.scope.lock(taskId);
    const [known] = await tx.select().from(taskMedia).where(eq(taskMedia.id, cmd.id));
    if (known) {
      if (known.taskId !== taskId || known.capturedByUserId !== p.userId) throw new AppError('NOT_FOUND');
      // A fresh PUT URL for a confirmed medium would let its uploader overwrite stored evidence.
      if (known.status === 'uploaded') return { mediaId: known.id, status: 'uploaded', uploadUrl: null, headers: {}, expiresAt: null };
      return this.ticket(known);
    }
    await this.assertMayAttach(p, t, cmd.role);
    assertNotFuture(new Date(cmd.capturedAt), receivedAt);
    if (!(MEDIA_LIMITS.mimeTypes[cmd.kind] as readonly string[]).includes(cmd.mime)) throw new AppError('MEDIA_TYPE_INVALID');
    if (tooLarge(cmd)) throw new AppError('MEDIA_TOO_LARGE');
    await this.assertRoom(t, cmd);
    const ext = MEDIA_LIMITS.extensions[cmd.mime as keyof typeof MEDIA_LIMITS.extensions];
    const [row] = await tx
      .insert(taskMedia)
      .values({
        id: cmd.id,
        tenantId: p.tenantId,
        taskId,
        role: cmd.role,
        kind: cmd.kind,
        source: cmd.source,
        mime: cmd.mime,
        bytes: cmd.bytes,
        width: cmd.width ?? null,
        height: cmd.height ?? null,
        durationMs: cmd.durationMs ?? null,
        capturedAt: new Date(cmd.capturedAt),
        capturedByUserId: p.userId,
        storageKey: `t/${p.tenantId}/k/${taskId}/${cmd.id}.${ext}`,
        createdAt: receivedAt,
      })
      .returning();
    return this.ticket(row!);
  }

  /** Instruction media: the creator or a manager, while the task is open. Response media: the assignee, once accepted. */
  private async assertMayAttach(p: Principal, t: TaskRow, role: TaskMediaRole): Promise<void> {
    if (role === 'instruction') {
      if (t.createdByUserId !== p.userId) {
        const a = await this.scope.access(p, t.id);
        if (!a.canRead) throw new AppError('NOT_FOUND');
        if (!a.canManage) throw new AppError('FORBIDDEN');
      }
      if (isFinalTaskStatus(t.status)) throw new AppError('TASK_STATE_INVALID');
      return;
    }
    await this.scope.assertWorker(p, t);
    if (t.assigneeUserId !== p.userId || (t.status !== 'accepted' && t.status !== 'in_progress')) throw new AppError('TASK_STATE_INVALID');
  }

  /** Live-only and evidence levels (FR-12.05–06) and the registration caps (spec Decision 20). */
  private async assertRoom(t: TaskRow, cmd: TaskMediaRegisterDto): Promise<void> {
    const tx = this.db.tx();
    if (cmd.role === 'instruction') {
      const [r] = await tx.select({ n: count() }).from(taskMedia).where(and(eq(taskMedia.taskId, t.id), eq(taskMedia.role, 'instruction')));
      if ((r?.n ?? 0) >= TASK_LIMITS.instructionMedia) throw new AppError('MEDIA_LIMIT_REACHED');
      return;
    }
    const evidence = t.evidence as TaskEvidence;
    if (evidence.liveOnly && cmd.source === 'gallery') throw new AppError('EVIDENCE_LIVE_ONLY');
    if (evidence[cmd.kind] === 'none') throw new AppError('MEDIA_LIMIT_REACHED');
    const limit = cmd.kind === 'photo' ? TASK_LIMITS.responsePhotos : TASK_LIMITS.responseVideos;
    const [r] = await tx
      .select({ n: count() })
      .from(taskMedia)
      .where(and(eq(taskMedia.taskId, t.id), eq(taskMedia.role, 'response'), eq(taskMedia.kind, cmd.kind), isNull(taskMedia.submissionId)));
    if ((r?.n ?? 0) >= storageCap(limit)) throw new AppError('MEDIA_LIMIT_REACHED');
  }

  private async ticket(m: TaskMediaRow): Promise<MediaUploadTicket> {
    const put = await this.s3.presignPut(m.storageKey, m.mime, m.bytes);
    return { mediaId: m.id, status: m.status, uploadUrl: put.url, headers: put.headers, expiresAt: put.expiresAt.toISOString() };
  }
}
```

In `apps/api/src/tasks/task-work.service.ts`:
- extend the imports: `type MediaKind`, `TASK_LIMITS`, `type TaskEvidence`, `taskEvidenceMet` from `@taskop/contracts`; `and`, `inArray`, `isNull` from `drizzle-orm`; `taskMedia`, `taskSubmissions` from `../db/schema`; `SubmissionDto` from `./dto`
- add the methods:

```ts
  /**
   * POST /tasks/:id/submissions. Idempotent on the submission id, whatever the status now (spec Decision 9).
   * Checks the status, the listed media and the evidence rules, links the media, and completes or sends to review.
   */
  async submit(p: Principal, id: string, cmd: SubmissionDto): Promise<TaskDetail> {
    const receivedAt = this.clock.now();
    const tx = this.db.tx();
    const t = await this.scope.lock(id);
    await this.scope.assertWorker(p, t);
    const [known] = await tx.select({ taskId: taskSubmissions.taskId }).from(taskSubmissions).where(eq(taskSubmissions.id, cmd.id));
    if (known) {
      if (known.taskId !== id) throw new AppError('NOT_FOUND');
      return this.queries.detail(p, id);
    }
    // An unaccepted team task is accepted and started first.
    if (t.assigneeUserId !== p.userId) throw new AppError('TASK_STATE_INVALID');
    const next = taskTransition(t.status, 'submit', t);
    if (!next) throw new AppError('TASK_STATE_INVALID');
    const media = await this.responseMedia(p, id, cmd.mediaIds);
    const evidence = taskEvidenceMet(t.evidence as TaskEvidence, media);
    if (!evidence.ok) {
      throw new AppError('EVIDENCE_REQUIRED', { fields: Object.fromEntries(evidence.missing.map((k) => [`evidence.${k}`, `tasks.issues.${k}Required`])) });
    }
    const at = boundTaskTime(new Date(cmd.submittedAt), t.startedAt ?? t.createdAt, receivedAt);
    await tx.insert(taskSubmissions).values({
      id: cmd.id,
      tenantId: p.tenantId,
      taskId: id,
      submittedByUserId: p.userId,
      text: cmd.text,
      mediaIds: cmd.mediaIds,
      submittedAt: at,
      receivedAt,
    });
    if (cmd.mediaIds.length) await tx.update(taskMedia).set({ submissionId: cmd.id }).where(inArray(taskMedia.id, cmd.mediaIds));
    const [row] = await tx
      .update(tasks)
      .set({ status: next, submittedAt: at, completedAt: next === 'completed' ? at : null, rev: sql`${tasks.rev} + 1`, updatedAt: receivedAt })
      .where(eq(tasks.id, id))
      .returning();
    await this.writer.history(t, [{ from: t.status, to: next, at, actorUserId: p.userId }]);
    await this.writer.emit('task.submitted', row!, p.userId);
    if (next === 'completed') await this.writer.emit('task.completed', row!, p.userId);
    return this.queries.detail(p, id);
  }

  /**
   * The listed media (spec Decision 12): distinct, response media of this task, captured by the caller and not used
   * by an earlier submission; at most 10 photos and 3 videos.
   */
  private async responseMedia(p: Principal, taskId: string, ids: string[]): Promise<Array<{ id: string; kind: MediaKind }>> {
    const unknown = () => new AppError('VALIDATION_FAILED', { fields: { mediaIds: 'tasks.issues.unknownMedia' } });
    if (new Set(ids).size !== ids.length) throw unknown();
    const rows = ids.length
      ? await this.db
          .tx()
          .select({ id: taskMedia.id, kind: taskMedia.kind })
          .from(taskMedia)
          .where(
            and(
              inArray(taskMedia.id, ids),
              eq(taskMedia.taskId, taskId),
              eq(taskMedia.role, 'response'),
              eq(taskMedia.capturedByUserId, p.userId),
              isNull(taskMedia.submissionId),
            ),
          )
      : [];
    if (rows.length !== ids.length) throw unknown();
    const photos = rows.filter((r) => r.kind === 'photo').length;
    if (photos > TASK_LIMITS.responsePhotos || rows.length - photos > TASK_LIMITS.responseVideos) {
      throw new AppError('VALIDATION_FAILED', { fields: { mediaIds: 'tasks.issues.tooManyMedia' } });
    }
    return rows;
  }
```

In `apps/api/src/tasks/tasks.controller.ts`, import `SubmissionDto` from `./dto` and add the route:

```ts
  @Post(':id/submissions')
  @HttpCode(200)
  @ApiOkResponse({ type: TaskDetailResponse })
  submit(@CurrentPrincipal() p: Principal, @Param('id', ParseIdPipe) id: string, @Body() body: SubmissionDto): Promise<TaskDetail> {
    return this.work.submit(p, id, body);
  }
```

```ts
import { uuidv7 } from 'uuidv7';

    expect(res.status, JSON.stringify(res.body)).toBe(200);

    expect(await auditActions(task.id)).toEqual(['task.created', 'task.updated']);

    const foreign = await createTask(w.owner, newTaskBody({ userId: w.workers[2].id }));
    expect((await w.manager.api.patch(`/api/v1/tasks/${foreign.id}`, { title: 'x' })).status).toBe(404);
    await w.manager.api.post(`/api/v1/tasks/${task.id}/cancel`, { reason: 'Lazım deyil' });

    expect([bare.status, bare.body.error.fields]).toEqual([400, { comment: 'tasks.issues.commentRequired' }]);
    const events = captureTaskEvents(t);
    const rejected = await w.manager.api.post(`/api/v1/tasks/${task.id}/review`, { decision: 'reject', comment: 'Foto bulanıqdır' });
    expect(rejected.status, JSON.stringify(rejected.body)).toBe(200);
    expect(rejected.body).toMatchObject({ status: 'in_progress', completedAt: null });
    expect(rejected.body.submissions[0]).toMatchObject({
      id: sub1,
      reviewDecision: 'rejected',
      reviewComment: 'Foto bulanıqdır',
      reviewedBy: { id: w.manager.id, fullName: 'Menecer' },
      reviewedAt: T('04:20'),
    });
    const reused = await w1.api.post(`/api/v1/tasks/${task.id}/submissions`, { id: uuidv7(), mediaIds: [photo1], submittedAt: T('04:21') });
    expect([reused.status, reused.body.error.fields]).toEqual([400, { mediaIds: 'tasks.issues.unknownMedia' }]);
    clock.set(T('04:40'));
    const photo2 = await registerTaskMedia(w1.api, task.id, 'response');
    const sub2 = uuidv7();
    expect((await w1.api.post(`/api/v1/tasks/${task.id}/submissions`, { id: sub2, mediaIds: [photo2], submittedAt: T('04:35') })).body.status).toBe('awaiting_review');
    const approved = await w.manager.api.post(`/api/v1/tasks/${task.id}/review`, { decision: 'approve', comment: 'Əla' });
    events.stop();
    expect(approved.body).toMatchObject({ status: 'completed', completedAt: T('04:40') });
    expect(approved.body.submissions.map((s: { id: string; reviewDecision: string }) => [s.id, s.reviewDecision])).toEqual([
      [sub1, 'rejected'],
      [sub2, 'approved'],
    ]);
    expect((await taskHistory(task.id)).slice(-3)).toEqual([
      ['awaiting_review', 'in_progress', w.manager.id, 'Foto bulanıqdır'],
      ['in_progress', 'awaiting_review', w1.id, null],
      ['awaiting_review', 'completed', w.manager.id, 'Əla'],
    ]);
    expect(await auditActions(task.id)).toEqual(['task.created', 'task.rejected', 'task.approved']);
    expect(events.seen).toEqual([
      ['task.rejected', task.id, w.manager.id],
      ['task.submitted', task.id, w1.id],
      ['task.completed', task.id, w.manager.id],
    ]);
    const late = await w.manager.api.post(`/api/v1/tasks/${task.id}/review`, { decision: 'reject', comment: 'x' });
    expect([late.status, late.body.error.code]).toEqual([409, 'TASK_STATE_INVALID']);
  });
});
```

```ts
  /** Manager commands (spec §6.1): 404 unless readable, 403 unless manageable. */
  async assertManage(p: Principal, id: string): Promise<TaskAccess> {
    const a = await this.access(p, id);
    if (!a.canRead) throw new AppError('NOT_FOUND');
    if (!a.canManage) throw new AppError('FORBIDDEN');
```

In `apps/api/src/tasks/tasks.service.ts`:
- extend the imports: `isFinalTaskStatus`, `taskTransition` from `@taskop/contracts`; `desc`, `sql` from `drizzle-orm`; `taskSubmissions` from `../db/schema`; `CancelTaskDto`, `ReviewTaskDto`, `UpdateTaskDto` from `./dto`; `TaskScope` from `./task-scope`
- add the constructor parameter `private readonly scope: TaskScope,` after `audit`

```ts
  /** PATCH /tasks/:id. An assignee change reassigns (spec §3): task.reassigned; other edits: task.updated. */
  async update(p: Principal, id: string, dto: UpdateTaskDto): Promise<TaskDetail> {
    const now = this.clock.now();
    const t = await this.scope.lock(id);
    await this.scope.assertManage(p, id);
    if (isFinalTaskStatus(t.status)) throw new AppError('TASK_STATE_INVALID');
    const set: Partial<typeof tasks.$inferInsert> = {};
    if (dto.title !== undefined) set.title = dto.title;
    if (dto.instructions !== undefined) set.instructions = dto.instructions;
    if (dto.priority !== undefined) set.priority = dto.priority;
    if (dto.evidence !== undefined) set.evidence = dto.evidence;
    if (dto.requiresReview !== undefined) set.requiresReview = dto.requiresReview;
    if (dto.siteId !== undefined && dto.siteId !== t.siteId) {
      if (dto.siteId) await this.assertSite(p, dto.siteId);
      set.siteId = dto.siteId;
    }
    if (dto.dueAt !== undefined) {
      const dueAt = dto.dueAt ? new Date(dto.dueAt) : null;
      set.dueAt = dueAt;
      // Spec Decision 11: no longer due → the flag goes. Completion never clears it.
      if (t.overdueAt && (dueAt === null || dueAt > now)) set.overdueAt = null;
    }
    const edited = Object.keys(set).length > 0;
    let reassigned = false;
    if (dto.assignee) {
      const next = await this.assignees.resolve(p, dto.assignee);
      // Spec Decision 21: the same person (no team) or the same team (nobody accepted) is not a change.
      const same = next.userId ? t.assigneeUserId === next.userId && t.assigneeTeamId === null : t.assigneeTeamId === next.teamId && t.assigneeUserId === null;
      if (!same) {
        reassigned = true;
        set.assigneeUserId = next.userId;
        set.assigneeTeamId = next.teamId;
        set.status = 'new';
        set.acceptedAt = null;
        set.startedAt = null;
      }
    }
    if (!edited && !reassigned) return this.queries.detail(p, id);
    const [row] = await this.db
      .tx()
      .update(tasks)
      .set({ ...set, rev: sql`${tasks.rev} + 1`, updatedAt: now })
      .where(eq(tasks.id, id))
      .returning();
    if (edited) await this.audit.record({ action: 'task.updated', entityType: 'task', entityId: id, before: auditView(t), after: auditView(row!) });
    if (reassigned) {
      // Earlier submissions stay; the history shows the reset.
      if (t.status !== 'new') await this.writer.history(t, [{ from: t.status, to: 'new', at: now, actorUserId: p.userId }]);
      await this.audit.record({
        action: 'task.reassigned',
        entityType: 'task',
        entityId: id,
        before: { assigneeUserId: t.assigneeUserId, assigneeTeamId: t.assigneeTeamId, status: t.status },
        after: { assigneeUserId: row!.assigneeUserId, assigneeTeamId: row!.assigneeTeamId, status: row!.status },
      });
      await this.writer.emit('task.assigned', row!, p.userId);
    }
    return this.queries.detail(p, id);
  }

  /** POST /tasks/:id/cancel: from any open status, with a reason. */
  async cancel(p: Principal, id: string, dto: CancelTaskDto): Promise<TaskDetail> {
    const now = this.clock.now();
    const t = await this.scope.lock(id);
    await this.scope.assertManage(p, id);
    if (!taskTransition(t.status, 'cancel', t)) throw new AppError('TASK_STATE_INVALID');
    const [row] = await this.db
      .tx()
      .update(tasks)
      .set({ status: 'cancelled', cancelledAt: now, cancelReason: dto.reason, rev: sql`${tasks.rev} + 1`, updatedAt: now })
      .where(eq(tasks.id, id))
      .returning();
    await this.writer.history(t, [{ from: t.status, to: 'cancelled', at: now, actorUserId: p.userId, comment: dto.reason }]);
    await this.audit.record({ action: 'task.cancelled', entityType: 'task', entityId: id, before: { status: t.status }, after: { status: 'cancelled', reason: dto.reason } });
    await this.writer.emit('task.cancelled', row!, p.userId);
    return this.queries.detail(p, id);
  }

  /** POST /tasks/:id/review: the creator or a manager; only from awaiting_review; the latest submission gets the review. */
  async review(p: Principal, id: string, dto: ReviewTaskDto): Promise<TaskDetail> {
    const now = this.clock.now();
    const tx = this.db.tx();
    const t = await this.scope.lock(id);
    const access = await this.scope.access(p, id);
    if (!access.canRead) throw new AppError('NOT_FOUND');
    if (!access.canReview) throw new AppError('FORBIDDEN');
    const next = taskTransition(t.status, dto.decision, t);
    if (!next) throw new AppError('TASK_STATE_INVALID');
    const [latest] = await tx
      .select({ id: taskSubmissions.id })
      .from(taskSubmissions)
      .where(eq(taskSubmissions.taskId, id))
      .orderBy(desc(taskSubmissions.receivedAt), desc(taskSubmissions.id))
      .limit(1);
    if (!latest) throw new AppError('TASK_STATE_INVALID');
    const approved = dto.decision === 'approve';
    const comment = dto.comment || null;
    await tx
      .update(taskSubmissions)
      .set({ reviewDecision: approved ? 'approved' : 'rejected', reviewComment: comment, reviewedByUserId: p.userId, reviewedAt: now })
      .where(eq(taskSubmissions.id, latest.id));
    const [row] = await tx
      .update(tasks)
      .set({ status: next, ...(approved ? { completedAt: now } : {}), rev: sql`${tasks.rev} + 1`, updatedAt: now })
      .where(eq(tasks.id, id))
      .returning();
    await this.writer.history(t, [{ from: t.status, to: next, at: now, actorUserId: p.userId, comment }]);
    await this.audit.record({ action: approved ? 'task.approved' : 'task.rejected', entityType: 'task', entityId: id, after: { submissionId: latest.id, comment } });
    await this.writer.emit(approved ? 'task.completed' : 'task.rejected', row!, p.userId);
    return this.queries.detail(p, id);
  }
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

In `apps/api/src/tasks/task-queries.ts`:
- extend the imports: `type Page`, `type TaskSummary` from `@taskop/contracts`; `and`, `desc`, `gte`, `isNotNull`, `isNull`, `lte`, `type SQL`, `sql` from `drizzle-orm`; `TaskListQueryDto` (type) from `./dto`
- add the method:

```ts
  /** GET /tasks (spec §6.2): readable tasks only, newest first, keyset on (created_at, id). */
  async list(p: Principal, q: TaskListQueryDto): Promise<Page<TaskSummary>> {
    const conds: (SQL | undefined)[] = [this.scope.readable(p)];
    if (q.status) conds.push(inArray(tasks.status, q.status));
    if (q.overdue !== undefined) conds.push(q.overdue ? isNotNull(tasks.overdueAt) : isNull(tasks.overdueAt));
    if (q.siteId) conds.push(eq(tasks.siteId, q.siteId));
    if (q.assigneeUserId) conds.push(eq(tasks.assigneeUserId, q.assigneeUserId));
    if (q.teamId) conds.push(eq(tasks.assigneeTeamId, q.teamId));
    if (q.createdBy) conds.push(eq(tasks.createdByUserId, q.createdBy));
    if (q.source) conds.push(q.source === 'problem' ? isNotNull(tasks.problemId) : isNull(tasks.problemId));
    if (q.awaitingMyReview) conds.push(eq(tasks.status, 'awaiting_review'), this.scope.reviewable(p));
    if (q.dueFrom) conds.push(gte(tasks.dueAt, new Date(q.dueFrom)));
    if (q.dueTo) conds.push(lte(tasks.dueAt, new Date(q.dueTo)));
    if (q.cursor) conds.push(sql`(${tasks.createdAt}, ${tasks.id}) < (select c.created_at, c.id from tasks c where c.id = ${q.cursor})`);
    const rows = await selectTasks(this.db.tx())
      .where(and(...conds))
      .orderBy(desc(tasks.createdAt), desc(tasks.id))
      .limit(q.limit + 1);
    const page = rows.slice(0, q.limit);
    return { items: page.map(toTaskSummary), nextCursor: rows.length > q.limit ? page[page.length - 1]!.t.id : null };
  }
```

```ts
  list(@CurrentPrincipal() p: Principal, @Query() q: TaskListQueryDto): Promise<Page<TaskSummary>> {
    return this.queries.list(p, q);
```

`apps/api/test/tasks-jobs.test.ts`:

```ts
import pg from 'pg';
import { uuidv7 } from 'uuidv7';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, inject, it } from 'vitest';
import { TaskJobs } from '../src/tasks/task-jobs';
import { createTestApp, type TestApp } from './app';
import { FakeClock } from './fake-clock';
import { ownerQuery } from './owner-db';
import { MONDAY_0800 } from './scheduling-fixtures';
import { act, captureTaskEvents, createTask, newTaskBody, taskRow, taskWorld } from './task-fixtures';

const T = (hhmm: string) => `2026-11-02T${hhmm}:00.000Z`;

describe('tasks.sweep', () => {
  const clock = new FakeClock(MONDAY_0800);
  let t: TestApp;
  let jobs: TaskJobs;
  beforeAll(async () => {
    t = await createTestApp({}, { clock });
    jobs = t.app.get(TaskJobs);
  });
  beforeEach(() => clock.set(MONDAY_0800));
  afterAll(() => t.close());

  it('flags open tasks once due (at due_at exactly), emits task.overdue, and never touches final tasks', async () => {
    const w = await taskWorld(t);
    const body = (dueAt: string | null) => newTaskBody({ userId: w.workers[0].id }, { dueAt });
    const due = await createTask(w.manager.api, body(T('05:00')));
    const done = await createTask(w.manager.api, body(T('05:00')));
    const gone = await createTask(w.manager.api, body(T('05:00')));
    const later = await createTask(w.manager.api, body(T('06:00')));
    const never = await createTask(w.manager.api, body(null));
    await ownerQuery("update tasks set status = 'completed' where id = $1", [done.id]);
    expect((await w.manager.api.post(`/api/v1/tasks/${gone.id}/cancel`, { reason: 'Lazım deyil' })).status).toBe(200);
    clock.set('2026-11-02T04:59:59.999Z');
    expect(await jobs.sweepAll([w.s.tenantId])).toBe(0);
    clock.set(T('05:00'));
    const events = captureTaskEvents(t);
    expect(await jobs.sweepAll([w.s.tenantId])).toBe(1);
    events.stop();
    expect(events.seen).toEqual([['task.overdue', due.id, null]]);
    expect(await taskRow(due.id)).toMatchObject({ overdue_at: new Date(T('05:00')), rev: 2, status: 'new' });
    expect(await jobs.sweepAll([w.s.tenantId])).toBe(0);
    for (const id of [done.id, gone.id, later.id, never.id]) expect((await taskRow(id)).overdue_at, id).toBeNull();
    clock.set(T('06:30'));
    expect(await jobs.sweepAll([w.s.tenantId])).toBe(1);
    expect((await w.manager.api.get(`/api/v1/tasks/${later.id}`)).body.overdueAt).toBe(T('06:30'));
  });

  it('keeps the flag when a late task is completed, and does not re-flag a task whose due date moved later', async () => {
    const w = await taskWorld(t);
    const late = await createTask(w.manager.api, newTaskBody({ userId: w.workers[0].id }, { dueAt: T('05:00') }));
    const moved = await createTask(w.manager.api, newTaskBody({ userId: w.workers[0].id }, { dueAt: T('05:00') }));
    clock.set(T('05:00'));
    expect(await jobs.sweepAll([w.s.tenantId])).toBe(2);
    clock.set(T('05:10'));
    await act(w.workers[0].api, late.id, 'start', T('05:05'));
    const done = await w.workers[0].api.post(`/api/v1/tasks/${late.id}/submissions`, { id: uuidv7(), submittedAt: T('05:10') });
    expect(done.body).toMatchObject({ status: 'completed', overdueAt: T('05:00') });
    expect((await w.manager.api.patch(`/api/v1/tasks/${moved.id}`, { dueAt: T('07:00') })).body.overdueAt).toBeNull();
    expect(await jobs.sweepAll([w.s.tenantId])).toBe(0);
    clock.set(T('07:00'));
    expect(await jobs.sweepAll([w.s.tenantId])).toBe(1);
    expect((await taskRow(moved.id)).overdue_at).toEqual(new Date(T('07:00')));
  });

  it('skips a task locked by a running command and flags it on the next run', async () => {
    const w = await taskWorld(t);
    const task = await createTask(w.manager.api, newTaskBody({ userId: w.workers[0].id }, { dueAt: T('05:00') }));
    clock.set(T('05:00'));
    const client = new pg.Client({ connectionString: inject('db').ownerUrl });
    await client.connect();
    try {
      await client.query('begin');
      await client.query('select id from tasks where id = $1 for update', [task.id]);
      expect(await jobs.sweepAll([w.s.tenantId])).toBe(0);
      await client.query('rollback');
    } finally {
      await client.end();
    }
    expect(await jobs.sweepAll([w.s.tenantId])).toBe(1);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`apps/api/src/tasks/task-jobs.ts`:

```ts
import { Injectable, Logger, type OnModuleInit } from '@nestjs/common';
import { and, asc, inArray, isNull, lte, notInArray, sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { Clock } from '../common/clock';
import { perTenant, tenantsWith } from '../common/per-tenant';
import { DbService } from '../db/db.service';
import { tasks } from '../db/schema';
import { JobsService } from '../scheduling/jobs.service';
import { TaskWriter } from './task-writer';

export const TASK_SWEEP_QUEUE = 'tasks.sweep';

/** The task cron jobs (spec §6.5). One RLS transaction per tenant. */
@Injectable()
export class TaskJobs implements OnModuleInit {
  private readonly logger = new Logger('TaskJobs');

  constructor(
    private readonly db: DbService,
    private readonly clock: Clock,
    private readonly jobs: JobsService,
    private readonly writer: TaskWriter,
  ) {}

  onModuleInit(): void {
    this.jobs.define({ name: TASK_SWEEP_QUEUE, cron: '* * * * *', handler: (tenantIds) => this.sweepAll(tenantIds) });
  }

  /** tasks.sweep: overdue_at = now on open tasks with now ≥ due_at, once; rev bumped; task.overdue per task. */
  async sweepAll(only?: string[]): Promise<number> {
    const now = this.clock.now();
    const tenantIds = await tenantsWith(
      this.db,
      sql`select distinct tenant_id from tasks where status not in ('completed', 'cancelled') and overdue_at is null and due_at <= ${now}`,
      only,
    );
    return perTenant(this.db, this.logger, 'Task sweep', tenantIds, () => this.sweepTenant(now));
  }

  /** SKIP LOCKED: a task held by a running command is flagged on the next run, never waited for. */
  private async sweepTenant(now: Date): Promise<number> {
    const tx = this.db.tx();
    const due = tx
      .select({ id: tasks.id })
      .from(tasks)
      .where(and(notInArray(tasks.status, ['completed', 'cancelled']), isNull(tasks.overdueAt), lte(tasks.dueAt, now)))
      .orderBy(asc(tasks.id))
      .for('update', { skipLocked: true });
    const flagged = await tx
      .update(tasks)
      .set({ overdueAt: now, rev: sql`${tasks.rev} + 1`, updatedAt: now })
      .where(and(inArray(tasks.id, due), isNull(tasks.overdueAt)))
      .returning();
    for (const t of flagged) await this.writer.emit('task.overdue', t, null);
    return flagged.length;
  }
}
```

In `apps/api/src/tasks/tasks.module.ts`, import `TaskJobs` from `./task-jobs` and add it to `providers`.

`apps/api/src/tasks/task-listeners.ts`:

```ts
import { Injectable, type OnModuleInit } from '@nestjs/common';
import type { TaskHistoryReason } from '@taskop/contracts';
import { and, asc, eq, isNotNull, notInArray, sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { Clock } from '../common/clock';
import { DomainEvents } from '../common/domain-events';
import { DbService } from '../db/db.service';
import { tasks, users } from '../db/schema';
import { TaskWriter } from './task-writer';

const DEACTIVATED: TaskHistoryReason = 'assignee_deactivated';

/** Keeps tasks in step with user changes (spec §6.5). Handlers run in the caller's transaction. */
@Injectable()
export class TaskListeners implements OnModuleInit {
  constructor(
    private readonly events: DomainEvents,
    private readonly db: DbService,
    private readonly clock: Clock,
    private readonly writer: TaskWriter,
  ) {}

  onModuleInit(): void {
    this.events.on('user.access_changed', (e) => this.onUserAccessChanged(e.userId));
  }

  /**
   * Deactivation: open team tasks the user accepted go back to the team. Direct tasks stay as they are (the API
   * reports assigneeInactive so a manager reassigns). Nothing is deleted (FR-04.11). Reactivation changes nothing.
   */
  private async onUserAccessChanged(userId: string): Promise<void> {
    const tx = this.db.tx();
    const [u] = await tx.select({ status: users.status }).from(users).where(eq(users.id, userId));
    if (u?.status !== 'deactivated') return;
    // In id order, so this never deadlocks with other changes that lock several tasks.
    const taken = await tx
      .select()
      .from(tasks)
      .where(and(eq(tasks.assigneeUserId, userId), isNotNull(tasks.assigneeTeamId), notInArray(tasks.status, ['completed', 'cancelled'])))
      .orderBy(asc(tasks.id))
      .for('update');
    const now = this.clock.now();
    for (const t of taken) {
      const [row] = await tx
        .update(tasks)
        .set({ assigneeUserId: null, status: 'new', acceptedAt: null, startedAt: null, rev: sql`${tasks.rev} + 1`, updatedAt: now })
        .where(eq(tasks.id, t.id))
        .returning();
      await this.writer.history(t, [{ from: t.status, to: 'new', at: now, actorUserId: null, comment: DEACTIVATED }]);
      await this.writer.emit('task.assigned', row!, null);
    }
  }
}
```

In `apps/api/src/tasks/tasks.module.ts`, import `TaskListeners` from `./task-listeners` and add it to `providers`.

```ts
import { uuidv7 } from 'uuidv7';
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`apps/api/src/tasks/task-sync.ts`:

```ts
import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { type SyncTask, TASK_LIMITS } from '@taskop/contracts';
import { and, asc, eq, gte, inArray, isNull, notInArray, or } from 'drizzle-orm';
import type { Principal } from '../common/request';
import { DbService } from '../db/db.service';
import { tasks } from '../db/schema';
import { TaskQueries } from './task-queries';
import { TaskScope } from './task-scope';

const DAY = 86_400_000;
const open = () => notInArray(tasks.status, ['completed', 'cancelled']);

/** The tasks section of GET /me/sync (spec §6.4). The sync has no change cursor: this is the complete set. */
@Injectable()
export class TaskSync {
  constructor(
    private readonly db: DbService,
    private readonly scope: TaskScope,
    private readonly queries: TaskQueries,
  ) {}

  /**
   * Open tasks assigned to the user, unaccepted open tasks of the user's teams, and the user's tasks closed in the
   * last 7 days. Tasks awaiting someone's review come from GET /tasks. `removedTaskIds`: known ids not in the set.
   */
  async forUser(p: Principal, knownTaskIds: string[], now: Date): Promise<{ tasks: SyncTask[]; removedTaskIds: string[] }> {
    const since = new Date(+now - TASK_LIMITS.syncClosedDays * DAY);
    const rows = await this.db
      .tx()
      .select({ id: tasks.id })
      .from(tasks)
      .where(
        or(
          and(eq(tasks.assigneeUserId, p.userId), open()),
          and(isNull(tasks.assigneeUserId), open(), this.scope.memberOfTeam(p.userId)),
          and(eq(tasks.assigneeUserId, p.userId), inArray(tasks.status, ['completed', 'cancelled']), gte(tasks.updatedAt, since)),
        ),
      )
      .orderBy(asc(tasks.createdAt), asc(tasks.id));
    const ids = rows.map((r) => r.id);
    const included = new Set(ids);
    return { tasks: await this.queries.syncTasks(ids), removedTaskIds: knownTaskIds.filter((id) => !included.has(id)) };
  }
}
```

In `apps/api/src/executions/sync.service.ts`:
- import `TaskSync` from `../tasks/task-sync`
- add the constructor parameter `private readonly taskSync: TaskSync,`
- replace the interim lines at the end of the returned object:

```ts

      })),
      ...(await this.taskSync.forUser(p, q.knownTaskIds, now)),
    };
```

  - Routes `POST /task-media/:id/uploaded` (the uploader; `403` for other readers, `404` otherwise), `GET /task-media/:id/url` (task readers), `GET /tasks/:id/problem-media/:mediaId/url` (task readers; only media ids listed on the task's problem)
  - `TASK_MEDIA_CLEANUP_QUEUE = 'task-media.cleanup'`, cron `45 3 * * *`; `TaskJobs.cleanupAll(only?: string[]): Promise<number>`

```ts
import type { MediaUploadTicket, MediaUrl, TaskDetail } from '@taskop/contracts';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { S3Service } from '../src/storage/s3.service';
import { TaskJobs } from '../src/tasks/task-jobs';
import { createTestApp, type TestApp } from './app';
import { completeExecution, liveExecution, registerBytes } from './execution-fixtures';
import { ownerQuery } from './owner-db';
import type { Api } from './scheduling-fixtures';
import { type Seaweed, startSeaweedfs } from './seaweedfs';
import { act, createTask, newTaskBody, taskMediaBody, taskWorld } from './task-fixtures';

/** Real time throughout: SeaweedFS checks signatures against its own clock. */
describe('task media against SeaweedFS', () => {
  let sw: Seaweed;
  let t: TestApp;
  beforeAll(async () => {
    sw = await startSeaweedfs();
    t = await createTestApp(sw.env);
  });
  afterAll(async () => {
    await t?.close();
    await sw?.stop();
  });

  const upload = (ticket: MediaUploadTicket, body: Buffer) =>
    fetch(ticket.uploadUrl!, { method: 'PUT', body: new Uint8Array(body), headers: { 'Content-Type': ticket.headers['Content-Type']! } });

  async function register(api: Api, taskId: string, role: 'instruction' | 'response', bytes: number) {
    const body = taskMediaBody(role, { bytes, capturedAt: new Date().toISOString() });
    const res = await api.post(`/api/v1/tasks/${taskId}/media`, body);
    expect(res.status, JSON.stringify(res.body)).toBe(200);
    return { id: body.id, ticket: res.body as MediaUploadTicket };
  }

  /** The manager's task for İşçi 1, started now. */
  async function startedTask() {
    const w = await taskWorld(t);
    const task = await createTask(w.manager.api, newTaskBody({ userId: w.workers[0].id }));
    expect((await act(w.workers[0].api, task.id, 'start', new Date().toISOString())).status).toBe(200);
    return { w, taskId: task.id };
  }

  it('confirms an upload for the uploader only, refuses it before the PUT, and serves view URLs to task readers', async () => {
    const { w, taskId } = await startedTask();
    const api = w.workers[0].api;
    const bytes = Buffer.alloc(2048, 1);
    const { id, ticket } = await register(api, taskId, 'response', bytes.length);
    const early = await api.post(`/api/v1/task-media/${id}/uploaded`);
    expect([early.status, early.body.error.code]).toEqual([422, 'MEDIA_NOT_FOUND_IN_STORAGE']);
    expect((await api.get(`/api/v1/task-media/${id}/url`)).body.error.code).toBe('MEDIA_NOT_FOUND_IN_STORAGE');
    expect((await upload(ticket, bytes)).status).toBe(200);
    expect((await w.manager.api.post(`/api/v1/task-media/${id}/uploaded`)).status).toBe(403);
    expect((await w.workers[1].api.post(`/api/v1/task-media/${id}/uploaded`)).status).toBe(404);
    const ok = await api.post(`/api/v1/task-media/${id}/uploaded`);
    expect(ok.status, JSON.stringify(ok.body)).toBe(200);
    expect(ok.body).toMatchObject({ mediaId: id, status: 'uploaded' });
    expect((await api.post(`/api/v1/task-media/${id}/uploaded`)).body.uploadedAt).toBe(ok.body.uploadedAt);
    const forManager = await w.manager.api.get(`/api/v1/task-media/${id}/url`);
    expect(forManager.status, JSON.stringify(forManager.body)).toBe(200);
    expect(Buffer.from(await (await fetch(forManager.body.url)).arrayBuffer())).toEqual(bytes);
    expect((await api.get(`/api/v1/task-media/${id}/url`)).status).toBe(200);
    expect((await w.workers[2].api.get(`/api/v1/task-media/${id}/url`)).status).toBe(404);
  });

  it('signs the source problem’s media for whoever can read the task, and nothing else', async () => {
    const { w, executionId, api } = await liveExecution(t);
    const bytes = Buffer.alloc(300, 4);
    const item = await registerBytes(api, executionId, w.c.photo.id, 10);
    const evidence = await registerBytes(api, executionId, null, bytes.length);
    expect((await upload(evidence.ticket, bytes)).status).toBe(200);
    expect((await api.post(`/api/v1/media/${evidence.id}/uploaded`)).status).toBe(200);
    const done = await completeExecution(
      api,
      executionId,
      1,
      {
        [w.c.problem.id]: { optionIds: [w.c.no.id] },
        [w.c.temp.id]: { number: 5, problem: { severity: 'critical', note: 'Qapı sınıqdır', mediaIds: [evidence.id] } },
        [w.c.photo.id]: { photos: [item.id] },
      },
      new Date().toISOString(),
    );
    expect(done.status, JSON.stringify(done.body)).toBe(200);
    const problemId = (await ownerQuery<{ id: string }>("select id from execution_problems where execution_id = $1 and source = 'manual'", [executionId])).rows[0]!.id;
    const task = await createTask(w.owner, newTaskBody({ userId: w.workers[1].id }, { problemId }));
    // The card says what to draw before anything is signed (Spec decision 24).
    expect(task.problem!.media).toEqual([{ id: evidence.id, kind: 'photo', status: 'uploaded' }]);
    const assignee = w.workers[1].api;
    expect((await assignee.get(`/api/v1/media/${evidence.id}/url`)).status).toBe(404);
    const signed = await assignee.get(`/api/v1/tasks/${task.id}/problem-media/${evidence.id}/url`);
    expect(signed.status, JSON.stringify(signed.body)).toBe(200);
    expect(Buffer.from(await (await fetch(signed.body.url)).arrayBuffer())).toEqual(bytes);
    expect((await assignee.get(`/api/v1/tasks/${task.id}/problem-media/${item.id}/url`)).status).toBe(404);
    expect((await w.workers[0].api.get(`/api/v1/tasks/${task.id}/problem-media/${evidence.id}/url`)).status).toBe(404);
  });

  it('deletes the objects of task media never confirmed after 14 days, once, and keeps the rows', async () => {
    const { w, taskId } = await startedTask();
    const old = await register(w.workers[0].api, taskId, 'response', 10);
    const recent = await register(w.manager.api, taskId, 'instruction', 10);
    expect((await upload(old.ticket, Buffer.alloc(10))).status).toBe(200);
    expect((await upload(recent.ticket, Buffer.alloc(10))).status).toBe(200);
    await ownerQuery("update task_media set created_at = now() - interval '15 days' where id = $1", [old.id]);
    await ownerQuery("update task_media set created_at = now() - interval '13 days' where id = $1", [recent.id]);
    const jobs = t.app.get(TaskJobs);
    const s3 = t.app.get(S3Service);
    expect(await jobs.cleanupAll([w.s.tenantId])).toBe(1);
    expect(await jobs.cleanupAll([w.s.tenantId])).toBe(0);
    const row = async (id: string) =>
      (await ownerQuery<{ status: string; storage_key: string; purged: boolean }>('select status, storage_key, storage_purged_at is not null as purged from task_media where id = $1', [id])).rows[0]!;
    const o = await row(old.id);
    expect([o.status, o.purged]).toEqual(['pending', true]);
    expect(await s3.head(o.storage_key)).toBeNull();
    const r = await row(recent.id);
    expect([r.status, r.purged]).toEqual(['pending', false]);
    expect(await s3.head(r.storage_key)).not.toBeNull();
    expect((await w.workers[0].api.post(`/api/v1/task-media/${old.id}/uploaded`)).body.error.code).toBe('MEDIA_NOT_FOUND_IN_STORAGE');
    // The purged medium stays in the task, still pending (Interfaces: there is no purged status).
    const detail = (await w.manager.api.get(`/api/v1/tasks/${taskId}`)).body as TaskDetail;
    expect(detail.media.find((m) => m.id === old.id)).toMatchObject({ status: 'pending', uploadedAt: null });
  });

  it('lets the web dev origins upload to and read from presigned URLs, and refuses other origins (bucket CORS)', async () => {
    const { w, taskId } = await startedTask();
    const { id, ticket } = await register(w.workers[0].api, taskId, 'response', 10);
    const preflight = (origin: string) =>
      fetch(ticket.uploadUrl!, { method: 'OPTIONS', headers: { Origin: origin, 'Access-Control-Request-Method': 'PUT', 'Access-Control-Request-Headers': 'content-type' } });
    for (const origin of ['http://localhost:5173', 'http://localhost:4173']) {
      const ok = await preflight(origin);
      expect(ok.status, origin).toBe(200);
      expect(ok.headers.get('access-control-allow-origin')).toBe(origin);
      expect(ok.headers.get('access-control-allow-methods')).toContain('PUT');
      expect(ok.headers.get('access-control-allow-headers')?.toLowerCase()).toContain('content-type');
    }
    // A bucket rule replaces SeaweedFS's default `-s3.allowedOrigins=*`.
    expect((await preflight('https://evil.example')).status).toBe(403);
    // The PUT and the GET answer the dev origin with the headers the browser checks; ETag is readable.
    const put = await fetch(ticket.uploadUrl!, { method: 'PUT', body: new Uint8Array(10), headers: { 'Content-Type': ticket.headers['Content-Type']!, Origin: 'http://localhost:5173' } });
    expect(put.status, await put.clone().text()).toBe(200);
    expect(put.headers.get('access-control-allow-origin')).toBe('http://localhost:5173');
    expect(put.headers.get('access-control-expose-headers')).toContain('ETag');
    expect((await w.workers[0].api.post(`/api/v1/task-media/${id}/uploaded`)).status).toBe(200);
    const view = (await w.manager.api.get(`/api/v1/task-media/${id}/url`)).body as MediaUrl;
    const get = await fetch(view.url, { headers: { Origin: 'http://localhost:5173' } });
    expect([get.status, get.headers.get('access-control-allow-origin')]).toEqual([200, 'http://localhost:5173']);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Confirm, view and problem-media URLs**

In `apps/api/src/tasks/task-media.service.ts`:
- extend the imports: `type MediaConfirmResult`, `type MediaUrl` from `@taskop/contracts`; `sql` from `drizzle-orm`; `executionMedia`, `executionProblems`, `tasks` from `../db/schema`
- add the methods:

```ts
  /**
   * POST /task-media/:id/uploaded: the uploader only; the object must exist with the registered size and type.
   * The row is locked first, so task-media.cleanup cannot delete the object between the check and the update.
   */
  async confirmUploaded(p: Principal, mediaId: string): Promise<MediaConfirmResult> {
    const tx = this.db.tx();
    const [m] = await tx.select().from(taskMedia).where(eq(taskMedia.id, mediaId)).for('update');
    if (!m) throw new AppError('NOT_FOUND');
    if (m.capturedByUserId !== p.userId) {
      if ((await this.scope.access(p, m.taskId)).canRead) throw new AppError('FORBIDDEN');
      throw new AppError('NOT_FOUND');
    }
    if (m.status === 'uploaded') return { mediaId, status: 'uploaded', uploadedAt: m.uploadedAt?.toISOString() ?? null };
    const stored = await this.s3.head(m.storageKey);
    if (!stored || stored.contentLength !== m.bytes || stored.contentType !== m.mime) throw new AppError('MEDIA_NOT_FOUND_IN_STORAGE');
    const now = this.clock.now();
    await tx.update(taskMedia).set({ status: 'uploaded', uploadedAt: now, storagePurgedAt: null }).where(eq(taskMedia.id, mediaId));
    return { mediaId, status: 'uploaded', uploadedAt: now.toISOString() };
  }

  /** GET /task-media/:id/url: a 5-minute presigned GET for anyone who can read the task (NFR-10.02). */
  async viewUrl(p: Principal, mediaId: string): Promise<MediaUrl> {
    const [m] = await this.db.tx().select().from(taskMedia).where(eq(taskMedia.id, mediaId));
    if (!m || !(await this.scope.access(p, m.taskId)).canRead) throw new AppError('NOT_FOUND');
    if (m.status !== 'uploaded') throw new AppError('MEDIA_NOT_FOUND_IN_STORAGE');
    const get = await this.s3.presignGet(m.storageKey);
    return { url: get.url, expiresAt: get.expiresAt.toISOString() };
  }

  /** GET /tasks/:id/problem-media/:mediaId/url (spec Decision 14): the source problem's media, for task readers. */
  async problemMediaUrl(p: Principal, taskId: string, mediaId: string): Promise<MediaUrl> {
    if (!(await this.scope.access(p, taskId)).canRead) throw new AppError('NOT_FOUND');
    const [m] = await this.db
      .tx()
      .select({ storageKey: executionMedia.storageKey, status: executionMedia.status })
      .from(tasks)
      .innerJoin(executionProblems, eq(executionProblems.id, tasks.problemId))
      .innerJoin(executionMedia, eq(executionMedia.executionId, executionProblems.executionId))
      .where(and(eq(tasks.id, taskId), eq(executionMedia.id, mediaId), sql`${executionMedia.id} = any(${executionProblems.mediaIds})`));
    if (!m) throw new AppError('NOT_FOUND');
    if (m.status !== 'uploaded') throw new AppError('MEDIA_NOT_FOUND_IN_STORAGE');
    const get = await this.s3.presignGet(m.storageKey);
    return { url: get.url, expiresAt: get.expiresAt.toISOString() };
  }
```

```ts
import { Controller, Get, HttpCode, Inject, Param, Post } from '@nestjs/common';
import { ApiBearerAuth, ApiOkResponse, ApiTags } from '@nestjs/swagger';
import type { MediaConfirmResult, MediaUrl } from '@taskop/contracts';
import { CurrentPrincipal } from '../common/decorators';
import { ParseIdPipe } from '../common/parse-id.pipe';
import type { Principal } from '../common/request';
import { MediaConfirmResultResponse, MediaUrlResponse } from '../executions/dto';
import { TaskMediaService } from './task-media.service';

@ApiTags('tasks')
@ApiBearerAuth()
@Controller('task-media')
export class TaskMediaController {
  constructor(@Inject(TaskMediaService) private readonly media: TaskMediaService) {}

  @Post(':id/uploaded')
  @HttpCode(200)
  @ApiOkResponse({ type: MediaConfirmResultResponse })
  confirm(@CurrentPrincipal() p: Principal, @Param('id', ParseIdPipe) id: string): Promise<MediaConfirmResult> {
    return this.media.confirmUploaded(p, id);
  }

  @Get(':id/url')
  @ApiOkResponse({ type: MediaUrlResponse })
  url(@CurrentPrincipal() p: Principal, @Param('id', ParseIdPipe) id: string): Promise<MediaUrl> {
    return this.media.viewUrl(p, id);
  }
}
```

In `apps/api/src/tasks/task-jobs.ts`:
- extend the imports: `MEDIA_LIMITS` from `@taskop/contracts`; `eq` from `drizzle-orm`; `taskMedia` from `../db/schema`; `S3Service` from `../storage/s3.service`
- add after `TASK_SWEEP_QUEUE`:

```ts
export const TASK_MEDIA_CLEANUP_QUEUE = 'task-media.cleanup';
const DAY = 86_400_000;
```

- add the constructor parameter `private readonly s3: S3Service,`
- `onModuleInit` becomes:

```ts
  onModuleInit(): void {
    this.jobs.define({ name: TASK_SWEEP_QUEUE, cron: '* * * * *', handler: (tenantIds) => this.sweepAll(tenantIds) });
    // 03:45 UTC = 07:45 in Baku, after media.cleanup and before the working day.
    this.jobs.define({ name: TASK_MEDIA_CLEANUP_QUEUE, cron: '45 3 * * *', handler: (tenantIds) => this.cleanupAll(tenantIds) });
  }
```

- add the methods:

```ts
  /** task-media.cleanup (spec §6.5, Decision 3): deletes objects of media still pending after 14 days; rows stay. */
  async cleanupAll(only?: string[]): Promise<number> {
    const cutoff = new Date(+this.clock.now() - MEDIA_LIMITS.pendingCleanupDays * DAY);
    const tenantIds = await tenantsWith(
      this.db,
      sql`select distinct tenant_id from task_media where status = 'pending' and storage_purged_at is null and created_at <= ${cutoff}`,
      only,
    );
    return perTenant(this.db, this.logger, 'Task media cleanup', tenantIds, () => this.cleanupTenant(cutoff));
  }

  /** Claims the rows first (locked, still pending, not yet purged), then deletes their objects, as media.cleanup does. */
  private async cleanupTenant(cutoff: Date): Promise<number> {
    const tx = this.db.tx();
    const due = tx
      .select({ id: taskMedia.id })
      .from(taskMedia)
      .where(and(eq(taskMedia.status, 'pending'), isNull(taskMedia.storagePurgedAt), lte(taskMedia.createdAt, cutoff)))
      .orderBy(asc(taskMedia.id))
      .limit(1000)
      .for('update', { skipLocked: true });
    const claimed = await tx
      .update(taskMedia)
      .set({ storagePurgedAt: this.clock.now() })
      .where(and(inArray(taskMedia.id, due), eq(taskMedia.status, 'pending'), isNull(taskMedia.storagePurgedAt)))
      .returning({ id: taskMedia.id, taskId: taskMedia.taskId, storageKey: taskMedia.storageKey });
    for (const m of claimed) {
      await this.s3.delete(m.storageKey);
      this.logger.warn({ mediaId: m.id, taskId: m.taskId }, 'Task medium never confirmed as uploaded; storage object deleted');
    }
    return claimed.length;
  }
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Bucket CORS for the browser**

The web (Part 3) is the first browser client: it `PUT`s instruction files to presigned URLs and loads media from presigned `GET`s, both cross-origin (`localhost:5173` → `localhost:8333`). Configure the bucket explicitly instead of relying on SeaweedFS's default `-s3.allowedOrigins=*` (Spec decision 26).

Create `docker/seaweedfs/cors.json` (the shape both `aws s3api put-bucket-cors --cors-configuration` and the SDK's `CORSConfiguration` take):

```json
{
  "CORSRules": [
    {
      "AllowedOrigins": ["http://localhost:5173", "http://localhost:4173"],
      "AllowedMethods": ["GET", "PUT", "HEAD"],
      "AllowedHeaders": ["Content-Type", "Content-Length"],
      "ExposeHeaders": ["ETag"],
      "MaxAgeSeconds": 3000
    }
  ]
}
```

In `docker-compose.yml`, the `seaweedfs-init` service mounts the file and applies it after making sure the bucket exists. `put-bucket-cors` replaces the rule, so it is safe on every `docker compose up`, and an existing dev volume gets the rule on the next `up`. Replace the service's `entrypoint` and add `volumes`:

```yaml
    volumes:
      - ./docker/seaweedfs/cors.json:/etc/seaweedfs/cors.json:ro
    entrypoint:
      - sh
      - -c
      - >-
        (aws --endpoint-url http://seaweedfs:8333 s3api head-bucket --bucket taskop-media
        || aws --endpoint-url http://seaweedfs:8333 s3api create-bucket --bucket taskop-media)
        && aws --endpoint-url http://seaweedfs:8333 s3api put-bucket-cors --bucket taskop-media --cors-configuration file:///etc/seaweedfs/cors.json
```

and change its comment to `# One-off: creates the private bucket and sets its CORS rule for the web dev origins; restarts until SeaweedFS answers.`

In `apps/api/test/seaweedfs.ts`, apply the same file to the test bucket, so the storage tests check the committed rule:
- extend the imports to:

```ts
import { uuidv7 } from 'uuidv7';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { createTestApp, type TestApp } from './app';
import { FakeClock } from './fake-clock';
import { MONDAY_0800 } from './scheduling-fixtures';
import { createTask, newTaskBody, registerTaskMedia, taskMediaBody, type TaskWorld, taskWorld } from './task-fixtures';

const AT = '2026-11-02T04:00:00.000Z';

describe('task tenant isolation', () => {
  const clock = new FakeClock(MONDAY_0800);
  let t: TestApp;
  let A: TaskWorld;
  let B: TaskWorld;
  const a: Record<string, string> = {};
  beforeAll(async () => {
    t = await createTestApp({}, { clock });
    A = await taskWorld(t);
    B = await taskWorld(t);
    a.task = (await createTask(A.manager.api, newTaskBody({ userId: A.workers[0].id }))).id;
    a.media = await registerTaskMedia(A.manager.api, a.task, 'instruction');
  });
  afterAll(() => t.close());

  // [route, method, url, body, also as B's worker?] Manager routes answer a worker 403 before any lookup.
  it.each([
    ['GET /tasks/:id', 'GET', () => `/api/v1/tasks/${a.task}`, undefined, true],
    ['PATCH /tasks/:id', 'PATCH', () => `/api/v1/tasks/${a.task}`, { title: 'x' }, false],
    ['POST /tasks/:id/cancel', 'POST', () => `/api/v1/tasks/${a.task}/cancel`, { reason: 'x' }, false],
    ['POST /tasks/:id/review', 'POST', () => `/api/v1/tasks/${a.task}/review`, { decision: 'approve' }, true],
    ['POST /tasks/:id/accept', 'POST', () => `/api/v1/tasks/${a.task}/accept`, { at: AT }, true],
    ['POST /tasks/:id/start', 'POST', () => `/api/v1/tasks/${a.task}/start`, { at: AT }, true],
    ['POST /tasks/:id/submissions', 'POST', () => `/api/v1/tasks/${a.task}/submissions`, { id: uuidv7(), submittedAt: AT }, true],
    ['POST /tasks/:id/media', 'POST', () => `/api/v1/tasks/${a.task}/media`, taskMediaBody('response'), true],
    ['POST /task-media/:id/uploaded', 'POST', () => `/api/v1/task-media/${a.media}/uploaded`, {}, true],
    ['GET /task-media/:id/url', 'GET', () => `/api/v1/task-media/${a.media}/url`, undefined, true],
    ['GET /tasks/:id/problem-media/:mediaId/url', 'GET', () => `/api/v1/tasks/${a.task}/problem-media/${a.media}/url`, undefined, true],
  ] as const)('%s on a foreign id returns 404', async (_name, method, url, body, asWorker) => {
    for (const api of asWorker ? [B.owner, B.workers[0].api] : [B.owner]) {
      const path = url();
      const res = method === 'GET' ? await api.get(path) : method === 'PATCH' ? await api.patch(path, body!) : await api.post(path, body ?? {});
      expect(res.status, `${method} ${path}`).toBe(404);
    }
  });

  it('never lists or syncs the other tenant’s tasks, and refuses its people, teams and sites', async () => {
    expect((await B.owner.get('/api/v1/tasks?limit=200')).body.items.map((i: { id: string }) => i.id)).not.toContain(a.task);
    expect((await B.workers[0].api.get('/api/v1/me/sync')).body.tasks).toEqual([]);
    expect((await B.owner.post('/api/v1/tasks', newTaskBody({ userId: A.workers[0].id }))).body.error.code).toBe('TASK_ASSIGNEE_INVALID');
    expect((await B.owner.post('/api/v1/tasks', newTaskBody({ teamId: A.teamId }))).body.error.code).toBe('TASK_ASSIGNEE_INVALID');
    expect((await B.owner.post('/api/v1/tasks', newTaskBody({ userId: B.workers[0].id }, { siteId: A.siteId }))).body.error.code).toBe('REFERENCE_NOT_FOUND');
    const options = (await B.owner.get('/api/v1/tasks/assignees')).body;
    expect(options.users.map((u: { id: string }) => u.id)).not.toContain(A.workers[0].id);
    expect(options.teams.map((x: { id: string }) => x.id)).toEqual([B.teamId]);
    expect(options.sites.map((x: { id: string }) => x.id)).not.toContain(A.siteId);
  });
});
```

### Task 19: Demo tasks in the seed, and follow-up notes

**Files:**
- Modify: `apps/api/src/db/scripts/seed.ts`
- Test: `apps/api/test/seed-demo.test.ts`
- Create: `docs/superpowers/tasks-follow-ups.md`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes the demo tenant from `seed.ts`: the manager `manager@demo.taskop.az` (Leyla Quliyeva), the workers `elvin` and `nigar`, the site `Anbar №1`, and the manual problem of the demo partial execution (`seedDemoExecution`).
- Produces, idempotently (skipped if the tenant already has a task):
  - the team **Texniki xidmət** (elvin, nigar), created if missing
  - an ad-hoc task from Leyla to elvin at Anbar №1, priority `high`, due tomorrow 18:00 Baku, photo required, live only
  - a team task from the demo run's manual problem, assigned to Texniki xidmət, review on
  - one `null → new` history row each
  This gives the web's tasks page and problems page (Part 3) and the phone's Tapşırıqlar tab (Part 2) data on a fresh demo database.

```ts
  it('seeds a team and two demo tasks, one from the run’s manual problem, once', async () => {
    await seed(inject('db').ownerUrl);
    await seed(inject('db').ownerUrl);
    const r = await ownerQuery<{ title: string; status: string; assignee: string | null; team: string | null; from_problem: boolean; requires_review: boolean; history: number }>(
      `select k.title, k.status, u.username as assignee, tm.name as team, k.problem_id is not null as from_problem, k.requires_review,
              (select count(*)::int from task_status_history h where h.task_id = k.id) as history

         join tenants t on t.id = k.tenant_id

        order by k.title`,
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

In `apps/api/src/db/scripts/seed.ts`, change the drizzle import to `import { and, eq, inArray } from 'drizzle-orm';` and add after `seedDemoExecution`:

```ts
/**
 * Idempotent: the team "Texniki xidmət" (elvin, nigar), an ad-hoc task from Leyla to elvin due tomorrow at 18:00 Baku,
 * and a team task created from the demo run's manual problem, with review on. Skipped once the tenant has a task.
 */
async function seedDemoTasks(tx: Tx, tenantId: string): Promise<void> {
  const existing = await tx.select({ id: schema.tasks.id }).from(schema.tasks).where(eq(schema.tasks.tenantId, tenantId)).limit(1);
  if (existing.length) return;
  const [leyla] = await tx
    .select({ id: schema.users.id })
    .from(schema.users)
    .where(and(eq(schema.users.tenantId, tenantId), eq(schema.users.email, 'manager@demo.taskop.az')));
  const workers = await tx
    .select({ id: schema.users.id, username: schema.users.username })
    .from(schema.users)
    .where(and(eq(schema.users.tenantId, tenantId), inArray(schema.users.username, ['elvin', 'nigar'])));
  const elvin = workers.find((u) => u.username === 'elvin');
  const [warehouse] = await tx.select({ id: schema.sites.id }).from(schema.sites).where(and(eq(schema.sites.tenantId, tenantId), eq(schema.sites.name, 'Anbar №1')));
  if (!leyla || !elvin || !warehouse) return;

  let [team] = await tx.select({ id: schema.teams.id }).from(schema.teams).where(and(eq(schema.teams.tenantId, tenantId), eq(schema.teams.name, 'Texniki xidmət')));
  if (!team) {
    [team] = await tx.insert(schema.teams).values({ tenantId, name: 'Texniki xidmət', description: 'Təmir və texniki işlər' }).returning({ id: schema.teams.id });
    await tx.insert(schema.userTeams).values(workers.map((u) => ({ tenantId, userId: u.id, teamId: team!.id })));
  }

  const now = new Date();
  const dueAt = zonedTimeToUtc(addDays(localDateOf(now, 'Asia/Baku'), 1), 18 * 60, 'Asia/Baku');
  const adhocId = uuidv7();
  await tx.insert(schema.tasks).values({
    id: adhocId,
    tenantId,
    title: 'Soyuducunun qapısını təmir edin',
    instructions: 'Qapının rezin kipləşdiricisini dəyişin və qapının tam bağlandığını yoxlayın.',
    priority: 'high',
    siteId: warehouse.id,
    createdByUserId: leyla.id,
    assigneeUserId: elvin.id,
    evidence: { photo: 'required', video: 'optional', liveOnly: true },
    dueAt,
    createdAt: now,
    updatedAt: now,
  });
  const history: (typeof schema.taskStatusHistory.$inferInsert)[] = [{ tenantId, taskId: adhocId, fromStatus: null, toStatus: 'new', actorUserId: leyla.id, at: now }];

  const [problem] = await tx
    .select({ id: schema.executionProblems.id, siteId: schema.executionProblems.siteId, note: schema.executionProblems.note })
    .from(schema.executionProblems)
    .where(and(eq(schema.executionProblems.tenantId, tenantId), eq(schema.executionProblems.source, 'manual')))
    .limit(1);
  if (problem) {
    const fixId = uuidv7();
    await tx.insert(schema.tasks).values({
      id: fixId,
      tenantId,
      title: 'Zibil qutusunun qapağını dəyişin',
      instructions: problem.note ?? '',
      siteId: problem.siteId,
      createdByUserId: leyla.id,
      assigneeTeamId: team!.id,
      requiresReview: true,
      problemId: problem.id,
      createdAt: now,
      updatedAt: now,
    });
    history.push({ tenantId, taskId: fixId, fromStatus: null, toStatus: 'new', actorUserId: leyla.id, at: now });
  }
  await tx.insert(schema.taskStatusHistory).values(history);
}
```

- Call it after `seedDemoExecution` in both branches of `seed`:
  - Existing tenant: `await seedDemoTasks(tx, existing.id);` inside the `db.transaction` callback, after `seedDemoExecution(tx, existing.id)`.
  - New tenant: `await seedDemoTasks(tx, tenantId);` after `seedDemoExecution(tx, tenantId)`.

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the follow-up notes**

`docs/superpowers/tasks-follow-ups.md`:

```markdown
# Tasks — follow-ups

Items found while building sub-project 5 that are out of its scope. ⚑ marks the ones to look at first.

- ⚑ **Notifications (SP6).** `task.assigned`, `task.accepted`, `task.submitted`, `task.rejected`, `task.completed`, `task.cancelled` and `task.overdue` are emitted with no listeners. SP6 subscribes for FR-14.10 (completion to the creator) and FR-15.03 (overdue).
- ⚑ **Production bucket CORS.** Apply the "Deployment plan note" of `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-12-tasks-1-api.md` to the deployment plan: the `taskop-media` bucket on `files.taskop.app` must allow origin `https://admin.taskop.app` (`GET`, `PUT`, `HEAD`; `Content-Type`, `Content-Length`; expose `ETag`), or web uploads fail in the browser while the phone keeps working.
- ⚑ **Platform-admin reads.** The task endpoints are tenant-only. Add `platform/tenants/:tenantId/tasks…` variants with the `route-mode` pattern if support needs them.
- **Permission backfill.** `0010_tasks_security.sql` gave the task keys to the existing Admin, Manager and Auditor roles (SP2–SP4 backfilled Admin only). Nothing was deployed then; revisit the rule before the first production migration of a later sub-project.
- **Overdue in the timeline.** `overdue_at` is a flag with a timestamp, not a `task_status_history` row. If reports (SP7, FR-18.12) need it in the status timeline, add an event-type column to the history table.
- **Sync size.** `/me/sync` returns every open task of the user and `knownTaskIds` is capped at 200. If a user ever holds more tasks, move tasks to a change cursor on `(updated_at, id)` (the `tasks_sync_idx` index is already there).
- **Concurrent duplicate creates** (the same task id sent twice in parallel) can produce one `500` from `tasks_pkey`; the retry returns the stored task. Map `tasks_pkey` to a replay if it shows up in logs.
- **Instruction media** can be added but not removed after creation (no `DELETE` on `task_media`). Add an `archived_at` column if managers ask to remove a wrong photo.
- **Media retention per plan** (NFR-10.03) → sub-project 9. `task-media.cleanup` only removes never-uploaded objects.
```
